Now let
x\in\overline{\mathbb{R}}^{3}_{+}
,
(x,v)\notin\Sigma_{0}\cup\Sigma_{-}
and
(t_{0},x_{0},v_{0})=(t,x,v)
. By the Maxwell reflection boundary condition (
1.2
), the back-time cycle is defined as

\displaystyle X_{cl}(s;t,x,v)=

\displaystyle\mathbbm{1}_{[t_{1},t)}(s)\big\{x-(t-s)v\big\}+\mathbbm{1}_{(-\infty,t_{1})}(s)\big\{x-[(1-\alpha_{\varepsilon})R_{x_{b}}v+\alpha_{\varepsilon}v^{\prime}](t-s)\big\}\,,

(3.16)

\displaystyle V_{cl}(s;t,x,v)=

\displaystyle\mathbbm{1}_{[t_{1},t)}(s)v+\mathbbm{1}_{(-\infty,t_{1})}(s)[(1-\alpha_{\varepsilon})R_{x_{b}}v+\alpha_{\varepsilon}v^{\prime}]\,,

where
v^{\prime}\in\mathcal{V}:=\{v^{\prime}:v^{\prime}\cdot n>0\}
.

According to the trajectories showed in Figure
1
and
2
, the points set
[0,\tau]\times\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+}\times\mathbb{R}^{3}
can be clarified by following two parts:

(3.17)

\displaystyle\mathscr{U}=

\displaystyle\{(t,x,v)\in[0,\tau]\times\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+}\times\mathbb{R}^{3}:t\in[0,\tau],v\cdot n\geq 0\}

\displaystyle\cup\{(t,x,v)\in[0,\tau]\times\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+}\times\mathbb{R}^{3}:v\cdot n<0\textrm{ and }t_{1}=t-t_{b}(x,v)\leq 0\}\,,

\displaystyle\mathscr{V}=

\displaystyle[0,\tau]\times\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+}\times\mathbb{R}^{3}-\mathscr{U}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\{(t,x,v)\in[0,\tau]\times\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+}\times\mathbb{R}^{3}:v\cdot n<0\textrm{ and }t_{1}=t-t_{b}(x,v)>0\}\,.

Define a nonnegative number

(3.18)

t_{\star}=\left\{\begin{aligned} 0\,,\quad\textrm{if }(t,x,v)\in\mathscr{U}\,,\\
t_{1}\,,\quad\textrm{if }(t,x,v)\in\mathscr{V}\,.\end{aligned}\right.

Observe that the trajectory over
s\in[t_{\star},t]
is

(3.19)

\displaystyle X_{cl}(s;t,x,v)=x-(t-s)v\,,\ V_{cl}(s;t,x,v)=v\,.

As in
[
9
]
, we define

\displaystyle\mathcal{L}_{M}g=-\tfrac{1}{\sqrt{\mathfrak{M}_{M}}}\big\{B(\mathfrak{M},\sqrt{\mathfrak{M}_{M}}g)+B(\sqrt{\mathfrak{M}_{M}}g,\mathfrak{M})\big\}=\big\{\nu(\mathfrak{M})+K\big\}g\,,

where
Kg=K_{1}g-K_{2}g
with

\displaystyle K_{1}g=

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}\times\mathbb{S}^{2}}b(\theta)|u-v|^{\gamma_{0}}\sqrt{\mathfrak{M}_{M}(u)}\tfrac{\mathfrak{M}(v)}{\sqrt{\mathfrak{M}_{M}(v)}}g(u)\mathrm{d}\omega\mathrm{d}u\,,

\displaystyle K_{2}g=

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}\times\mathbb{S}^{2}}b(\theta)|u-v|^{\gamma_{0}}\mathfrak{M}(u^{\prime})\tfrac{\sqrt{\mathfrak{M}_{M}(v^{\prime})}}{\sqrt{\mathfrak{M}_{M}(v)}}g(v^{\prime})\mathrm{d}\omega\mathrm{d}u

\displaystyle+\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}\times\mathbb{S}^{2}}b(\theta)|u-v|^{\gamma_{0}}\mathfrak{M}(v^{\prime})\tfrac{\sqrt{\mathfrak{M}_{M}(u^{\prime})}}{\sqrt{\mathfrak{M}_{M}(v)}}g(u^{\prime})\mathrm{d}\omega\mathrm{d}u\,.

Consider a smooth cutoff function
0\leq\chi_{m}\leq 1
such that for
m>0
,
\chi_{m}(s)\equiv 1
for
s\leq m
, and
\chi_{m}(s)\equiv 0
for
s\geq 2m
. Then we introduce (see
[
22
]
)

\displaystyle K^{m}g=

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}\times\mathbb{S}^{2}}b(\theta)|u-v|^{\gamma_{0}}\chi_{m}(|u-v|)\sqrt{\mathfrak{M}_{M}(u)}\tfrac{\mathfrak{M}_{(}v)}{\mathfrak{M}_{M}(v)}g(u)\mathrm{d}\omega\mathrm{d}u

\displaystyle-\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}\times\mathbb{S}^{2}}b(\theta)|u-v|^{\gamma_{0}}\chi_{m}(|u-v|)\mathfrak{M}(u^{\prime})\tfrac{\sqrt{\mathfrak{M}_{M}(v^{\prime})}}{\sqrt{\mathfrak{M}_{M}(v)}}g(v^{\prime})\mathrm{d}\omega\mathrm{d}u

\displaystyle-\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}\times\mathbb{S}^{2}}b(\theta)|u-v|^{\gamma_{0}}\chi_{m}(|u-v|)\mathfrak{M}(v^{\prime})\tfrac{\sqrt{\mathfrak{M}_{M}(u^{\prime})}}{\sqrt{\mathfrak{M}_{M}(v)}}g(u^{\prime})\mathrm{d}\omega\mathrm{d}u\,,

and further define
K^{c}g=Kg-K^{m}g
.

Lemma 3.3

(Lemma 2.3 of
[
22
]
)
.

(3.20)

\displaystyle|K^{m}g(v)|\leq Cm^{3+\gamma_{0}}\nu(\mathfrak{M})\|g\|_{\infty}

and
K^{c}g(v)=\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\Bbbk(v,v^{\prime})g(v^{\prime})\mathrm{d}v^{\prime}
, where the kernel
\Bbbk(v,v^{\prime})
satisfies

(3.21)

\displaystyle\Bbbk(v,v^{\prime})\leq C_{m}\tfrac{\mathrm{exp}\big\{-c|v-v^{\prime}|^{2}\big\}}{|v-v^{\prime}|(1+|v|+|v^{\prime}|)^{1-\gamma_{0}}}

for some
c>0
.

Proof of Lemma
3.2
.

The proof is similar to
[
22
]
. We will only elaborate on the proof ideas and give a sketch of the proof process. The details of calculation will be omitted for simplicity of presentation. We will especially focus on the details of the relationship between
\|h_{R,\varepsilon}^{\ell}\|_{\infty}
and
\|f_{R,\varepsilon}\|_{2}
.

We first define

\displaystyle K_{\ell}g\equiv\langle v\rangle^{\ell}K\Big(\tfrac{g}{\langle v\rangle^{\ell}}\Big)\,.

From the remainder equation (
1.52
) and the definition of
h^{\ell}_{R,\varepsilon}
given in (
1.59
), we deduce that

\displaystyle\partial_{t}h^{\ell}_{R,\varepsilon}+v\cdot\nabla_{x}h^{\ell}_{R,\varepsilon}+\tfrac{\nu(\mathfrak{M})}{\varepsilon}h^{\ell}_{R,\varepsilon}-\tfrac{1}{\varepsilon}K_{\ell}h^{\ell}_{R,\varepsilon}

(3.22)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\tfrac{\sqrt{\varepsilon}^{2}\langle v\rangle^{\ell}}{\sqrt{\mathfrak{M}}_{M}}B\Big(\tfrac{\sqrt{\mathfrak{M}_{M}}}{\langle v\rangle^{\ell}}h^{\ell}_{R,\varepsilon},\tfrac{\sqrt{\mathfrak{M}_{M}}}{\langle v\rangle^{\ell}}h^{\ell}_{R,\varepsilon}\Big)+\tfrac{\langle v\rangle^{\ell}}{\sqrt{\mathfrak{M}_{M}}}\big(R_{\varepsilon}+R^{b}_{\varepsilon}+R^{bb}_{\varepsilon}\big)

\displaystyle+\sum_{i=k}^{3}\sqrt{\varepsilon}^{k-2}\tfrac{\langle v\rangle^{\ell}}{\sqrt{\mathfrak{M}_{M}}}\Big\{B\Big(F_{k}+F^{b}_{k}+F^{bb}_{k},\tfrac{\sqrt{\mathfrak{M}_{M}}}{\langle v\rangle^{\ell}}h^{\ell}_{R,\varepsilon}\Big)+B\Big(\tfrac{\sqrt{\mathfrak{M}_{M}}}{\langle v\rangle^{\ell}}h^{\ell}_{R,\varepsilon},F_{k}+F^{b}_{k}+F^{bb}_{k}\Big)\Big\}

with boundary condition on
\Sigma_{-}

(3.23)

\displaystyle\gamma_{-}h^{\ell}_{R,\varepsilon}=(1-\alpha_{\varepsilon})h^{\ell}_{R,\varepsilon}(t,x,R_{x}v)+\alpha_{\varepsilon}M_{w}\tfrac{\langle v\rangle^{\ell}}{\sqrt{\mathfrak{M}_{M}}}\int_{v^{\prime}\cdot n>0}(v^{\prime}\cdot n)\tfrac{\sqrt{\mathfrak{M}_{M}}}{\langle v^{\prime}\rangle^{\ell}}h^{\ell}_{R,\varepsilon}(t,x,v^{\prime})\mathrm{d}v^{\prime}\,.

For any
(t,x,v)
, integrating (
3.2
) along the backward trajectory (
3.2
) over
s\in[t_{\star},t]
, one obtains that

\displaystyle h^{\ell}_{R,\varepsilon}(t,x,v)=\underbrace{\mathrm{exp}\big\{-\tfrac{1}{\varepsilon}\int_{t_{\star}}^{t}\nu(\phi)\mathrm{d}\phi\big\}h^{\ell}_{R,\varepsilon}(t_{\star},X_{cl}(t_{\star}),V_{cl}(t_{\star}))}_{I_{1}}

\displaystyle+\underbrace{\int_{t_{\star}}^{t}\mathrm{exp}\big\{-\tfrac{1}{\varepsilon}\int_{s}^{t}\nu(\phi)\mathrm{d}\phi\big\}\big(\tfrac{1}{\varepsilon}K_{\ell}^{m}h^{\ell}_{R,\varepsilon}\big)(s,X_{cl}(s),V_{cl}(s))\mathrm{d}s}_{I_{2}}

\displaystyle+\underbrace{\int_{t_{\star}}^{t}\mathrm{exp}\big\{-\tfrac{1}{\varepsilon}\int_{s}^{t}\nu(\phi)\mathrm{d}\phi\big\}\big(\tfrac{1}{\varepsilon}K_{\ell}^{c}h^{\ell}_{R,\varepsilon}\big)(s,X_{cl}(s),V_{cl}(s))\mathrm{d}s}_{I_{3}}

\displaystyle+\underbrace{\int_{t_{\star}}^{t}\mathrm{exp}\big\{-\tfrac{1}{\varepsilon}\int_{s}^{t}\nu(\phi)\mathrm{d}\phi\big\}\Big(\sqrt{\varepsilon}\tfrac{\langle v\rangle^{\ell}}{\sqrt{\mathfrak{M}_{M}}}B\big(\tfrac{\sqrt{\mathfrak{M}_{M}}}{\langle v\rangle^{\ell}}h^{\ell}_{R,\varepsilon},\tfrac{\sqrt{\mathfrak{M}_{M}}}{\langle v\rangle^{\ell}}h^{\ell}_{R,\varepsilon}\big)\Big)(s,X_{cl}(s),V_{cl}(s))\mathrm{d}s}_{I_{4}}

\displaystyle\left.\begin{aligned} &+\sum_{k=1}^{5}\sqrt{\varepsilon}^{k-2}\int_{t_{\star}}^{t}\mathrm{exp}\big\{-\tfrac{1}{\varepsilon}\int_{s}^{t}\nu(\phi)\mathrm{d}\phi\big\}\Big\{\tfrac{\langle v\rangle^{\ell}}{\sqrt{\mathfrak{M}_{M}}}B\Big(F_{k}+F^{b}_{k}+F^{bb}_{k},\tfrac{\sqrt{\mathfrak{M}_{M}}}{\langle v\rangle^{\ell}}h^{\ell}_{R,\varepsilon}\Big)\\
&\qquad\qquad\qquad\quad+\tfrac{\langle v\rangle^{\ell}}{\sqrt{\mathfrak{M}_{M}}}B\Big(\tfrac{\sqrt{\mathfrak{M}_{M}}}{\langle v\rangle^{\ell}}h^{\ell}_{R,\varepsilon},F_{k}+F^{b}_{k}+F^{bb}_{k}\Big)\Big\}(s,X_{cl}(s),V_{cl}(s))\mathrm{d}s\end{aligned}\right\}{:=I_{5}}

(3.24)

\displaystyle+\underbrace{\int_{t_{\star}}^{t}\mathrm{exp}\big\{-\tfrac{1}{\varepsilon}\int_{s}^{t}\nu(\phi)\mathrm{d}\phi\big\}\Big(\tfrac{\langle v\rangle^{\ell}}{\sqrt{\mathfrak{M}_{M}}}\big(R_{\varepsilon}+R^{b}_{\varepsilon}+R^{bb}_{\varepsilon}\big)\Big)\big(s,X_{cl}(s),V_{cl}(s)\big)\mathrm{d}s}_{I_{6}}\,,

where the simplified symbol has been employed:

\displaystyle\nu(\phi)=\nu(\mathfrak{M})\big(\phi,X_{cl}(\phi),V_{cl}(\phi)\big)\,.

Case 1:
(t,x,v)\in\mathscr{U}
.

Notice that
t_{\star}=0
. Then the term
I_{1}
in (
3.2
) can be bounded by

(3.25)

\displaystyle|I_{1}|\leq C\varepsilon\langle v\rangle^{-\gamma_{0}}\|h^{\ell}_{R,\varepsilon}(0)\|_{\infty}\leq C\varepsilon\|h^{\ell}_{R,\varepsilon}(0)\|_{\infty}\,.

For small
m>0
, one has

\displaystyle|I_{2}|\leq Cm^{3+\gamma_{0}}\sup_{s\in[0,t]}\|h^{\ell}_{R,\varepsilon}(s)\|_{\infty}\,,\ |I_{4}|\leq C\sqrt{\varepsilon}^{3}\sup_{s\in[0,t]}\|h^{\ell}_{R,\varepsilon}(s)\|_{\infty}^{2}\,,

(3.26)

\displaystyle|I_{5}|\leq C\sqrt{\varepsilon}\sup_{s\in[0,t]}\|h^{\ell}_{R,\varepsilon}(s)\|_{\infty}\,,\ |I_{6}|\leq C\sqrt{\varepsilon}^{2}\,.

We then focus on the estimate of the term
I_{3}
in (
3.2
). Let
\Bbbk_{\ell}(v,v^{\prime})
be the corresponding kernel associated with
K^{c}_{\ell}
. From Lemma
3.3
,

(3.27)

\displaystyle|\Bbbk_{\ell}(v,v^{\prime})|\leq\tfrac{C\langle v^{\prime}\rangle^{\ell}\mathrm{exp}\big\{-c|v-v^{\prime}|^{2}\big\}}{|v-v^{\prime}|\langle v\rangle^{\ell}(1+|v|+|v^{\prime}|)^{1-\gamma_{0}}}\leq\tfrac{C\mathrm{exp}\{-\frac{3}{4}c|v-v^{\prime}|^{2}\}}{|v-v^{\prime}|(1+|v|+|v^{\prime}|)^{1-\gamma_{0}}}\,.

We therefore bound
I_{3}
in (
3.2
) by

\displaystyle|I_{3}|\leq\tfrac{1}{\varepsilon}\int_{t_{\star}}^{t}\mathrm{exp}\Big\{-\tfrac{1}{\varepsilon}\int_{s}^{t}\nu(\phi)\mathrm{d}\phi\Big\}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\big|\Bbbk_{\ell}(V_{cl}(s),v^{\prime})h^{\ell}_{R,\varepsilon}(s,X_{cl}(s),v^{\prime})\big|\mathrm{d}v^{\prime}\mathrm{d}s\,.

We now use (
3.2
) again to evaluate
h^{\ell}_{R,\varepsilon}
via replacing
(t,x,v)
with
(s,X_{cl}(s),v^{\prime})
. Together with (
3.2
), the above can be further bounded by

\displaystyle\left.\begin{aligned} |I_{3}|\leq&\tfrac{1}{\varepsilon}\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\mathrm{exp}\Big\{-\tfrac{1}{\varepsilon}\int_{s}^{t}\nu(\phi)\mathrm{d}\phi-\tfrac{1}{\varepsilon}\int_{0}^{s}\nu(v^{\prime})(\phi)\mathrm{d}\phi\Big\}|\Bbbk_{\ell}(V_{cl}(s),v^{\prime})|\\
&\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\times|h^{\ell}_{R,\varepsilon}\big(0,X_{cl}(0;s,X_{cl}(s),v^{\prime}),v^{\prime}\big)|\mathrm{d}v^{\prime}\mathrm{d}s\end{aligned}\right\}{:=I^{1}_{3}}

\displaystyle\left.\begin{aligned} &+\tfrac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}\int_{0}^{t}\mathrm{exp}\Big\{-\tfrac{1}{\varepsilon}\int_{s}^{t}\nu(\phi)\mathrm{d}\phi\Big\}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\Bbbk_{\ell}(V_{cl}(s),v^{\prime})|\int_{0}^{s}\mathrm{exp}\Big\{-\tfrac{1}{\varepsilon}\int_{s_{1}}^{s}\nu(\phi)\mathrm{d}\phi\Big\}\\
&\qquad\qquad\times\big|K_{\ell}^{m}h^{\ell}_{R,\varepsilon}\big(s_{1},X_{cl}(s_{1};s,X_{cl}(s),v^{\prime}),V_{cl}(s_{1};s,X_{cl}(s),v^{\prime})\big)\big|\mathrm{d}s_{1}\mathrm{d}v^{\prime}\mathrm{d}s\end{aligned}\right\}{:=I^{2}_{3}}

\displaystyle\left.\begin{aligned} &+\tfrac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}\int_{0}^{t}\mathrm{exp}\Big\{-\tfrac{1}{\varepsilon}\int_{s}^{t}\nu(\phi)\mathrm{d}\phi\Big\}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}\times\mathbb{R}^{3}}\big|\Bbbk_{\ell}\big(V_{cl}(s),v^{\prime}\big)\Bbbk_{\ell}(v^{\prime},v^{\prime\prime})\big|\\
&\times\int_{0}^{s}\mathrm{exp}\Big\{-\tfrac{1}{\varepsilon}\int_{s_{1}}^{s}\nu(v^{\prime})(\phi)\mathrm{d}\phi\Big\}\big|h^{\ell}_{R,\varepsilon}\big(s_{1},X_{cl}(s_{1};s,X_{cl}(s),v^{\prime}),v^{\prime\prime}\big)\big|\mathrm{d}v^{\prime\prime}\mathrm{d}v^{\prime}\mathrm{d}s_{1}\mathrm{d}s\end{aligned}\right\}{:=I^{3}_{3}}

\displaystyle+\underbrace{\tfrac{C}{\varepsilon}\int_{0}^{t}\mathrm{exp}\Big\{-\tfrac{1}{\varepsilon}\int_{s}^{t}\nu(\phi)\mathrm{d}\phi\Big\}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\Bbbk_{\ell}(V_{cl}(s),v^{\prime})|\mathrm{d}v^{\prime}\mathrm{d}s\cdot\sqrt{\varepsilon}^{3}\sup_{s\in[0,t]}\|h^{\ell}_{R,\varepsilon}(s)\|_{\infty}^{2}}_{I^{4}_{3}}

\displaystyle+\underbrace{\tfrac{C}{\varepsilon}\int_{0}^{t}\mathrm{exp}\Big\{-\tfrac{1}{\varepsilon}\int_{s}^{t}\nu(\phi)\mathrm{d}\phi\Big\}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\Bbbk_{\ell}(V_{cl}(s),v^{\prime})|\mathrm{d}v^{\prime}\mathrm{d}s\cdot\sqrt{\varepsilon}\sup_{s\in[0,t]}\|h^{\ell}_{R,\varepsilon}(s)\|_{\infty}}_{I^{5}_{3}}

(3.28)

\displaystyle+\underbrace{\tfrac{C}{\varepsilon}\int_{0}^{t}\mathrm{exp}\Big\{-\tfrac{1}{\varepsilon}\int_{s}^{t}\nu(\phi)\mathrm{d}\phi\Big\}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\Bbbk_{\ell}(V_{cl}(s),v^{\prime})|\mathrm{d}v^{\prime}\mathrm{d}s\cdot\sqrt{\varepsilon}^{2}}_{I^{6}_{3}}\,.

Except for the term
I_{3}^{3}
, the sum of all other terms can be bounded by

\displaystyle I^{1}_{3}+I^{2}_{3}+I^{4}_{3}+

\displaystyle I^{5}_{3}+I^{6}_{3}

(3.29)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\big\{\|h^{\ell}_{R,\varepsilon}(0)\|_{\infty}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}^{3}\sup_{s\in[0,t]}\|h^{\ell}_{R,\varepsilon}(s)\|_{\infty}^{2}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\sup_{s\in[0,t]}\|h^{\ell}_{R,\varepsilon}(s)\|_{\infty}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}^{2}\big\}\,.

It remains to control the quantity
I^{3}_{3}
in (
3.2
). As in
[
22
]
, we will finish our arguments by the following several cases. Let large
N_{0}>0
and small
\kappa_{*}>0
be undetermined.

Case 1. If
|v|\geq N_{0}
,

(3.30)

\displaystyle I^{3}_{3}\leq\tfrac{C}{N_{0}}\sup_{s\in[0,t]}\|h^{\ell}_{R,\varepsilon}(s)\|_{\infty}\,.

Case 2. If either
|v|\leq N_{0}
,
|v^{\prime}|\geq 2N_{0}
or
|v^{\prime}|\leq 2N_{0}
,
|v^{\prime\prime}|\geq 3N_{0}
,

(3.31)

\displaystyle I^{3}_{3}\leq C_{\eta}e^{-\frac{\eta}{8}N^{2}_{0}}\sup_{s\in[0,t]}\|h^{\ell}_{R,\varepsilon}(s)\|_{\infty}

for some small
\eta>0
.

Case 3a. If
|v|\leq N_{0}
,
|v^{\prime}|\leq 2N_{0}
,
|v^{\prime\prime}|\leq 3N_{0}
,
s-s_{1}\leq\varepsilon\kappa_{*}
,

(3.32)

\displaystyle I^{3}_{3}\leq C_{N_{0}}\kappa_{*}\sup_{s\in[0,t]}\|h^{\ell}_{R,\varepsilon}(s)\|_{\infty}\,.

Case 3b. If
|v|\leq N_{0}
,
|v^{\prime}|\leq 2N_{0}
,
|v^{\prime\prime}|\leq 3N_{0}
,
s-s_{1}\geq\varepsilon\kappa_{*}
, together with (
1.12
), one has

\displaystyle I^{3}_{3}\leq

\displaystyle\tfrac{C}{\varepsilon^{2}}\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\big\{|v^{\prime}|\leq 2N_{0},|v^{\prime\prime}|\leq 3N_{0}\big\}}\int_{0}^{s-\varepsilon\kappa_{*}}\mathrm{exp}\Big\{-\tfrac{C\langle v\rangle^{\gamma_{0}}(t-s)}{\varepsilon}\Big\}\mathrm{exp}\Big\{-\tfrac{C\langle v^{\prime}\rangle^{\gamma_{0}}(s-s_{1})}{\varepsilon}\Big\}

\displaystyle\times\big|\Bbbk_{\bar{\ell}}(V_{cl}(s),v^{\prime})\Bbbk_{\ell}(v^{\prime},v^{\prime\prime})h^{\ell}_{R,\varepsilon}\big(s_{1};X_{cl}(s_{1};s,X_{cl}(s),v^{\prime}),v^{\prime\prime}\big)\big|\mathrm{d}s_{1}\mathrm{d}v^{\prime}\mathrm{d}v^{\prime\prime}\mathrm{d}s\,.

We remark that the relation between
\|h_{R,\varepsilon}^{\ell}\|_{\infty}
and
\|f_{R,\varepsilon}\|_{2}
comes from this case. By (
3.27
),
\Bbbk_{\ell}(V_{cl}(s),v^{\prime})
has a possible integrable singularity of
\frac{1}{|V_{cl}(s)-v^{\prime}|}
. We can choose
\Bbbk_{N_{0}}(V_{cl}(s),v^{\prime})
smooth with compact support such that

(3.33)

\displaystyle\sup_{|p|\leq 3N_{0}}\int_{|v^{\prime}|\leq 3N_{0}}\big|\Bbbk_{N_{0}}(p,v^{\prime})-\Bbbk_{\ell}(p,v^{\prime})\big|\mathrm{d}v^{\prime}\leq\tfrac{1}{N_{0}}\,.

Splitting

\displaystyle\Bbbk_{\ell}(V_{cl}(s),v^{\prime})

\displaystyle\Bbbk_{\ell}(v^{\prime},v^{\prime\prime})=\big[\Bbbk_{\ell}(V_{cl}(s),v^{\prime})-\Bbbk_{N_{0}}(V_{cl}(s),v^{\prime})\big]\Bbbk_{\ell}(v^{\prime},v^{\prime\prime})

\displaystyle+\big[\Bbbk_{\ell}(v^{\prime},v^{\prime\prime})-\Bbbk_{N_{0}}(v^{\prime},v^{\prime\prime})\big]\Bbbk_{N_{0}}(V_{cl}(s),v^{\prime})+\Bbbk_{N_{0}}(V_{cl}(s),v^{\prime})\Bbbk_{N_{0}}(v^{\prime},v^{\prime\prime})\,,

we derive from (
3.33
) that

\displaystyle I^{3}_{3}\leq

\displaystyle\tfrac{C}{N_{0}}\sup_{s\in[0,t]}\|h^{\ell}_{R,\varepsilon}(s)\|_{\infty}

(3.34)

\displaystyle+\tfrac{C}{\varepsilon^{2}}\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\{|v^{\prime}|\leq 2N_{0},|v^{\prime\prime}|\leq 3N_{0}\}}\int_{0}^{s-\kappa_{*}\varepsilon}\mathrm{exp}\big\{-\tfrac{C\langle v\rangle^{\gamma_{0}}(t-s)}{\varepsilon}\big\}\mathrm{exp}\big\{-\tfrac{C\langle v^{\prime}\rangle^{\gamma_{0}}(s-s_{1})}{\varepsilon}\big\}

\displaystyle\times\big|\Bbbk_{N_{0}}(V_{cl}(s),v^{\prime})\Bbbk_{N_{0}}(v^{\prime},v^{\prime\prime})h^{\ell}_{R,\varepsilon}\big(s_{1},X_{cl}(s_{1};s,X_{cl}(s),v^{\prime}),v^{\prime\prime}\big)\big|\mathrm{d}s_{1}\mathrm{d}v^{\prime}\mathrm{d}v^{\prime\prime}\mathrm{d}s\,,

where the following bound has been used:

\sup_{|v^{\prime}|\leq 2N_{0}}\int_{\{|v^{\prime\prime}|\leq 3N_{0}\}}|\Bbbk_{\ell}(v^{\prime},v^{\prime\prime})\mathrm{d}v^{\prime\prime}+\sup_{|V_{cl}(s)|=|v|\leq 2N_{0}}\int_{\{|v^{\prime}|\leq 2N_{0}\}}|\Bbbk_{N_{0}}(V_{cl}(s),v^{\prime})|\mathrm{d}v^{\prime}\leq C\,.

For the integration over
v^{\prime}
in (
3.2
), we make a change of variable
v^{\prime}\to y:=X_{cl}\big(s_{1};s,X_{cl}(s),v^{\prime}\big)
. From the explicit formula (
3.2
), one has
\frac{\partial y}{\partial v^{\prime}}=-(s-s_{1})\mathrm{Diag}(1,1,1)
for
s_{1}\in[0,s-\varepsilon\kappa_{*}]
. Therefore, it holds that

\displaystyle\Big|\det\Big(\frac{\partial y}{\partial v^{\prime}}\Big)(s_{1})\Big|=(s-s_{1})^{s}\geq(\varepsilon\kappa_{*})^{3}>0\ {\text{for}}\ s_{1}\in[0,s-\kappa_{*}\varepsilon]\,,

which yields that for any
|v^{\prime\prime}|\leq 3N_{0}

\displaystyle\int_{|v^{\prime}|\leq 2N_{0}}\big|h^{\ell}_{R,\varepsilon}\big(s_{1},X_{cl}(s_{1};s,X_{cl}(s),v^{\prime}),v^{\prime\prime}\big)\big|\mathrm{d}v^{\prime}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{N_{0}}\Big(\int_{|v^{\prime}|\leq 2N_{0}}\mathbbm{1}_{\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+}}\big(X_{cl}(s_{1};s,X_{cl}(s),v^{\prime})\big)\big|h^{\ell}_{R,\varepsilon}\big(s_{1},X_{cl}(s_{1};s,X_{cl}(s),v^{\prime})\big)\big|^{2}\mathrm{d}v^{\prime}\Big)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\tfrac{C_{N_{0}}}{(\kappa_{*}\varepsilon)^{\frac{3}{2}}}\Big(\int_{|y|\leq 3N_{0}(s-s_{1})}\big|h^{\ell}_{R,\varepsilon}\big(s_{1},y,v^{\prime\prime})\big|^{2}\mathrm{d}y\Big)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\tfrac{C_{N_{0}}\big((s-s_{1})^{\frac{3}{2}}+1\big)}{(\kappa_{*}\varepsilon)^{\frac{3}{2}}}\Big(\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+}}\big|h^{\ell}_{R,\varepsilon}\big(s_{1},y,v^{\prime\prime})\big|^{2}\mathrm{d}y\Big)^{\frac{1}{2}}\,.

Together with the definition of
f_{R,\varepsilon}
and
h^{\ell}_{R,\varepsilon}
in (
1.59
), the last term in (
3.2
) can be bounded by
\tfrac{C_{N_{0},\kappa_{*}}}{\varepsilon^{\frac{3}{2}}}\sup_{s\in[0,t]}\|f_{R,\varepsilon}(s)\|_{2}
. One thereby has

(3.35)

\displaystyle I^{3}_{3}\leq\tfrac{C}{N_{0}}\sup_{s\in[0,t]}\|h^{\ell}_{R,\varepsilon}(s)\|_{\infty}+\tfrac{C_{N_{0},\kappa_{*}}}{\varepsilon^{\frac{3}{2}}}\sup_{s\in[0,t]}\|f_{R,\varepsilon}(s)\|_{2}\,.

Collecting the above all estimates implies that

(3.36)

\displaystyle\|h^{\ell}_{R,\varepsilon}\mathbbm{1}_{\mathscr{U}}(t)\|_{\infty}\leq C\varepsilon\|h^{\ell}_{R,\varepsilon}(0)\|_{\infty}+\aleph(h^{\ell}_{R,\varepsilon},f_{R,\varepsilon})\,.

where

(3.37)

\displaystyle\aleph(h^{\ell}_{R,\varepsilon},f_{R,\varepsilon})=

\displaystyle C(m^{3+\gamma_{0}}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}+\tfrac{1}{N_{0}}+\kappa_{*})\sup_{s\in[0,t]}\|h^{\ell}_{R,\varepsilon}(s)\|_{\infty}

\displaystyle+C\sqrt{\varepsilon}^{3}\sup_{s\in[0,t]}\|h^{\ell}_{R,\varepsilon}(s)\|_{\infty}^{2}+\tfrac{C_{N_{0}.\kappa_{*}}}{\varepsilon^{\frac{3}{2}}}\sup_{s\in[0,t]}\|f_{R,\varepsilon}(s)\|_{2}+C\sqrt{\varepsilon}^{2}\,.

Case 2:
(t,x,v)\in\mathscr{V}
.

Note that
t_{\star}=t_{1}>0
, and
(X_{cl}(t_{1}),V_{cl}(t_{1}))=(x_{b}(x,v),v)\in\Sigma_{-}
. Then, denoting by
x_{b}=x_{b}(x,v)
,

\displaystyle I_{1}=\mathrm{exp}\big\{-\tfrac{1}{\varepsilon}\int_{t_{1}}^{t}\nu(\phi)\mathrm{d}\phi\big\}h^{\ell}_{R,\varepsilon}(t_{1},x_{b},v)\,.

Recalling the boundary condition (
3.23
), one has

\displaystyle h^{\ell}_{R,\varepsilon}(t_{1},x_{b},v)=(1-\alpha_{\varepsilon})h^{\ell}_{R,\varepsilon}(t_{1},x_{b},R_{x_{b}}v)+\alpha_{\varepsilon}M_{w}\tfrac{\langle v\rangle^{\ell}}{\sqrt{\mathfrak{M}_{M}}}\int_{v^{\prime}\cdot n>0}(v^{\prime}\cdot n)\tfrac{\sqrt{\mathfrak{M}_{M}}}{\langle v^{\prime}\rangle^{\ell}}h^{\ell}_{R,\varepsilon}(t_{1},x_{b},v^{\prime})\mathrm{d}v^{\prime}\,.

Thanks to (
1.61
) and
M_{w}/\mathfrak{M}^{0}=\frac{1}{\rho^{0}}\sqrt{\frac{2\pi}{T^{0}}}
, one has
0<M_{w}\tfrac{\langle v\rangle^{\ell}}{\sqrt{\mathfrak{M}_{M}}}\int_{v^{\prime}\cdot n>0}(v^{\prime}\cdot n)\tfrac{\sqrt{\mathfrak{M}_{M}}}{\langle v^{\prime}\rangle^{\ell}}\mathrm{d}v^{\prime}\leq C
. Since
(t_{1},x_{b},R_{x_{b}}v),(t_{1},x_{b},v^{\prime})\in\mathscr{U}
,

\displaystyle|I_{1}|\leq(1-\alpha_{\varepsilon})|h^{\ell}_{R,\varepsilon}(t_{1},x_{b},R_{x_{b}}v)|+C\alpha_{\varepsilon}\sup_{v^{\prime}\in\mathcal{V}}|h^{\ell}_{R,\varepsilon}(t_{1},x_{b},v^{\prime})|\leq 2\|h^{\ell}_{R,\varepsilon}\mathbbm{1}_{\mathscr{U}}(t_{1})\|_{\infty}

for sufficiently small
\varepsilon>0
. Via employing the similar arguments in Case 1:
(t,x,v)\in\mathscr{U}
, the other terms except for
I_{1}
can be bounded by

\displaystyle I_{2}+I_{3}+I_{4}+I_{5}+I_{6}\leq

\displaystyle 2\|h^{\ell}_{R,\varepsilon}\mathbbm{1}_{\mathscr{U}}(t_{1})\|_{\infty}+\aleph(h^{\ell}_{R,\varepsilon},f_{R,\varepsilon})\,.

It thereby infers that

(3.38)

\displaystyle\|h^{\ell}_{R,\varepsilon}\mathbbm{1}_{\mathscr{V}}(t)\|_{\infty}\leq 4\|h^{\ell}_{R,\varepsilon}\mathbbm{1}_{\mathscr{U}}(t_{1})\|_{\infty}\aleph(h^{\ell}_{R,\varepsilon},f_{R,\varepsilon})\,.

Note that
\mathscr{U}\cup\mathscr{V}=[0,\tau]\times\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+}\times\mathbb{R}^{3}
. By choosing sufficiently small
\varepsilon>0
,
m>0
,
\kappa_{*}>0
and large
N_{0}>0
, (
3.36
) and (
3.38
) conclude Lemma
3.2
.
∎

4.
Boundary conditions for equations of fluid variables: Proof of Lemma
1.2

In this section, we will formally derive the boundary conditions for the linear hyperbolic system (
1.15
) and linear compressible Prandtl-type equations (
1.26
), which, respectively, are slip boundary conditions and Robin-type boundary conditions, hence, prove Lemma
1.2
.

Proof of Lemma
1.2
.

We will prove our conclusion from the solvability condition (
1.44
), i.e.,

\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}v_{3}\mathbbm{f}_{k}(t,\bar{x},v)\left(\begin{array}[]{c}1\\
(\bar{v}-\bar{\mathfrak{u}}^{0})\\
|v-\mathfrak{u}^{0}|^{2}\end{array}\right)\sqrt{\mathfrak{M}^{0}}\mathrm{d}v=0\,,

where
\mathbbm{f}_{k}(t,\bar{x},v)
is given in (
1.47
). We split

\displaystyle\mathbbm{f}_{k}(t,\bar{x},v)=\hat{g}_{k}(t,\bar{x},v)+\mathbbm{g}_{k}(t,\bar{x},v)\,,

where, for
v_{3}<0
,

\displaystyle\hat{g}_{k}(t,\bar{x},v)=

\displaystyle(f_{k}+f^{b}_{k}+f^{bb}_{k,1})(t,\bar{x},0,\bar{v},v_{3})-(f_{k}+f^{b}_{k}+f^{bb}_{k,1})(t,\bar{x},0,\bar{v},-v_{3})\,,

\displaystyle\mathbbm{g}_{k}(t,\bar{x},v)=

\displaystyle\sqrt{2\pi}\big\{\langle\gamma_{+}(f_{k-1}+f^{b}_{k-1}+f^{bb}_{k-1})\rangle_{\partial\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+}}\sqrt{\mathfrak{M}^{0}}-(f_{k-1}+f^{b}_{k-1}+f^{bb}_{k-1})(t,\bar{x},0,\bar{v},v_{3})\big\}\,,

ans they both vanish for
v_{3}>0
. We remark that
\mathbbm{g}_{k}(t,\bar{x},v)\equiv 0
in the case of specular reflection boundary condition, see
[
23
]
.

First,

\displaystyle 0=

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}v_{3}\mathbbm{f}_{k}(t,\bar{x},v)\sqrt{\mathfrak{M}^{0}}\mathrm{d}v=\underbrace{\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}v_{3}\hat{g}_{k}(t,\bar{x},v)\sqrt{\mathfrak{M}^{0}}\mathrm{d}v}_{Q_{1}}+\underbrace{\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}v_{3}\mathbbm{g}_{k}(t,\bar{x},v)\sqrt{\mathfrak{M}^{0}}\mathrm{d}v}_{Q_{2}}\,.

Following Section 2.4 of
[
23
]
,

(4.1)

\displaystyle Q_{1}=

\displaystyle\rho^{0}\big[u_{k,3}^{0}+u_{k,3}^{b,0}+T^{0}(\Psi_{k}^{0}+5T^{0}\Theta_{k}^{0})\big]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\rho^{0}\Big\{u_{k,3}^{0}+\int_{0}^{+\infty}\tfrac{1}{\rho^{0}}\big[\partial_{t}\rho^{b}_{k-1}+\mathrm{div}_{\bar{x}}\big(\rho^{0}\bar{u}^{b}_{k-1}+\rho^{b}_{k-1}\bar{\mathfrak{u}}^{0}\big)\big]\mathrm{d}\zeta+T^{0}(\Psi_{k}^{0}+5T^{0}\Theta_{k}^{0})\Big\}\,.

Since
M_{w}=\mathfrak{M}^{0}
and
\sqrt{2\pi}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\int_{-\infty}^{0}v_{3}M_{w}(v)\mathrm{d}\bar{v}\mathrm{d}v_{3}=-\rho^{0}\sqrt{T^{0}}
, we derive from the direct calculation that

(4.2)

\displaystyle Q_{2}=

\displaystyle\Big(\sqrt{2\pi}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\int_{-\infty}^{0}v_{3}M_{w}(v)\mathrm{d}\bar{v}\mathrm{d}v_{3}-1\Big)

\displaystyle\times\sqrt{2\pi}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\int_{-\infty}^{0}v_{3}(f_{k-1}+f^{b}_{k-1}+f^{bb}_{k-1})(t,\bar{x},0,\bar{v},v_{3})\sqrt{\mathfrak{M}^{0}}\mathrm{d}\bar{v}\mathrm{d}v_{3}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-(\rho^{0}\sqrt{T^{0}}+1)\sqrt{2\pi}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\int_{-\infty}^{0}v_{3}(f_{k-1}+f^{b}_{k-1}+f^{bb}_{k-1})(t,\bar{x},0,\bar{v},v_{3})\sqrt{\mathfrak{M}^{0}}\mathrm{d}\bar{v}\mathrm{d}v_{3}\,.

Consequently, the above three relations imply (
1.48
), and (
1.50
) will automatically hold by letting
k=1
.

Second, for
i=1,2
, one has

(4.3)

\displaystyle 0=

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}(v_{i}-\mathfrak{u}^{0}_{i})v_{3}\mathbbm{f}_{k}(t,\bar{x},v)\sqrt{\mathfrak{M}^{0}}\mathrm{d}v

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}(v_{i}-\mathfrak{u}^{0}_{i})v_{3}\hat{g}_{k}(t,\bar{x},v)\sqrt{\mathfrak{M}^{0}}\mathrm{d}v+\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}(v_{i}-\mathfrak{u}^{0}_{i})v_{3}\mathbbm{g}_{k}(t,\bar{x},v)\sqrt{\mathfrak{M}^{0}}\mathrm{d}v=:Y_{1}+Y_{2}\,.

As shown in
[
23
]
, together with (
1.50
), one has

(4.4)

\displaystyle Y_{1}=

\displaystyle\big[-\mu(T^{0})\partial_{\zeta}u^{b}_{k-1,i}+\rho^{0}\sqrt{T^{0}}(1+\rho^{0}\sqrt{T^{0}})u^{b}_{k-1,i}\big](t,\bar{x},0)+\rho^{0}\big[\big(u_{1,i}^{0}+u^{b,0}_{1,i}\big)u^{b,0}_{k-1,3}\big]

\displaystyle+T^{0}\langle\mathcal{A}^{0}_{3i},J^{b}_{k-2}+(\mathcal{I}-\mathcal{P}^{0})f_{k}\rangle(t,\bar{x},0)+\rho^{0}(T^{0})^{2}\big(\delta_{i1}\Phi_{k,1}^{0}+\delta_{i2}\Phi_{k,2}^{0}\big)\,.

Since
M_{w}=\mathfrak{M}^{0}
and
\sqrt{2\pi}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\int_{-\infty}^{0}(v_{i}-\mathfrak{u}^{0}_{i})v_{3}M_{w}(v)\mathrm{d}\bar{v}\mathrm{d}v_{3}=0
for
i=1,2
, one has

\displaystyle Y_{2}=

\displaystyle 2\pi\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\int_{-\infty}^{0}(v_{i}-\mathfrak{u}^{0}_{i})v_{3}M_{w}(v)\mathrm{d}\bar{v}\mathrm{d}v_{3}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\int_{-\infty}^{0}(-v_{3})(f_{k-1}+f^{b}_{k-1}+f^{bb}_{k-1})\sqrt{\mathfrak{M}^{0}}\mathrm{d}\bar{v}\mathrm{d}v_{3}

\displaystyle-\sqrt{2\pi}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\int_{-\infty}^{0}(v_{i}-\mathfrak{u}^{0}_{i})v_{3}(f_{k-1}+f^{b}_{k-1}+f^{bb}_{k-1})\sqrt{\mathfrak{M}^{0}}\mathrm{d}\bar{v}\mathrm{d}v_{3}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\sqrt{2\pi}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\int_{-\infty}^{0}(v_{i}-\mathfrak{u}^{0}_{i})v_{3}(f_{k-1}+f^{b}_{k-1}+f^{bb}_{k-1})\sqrt{\mathfrak{M}^{0}}\mathrm{d}\bar{v}\mathrm{d}v_{3}\,.

Noticing that for
i=1,2
,

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\int_{-\infty}^{0}(v_{i}-\mathfrak{u}^{0}_{i})v_{3}\mathcal{P}^{0}(f_{k-1}+f_{k-1}^{b})\sqrt{\mathfrak{M}^{0}}\mathrm{d}\bar{v}\mathrm{d}v_{3}=-\tfrac{\rho^{0}\sqrt{T^{0}}}{\sqrt{2\pi}}(u_{k-1,i}+u_{k-1,i}^{b})\,,

the
Y_{2}
can be further computed as

(4.5)

\displaystyle Y_{2}=

\displaystyle\rho^{0}\sqrt{T^{0}}(u_{k-1,i}^{0}+u_{k-1,i}^{b,0})

\displaystyle-\sqrt{2\pi}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\int_{-\infty}^{0}(v_{i}-\mathfrak{u}^{0}_{i})v_{3}[(\mathcal{I}-\mathcal{P}^{0})(f_{k-1}+f^{b}_{k-1})+f^{bb}_{k-1}]\sqrt{\mathfrak{M}^{0}}\mathrm{d}\bar{v}\mathrm{d}v_{3}\,.

Then, plugging (
4.4
) and (
4.5
) into (
4.3
) implies the first boundary condition of (
1.49
).

At the end,

(4.6)

\displaystyle 0=

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|v-\mathfrak{u}^{0}|^{2}v_{3}\mathbbm{f}_{k}(t,\bar{x},v)\sqrt{\mathfrak{M}^{0}}\mathrm{d}v

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\underbrace{\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|v-\mathfrak{u}^{0}|^{2}v_{3}\hat{g}_{k}(t,\bar{x},v)\sqrt{\mathfrak{M}^{0}}\mathrm{d}v}_{Z_{1}}+\underbrace{\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|v-\mathfrak{u}^{0}|^{2}v_{3}\mathbbm{g}_{k}(t,\bar{x},v)\sqrt{\mathfrak{M}^{0}}\mathrm{d}v}_{Z_{2}}

As computed in Section 2.4 of
[
23
]
,

(4.7)

\displaystyle Z_{1}=

\displaystyle-\kappa(T^{0})\partial_{\zeta}\theta^{b}_{k-1}(t,\bar{x},0)+2(T^{0})^{\frac{3}{2}}\langle\mathcal{B}^{0}_{3},(\mathcal{I}-\mathcal{P}_{0})f_{k}+J^{b}_{k-2}\rangle(t,\bar{x},0)

\displaystyle+\tfrac{5}{3}\rho^{0}[(\theta_{1}+\theta^{b}_{1})u^{b}_{k-1,3}](t,\bar{x},0)+10\rho^{0}(T^{0})^{3}\Theta_{k}(t,\bar{x},0)\,.

Since
M_{w}=\mathfrak{M}^{0}
and

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\int_{-\infty}^{0}v_{3}|v-\mathfrak{u}^{0}|^{2}M_{w}(v)\mathrm{d}\bar{v}\mathrm{d}v_{3}=-\tfrac{4\rho^{0}(T^{0})^{\frac{3}{2}}}{\sqrt{2\pi}}\,,

one has

\displaystyle Z_{2}=

\displaystyle 2\pi\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\int_{-\infty}^{0}v_{3}|v-\mathfrak{u}^{0}|^{2}M_{w}(v)\mathrm{d}\bar{v}\mathrm{d}v_{3}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\int_{-\infty}^{0}(-v_{3})(f_{k-1}+f_{k-1}^{b}+f_{k-1}^{bb})\sqrt{\mathfrak{M}^{0}}\mathrm{d}\bar{v}\mathrm{d}v_{3}

\displaystyle-\sqrt{2\pi}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\int_{-\infty}^{0}v_{3}|v-\mathfrak{u}^{0}|^{2}(f_{k-1}+f_{k-1}^{b}+f_{k-1}^{bb})\sqrt{\mathfrak{M}^{0}}\mathrm{d}\bar{v}\mathrm{d}v_{3}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\sqrt{2\pi}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\int_{-\infty}^{0}v_{3}(|v-\mathfrak{u}^{0}|^{2}-4\rho^{0}(T^{0})^{\frac{3}{2}})(f_{k-1}+f_{k-1}^{b}+f_{k-1}^{bb})\sqrt{\mathfrak{M}^{0}}\mathrm{d}\bar{v}\mathrm{d}v_{3}\,.

A direct calculation implies

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\int_{-\infty}^{0}v_{3}(|v-\mathfrak{u}^{0}|^{2}-4\rho^{0}(T^{0})^{\frac{3}{2}})\mathcal{P}^{0}(f_{k-1}+f_{k-1}^{b})\sqrt{\mathfrak{M}^{0}}\mathrm{d}\bar{v}\mathrm{d}v_{3}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\tfrac{\rho^{0}\sqrt{T^{0}}}{\sqrt{2\pi}}\big(2\rho^{0}\sqrt{T^{0}}+\tfrac{\sqrt{2\pi}}{3}\rho^{0}+\tfrac{2}{3}\big)\theta^{b}_{k-1}+\rho^{0}\big(\tfrac{1}{2}+\tfrac{3}{2}T^{0}-2\rho^{0}(T^{0})^{\frac{3}{2}}\big)(u_{k-1,3}+u^{b}_{k-1,3})

\displaystyle-\tfrac{\rho^{0}\sqrt{T^{0}}}{\sqrt{2\pi}}\big(\tfrac{2}{3}\rho^{0}\sqrt{T^{0}}+\tfrac{\sqrt{2\pi}}{3}\rho^{0}+2\big)\theta_{k-1}+\tfrac{4\sqrt{T^{0}}}{\sqrt{2\pi}}(\rho^{0}\sqrt{T^{0}}-1)(T^{0}\rho_{k-1}+p^{b}_{k-1})\,.

One therefore obtains

\displaystyle Z_{2}=

\displaystyle\rho^{0}\sqrt{T^{0}}\big(2\rho^{0}\sqrt{T^{0}}+\tfrac{\sqrt{2\pi}}{3}\rho^{0}+\tfrac{2}{3}\big)\theta^{b}_{k-1}-4\sqrt{T^{0}}(\rho^{0}\sqrt{T^{0}}-1)(T^{0}\rho_{k-1}+p^{b}_{k-1})

\displaystyle+\rho^{0}\sqrt{T^{0}}\big(\tfrac{2}{3}\rho^{0}\sqrt{T^{0}}+\tfrac{\sqrt{2\pi}}{3}\rho^{0}+2\big)\theta_{k-1}

(4.8)

\displaystyle-\sqrt{2\pi}\rho^{0}\big(\tfrac{1}{2}+\tfrac{3}{2}T^{0}-2\rho^{0}(T^{0})^{\frac{3}{2}}\big)(u_{k-1,3}+u^{b}_{k-1,3})

\displaystyle-\sqrt{2\pi}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\int_{-\infty}^{0}v_{3}(|v-\mathfrak{u}^{0}|^{2}-4\rho^{0}(T^{0})^{\frac{3}{2}})[(\mathcal{I}-\mathcal{P}^{0})(f_{k-1}+f_{k-1}^{b})+f_{k-1}^{bb}]\sqrt{\mathfrak{M}^{0}}\mathrm{d}\bar{v}\mathrm{d}v_{3}\,.

Consequently, the second condition of (
1.49
) is derived from using (
1.48
) and plugging (
4.7
)-(
4
) into (
4.6
), and then the proof of Lemma
1.2
is finished.
∎

5.
Existence for the linear compressible Prandtl-type system (
2.6
): Proof of Lemma
2.2

In this section, we focus on the existence of the smooth solutions to the linear compressible Prandtl-type equations (
2.6
) with Robin-type boundary conditions (
2.8
), which derives from the Maxwell reflection boundary condition (
1.2
). We remark that if the (
1.2
) is replaced by the specular reflection boundary condition, one can obtain Neumann boundary condition, see
[
23
]
.

Observe that the linear compressible Prandtl-type system (
2.6
) is a degenerated parabolic system with nontrivial Robin-type boundary values (
2.8
). First, we can introduce some explicit functions to zeroize the inhomogeneous boundary values (see (
5.4
)), which actually converts the boundary values into the form of external force term. We therefore obtain the system (
5.5
) with zero boundary values. We then construct a linear parabolic approximate system (
5.8
) while proving the existence of (
5.5
). When deriving the uniform bounds of the approximate system in the space
L^{\infty}(0,\tau;\mathbb{H}^{k}_{l}(\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+}))
, we will employ the structures of equations to convert the higher normal
\zeta
-derivatives to the values of tangential
\bar{x}
-derivatives and time derivatives on
\{\zeta=0\}
, see Lemma
5.2
below.

We first quote the following results to control some integral values on the boundary
\partial\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+}
by that in the interior
\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+}
. More precisely,

Lemma 5.1

(Lemma 8.1 of
[
23
]
)
.

Let
\Omega_{b}:=\{(\bar{x},x_{3}):\bar{x}\in\mathbb{R}^{2},x_{3}\in[0,b)\}
with
1\leq b\leq\infty
. We assume
f,g\in H^{1}(\Omega_{b})
. There holds, for any
x_{3}\in[0,b)
, that

(5.1)

\displaystyle\Big|\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}(fg)(\bar{x},x_{3})\mathrm{d}\bar{x}\Big|\leq\|\partial_{x_{3}}(f,g)\|_{L^{2}(\Omega_{b})}\|(f,g)\|_{L^{2}(\Omega_{b})}+\tfrac{1}{b}\|f\|_{L^{2}(\Omega_{b})}\|g\|_{L^{2}(\Omega_{b})}\,.

For
i=1,2
, there holds

(5.2)

\displaystyle\Big|\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}(\partial_{x_{i}}fg)(\bar{x},x_{3})\mathrm{d}\bar{x}\Big|\leq\|\partial_{x_{3}}(f,g)\|_{L^{2}(\Omega_{b})}

\displaystyle\|\partial_{x_{i}}(f,g)\|_{L^{2}(\Omega_{b})}+\tfrac{1}{b}\|\partial_{x_{i}}f\|_{L^{2}(\Omega_{b})}\|g\|_{L^{2}(\Omega_{b})}\,.

We remark that if
b=\infty
,
\Omega_{\infty}=\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+}
and the
\frac{1}{b}
-terms in (
5.1
)-(
5.2
) will be automatically vanished. We will apply Lemma
5.1
by letting
b=\frac{3}{\sigma}
, which means
0<\tfrac{1}{b}\leq\tfrac{1}{3}
for
0<\sigma\leq 1
below.

Proof of Lemma
2.2
.

We consider the system (
2.6
)-(
2.10
), namely,

(5.3)

\left\{\begin{aligned} &\rho^{0}(\partial_{t}+\bar{\mathfrak{u}}^{0}\cdot\nabla_{\bar{x}})u_{i}+\rho^{0}(\partial_{x_{3}}\mathfrak{u}^{0}_{3}\zeta+u^{0}_{1,3})\partial_{\zeta}u_{i}+\rho^{0}u\cdot\nabla_{\bar{x}}\mathfrak{u}^{0}_{i}+\tfrac{\partial_{x_{i}}p^{0}}{3T^{0}}\theta=\mu(T^{0})\partial_{\zeta}^{2}u_{i}+f_{i}\,,\\
&\rho^{0}(\partial_{t}+\bar{\mathfrak{u}}^{0}\cdot\nabla_{\bar{x}})\theta+\rho^{0}(\partial_{x_{3}}\mathfrak{u}^{0}_{3}\zeta+u^{0}_{1,3})\partial_{\zeta}\theta+\tfrac{2}{3}\rho^{0}\mathrm{div}_{x}\mathfrak{u}^{0}\theta=\tfrac{3}{5}\kappa(T^{0})\partial_{\zeta}^{2}\theta+g\,,\\
&(\partial_{\zeta}\theta-R_{\theta}\theta)|_{\zeta=0}=a(t,\bar{x})\,,\ \big(\partial_{\zeta}u_{i}-R_{u}u_{i}\big)\big|_{\zeta=0}=b_{i}(t,\bar{x})\,,\ i=1,2\,,\\
&\lim_{\zeta\to\infty}(u,\theta)(t,\bar{x},\zeta)=0\,,\\
&u(t,\bar{x},\zeta)|_{t=0}=u_{0}(\bar{x},\zeta)\in\mathbb{R}^{2}\,,\ \theta(t,\bar{x},\zeta)|_{t=0}=\theta_{0}(\bar{x},\zeta)\,.\end{aligned}\right.

We introduce the following functions
u_{b}(t,\bar{x},\zeta)=(u_{b1},u_{b2})(t,\bar{x},\zeta)
and
\theta_{a}(t,\bar{x},\zeta)
as

\displaystyle u_{bi}(t,\bar{x},\zeta)=(\tfrac{1}{R_{u}}+2\zeta)b_{i}(t,\bar{x})\chi(\zeta)\,,\ i=1,2\,,\ \theta_{a}(t,\bar{x},\zeta)=\big(\tfrac{1}{R_{\theta}}+2\zeta\big)a(t,\bar{x})\chi(\zeta)\,,

where

\chi(\zeta)=\left\{\begin{array}[]{l}1\,,\qquad 0\leq\zeta\leq 1\,,\\
0\,,\qquad\zeta\geq 2\,,\\
\end{array}\right.

is a monotone cut-off function belonging to
C^{\infty}\big([0,\infty)\big)
. It is easy to verify that

(5.4)

\lim_{\zeta\to\infty}(u_{b},\theta_{a})(t,\bar{x},\zeta)=0\,,\ \big(\partial_{\zeta}\theta_{a}-R_{\theta}\theta_{a}\big)|_{\zeta=0}=a(t,\bar{x})\,,\ (\partial_{\zeta}u_{b}-R_{u}u_{b})|_{\zeta=0}=b(t,\bar{x})\,.

Let
\mho=u-u_{b}\in\mathbb{R}^{2}
and
\Theta=\theta-\theta_{a}
. Combining with (
5.3
) implies

(5.5)

\left\{\begin{aligned} &\partial_{t}\mho+\bar{\mathfrak{u}}^{0}\cdot\nabla_{\bar{x}}\mho+(\partial_{x_{3}}\mathfrak{u}^{0}_{3}\zeta+u_{1,3}^{0})\partial_{\zeta}\mho+\mho\cdot\nabla_{\bar{x}}\bar{\mathfrak{u}}^{0}+\tfrac{\nabla_{\bar{x}}p^{0}}{3T^{0}}\Theta=\tilde{\mu}\partial_{\zeta}^{2}\mho+\tilde{f}\,,\\
&\partial_{t}\Theta+\bar{\mathfrak{u}}^{0}\cdot\nabla_{\bar{x}}\Theta+(\partial_{x_{3}}\mathfrak{u}^{0}_{3}\zeta+u^{0}_{1,3})\partial_{\zeta}\Theta+\tfrac{2}{3}\mathrm{div}_{x}\mathfrak{u}^{0}\Theta=\tilde{\kappa}\partial_{\zeta}^{2}\Theta+\tilde{g}\,,\\
&\lim_{\zeta\to\infty}(\mho,\Theta)(t,\bar{x},\zeta)=0\,,\ (\partial_{\zeta}\mho-R_{u}\mho)|_{\zeta=0}=0\,,\ (\partial_{\zeta}\Theta-R_{\theta}\Theta)|_{\zeta=0}=0\,,\\
&(\mho,\Theta)(t,\bar{x},\zeta)|_{t=0}=(\mho,\Theta)(0):=\big(u_{0}(\bar{x},\zeta)-u_{b}(0,\bar{x},\zeta)\,,\theta_{0}(\bar{x},\zeta)-\theta_{a}(0,\bar{x},\zeta)\big)\,,\end{aligned}\right.

where

(5.6)

\displaystyle\tilde{\mu}=

\displaystyle\tfrac{1}{\rho^{0}}\mu(T^{0})\,,\ \,\tilde{f}=\tfrac{1}{\rho^{0}}f-\partial_{t}u_{b}-\bar{\mathfrak{u}}^{0}\cdot\nabla_{\bar{x}}u_{b}-(\partial_{x_{3}}\mathfrak{u}^{0}_{3}\zeta+u^{0}_{1,3})\partial_{\zeta}u_{b}-u_{b}\cdot\nabla_{\bar{x}}\bar{\mathfrak{u}}^{0}-\tfrac{\nabla_{\bar{x}}p^{0}}{3\rho^{0}T^{0}}\theta_{a}\,,

\displaystyle\tilde{\kappa}=

\displaystyle\tfrac{3}{5\rho^{0}}\kappa(T^{0})\,,\ \tilde{g}=\tfrac{1}{\rho^{0}}g-\partial_{t}\theta_{a}-\bar{\mathfrak{u}}^{0}\cdot\nabla_{\bar{x}}\theta_{a}-(\partial_{x_{3}}\mathfrak{u}^{0}_{3}\zeta+u^{0}_{1,3})\partial_{\zeta}\theta_{a}-\tfrac{2}{3}\mathrm{div}_{x}\mathfrak{u}^{0}\theta_{a}+\tilde{\kappa}\partial_{\zeta}^{2}\theta_{a}\,.

From Proposition
1.1
and (
2.7
), it is easy to see

(5.7)

\displaystyle\tilde{\mu}\geq\tfrac{2\mu_{0}}{\rho_{\#}}:=\tilde{\mu}_{0}>0\,,\ \tilde{\kappa}\geq\tfrac{2\kappa_{0}}{\rho_{\#}}:=\tilde{\kappa}_{0}>0\,.

To prove the existence of smooth solution to (
5.5
), we first construct the following approximate system

(5.8)

\left\{\begin{aligned} &\partial_{t}\mho+\bar{\mathfrak{u}}^{0}\cdot\nabla_{\bar{x}}\mho+(\partial_{x_{3}}\mathfrak{u}^{0}_{3}\zeta+u^{0}_{1,3})\chi_{\sigma}(\zeta)\partial_{\zeta}\mho+\mho\cdot\nabla_{\bar{x}}\bar{\mathfrak{u}}^{0}+\tfrac{\nabla_{\bar{x}}p^{0}}{3T^{0}}\Theta=\tilde{\mu}\partial_{\zeta}^{2}\mho+\lambda\Delta_{\bar{x}}\mho+\tilde{f}^{\sigma}\,,\\
&\partial_{t}\Theta+\bar{\mathfrak{u}}^{0}\cdot\nabla_{\bar{x}}\Theta+(\partial_{x_{3}}\mathfrak{u}^{0}_{3}\zeta+u^{0}_{1,3})\chi_{\sigma}(\zeta)\partial_{\zeta}\Theta+\tfrac{2}{3}\mathrm{div}_{x}\mathfrak{u}^{0}\Theta=\tilde{\kappa}\partial_{\zeta}^{2}\Theta+\lambda\Delta_{\bar{x}}\Theta+\tilde{g}^{\sigma}\,,\\
&(\partial_{\zeta}\mho-R_{u}\mho)|_{\zeta=0}=0\,,\ (\partial_{\zeta}\Theta-R_{\theta}\Theta)|_{\zeta=0}=0\,,\quad(\mho,\Theta)(t,\bar{x},\zeta)|_{\zeta=\frac{3}{\sigma}}=0\,,\\
&\mho(0,\bar{x},\zeta)=(u_{0}-u_{b})\chi_{\sigma}(\zeta)\,,\ \Theta(0,\bar{x},\zeta)=(\theta_{0}-\theta_{a})\chi_{\sigma}(\zeta)\end{aligned}\right.

for
(t,\bar{x},\zeta)\in[0,\tau]\times\mathbb{R}^{2}\times[0,\tfrac{3}{\sigma}]
and
0<\sigma,\lambda\leq 1
, where
\chi_{\sigma}(\zeta)=\chi(\sigma\zeta)
,
\tilde{f}^{\sigma}=\tilde{f}\chi_{\sigma}(\zeta)
and
\tilde{g}^{\sigma}=\tilde{g}\chi_{\sigma}(\zeta)
. One notices that the compatibility condition of initial data at
\zeta=\tfrac{3}{\sigma}
is also satisfied due to the property of
\chi(\zeta)
. For the approximate problem (
5.8
), we can use the standard linear parabolic theory to obtain the existence of smooth solution in Sobolev space provided that the initial data and
(\rho^{0},\mathfrak{u}^{0},T^{0})
are suitably smooth. To prove the existence of the smooth solutions to (
5.5
), we only need to obtain some uniform estimates of
(\mho,\Theta)
associated with
\sigma
and
\lambda
, then take the limit
\sigma,\lambda\to 0+
.

We will first estimate the uniform bounds on
\Theta
. The derivation of uniform bounds for
\mho
is similar to that for
\Theta
. For simplicity, we still employ the notation

\displaystyle\|f\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{l}}=\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\int_{0}^{\frac{3}{\sigma}}(1+\zeta)^{l}|f(\bar{x},\zeta)|^{2}\mathrm{d}\bar{x}\mathrm{d}\zeta

in (
1.64
) and, correspondingly, employ the notations
\|f(t)\|^{2}_{\mathbb{H}^{r}_{l}(\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+})}
and
\|g\|_{\mathbb{H}^{k}_{l}(\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+})}
in (
1.66
) and (
1.67
), respectively. Moreover, the following properties of
\chi_{\sigma}(\zeta)
will be frequently used:

(5.9)

\displaystyle\zeta\chi_{\sigma}(\zeta)|_{\zeta=0}=\zeta\chi_{\sigma}(\zeta)|_{\zeta=\frac{3}{\sigma}}=0\,,\ \big|\partial_{\zeta}[\zeta(1+\zeta)^{l}\chi_{\sigma}(\zeta)]\big|\leq C(1+\zeta)^{l}\ \textrm{for any }l\in\mathbb{N}^{*}\,,

\displaystyle\big|\partial_{\zeta}^{n}(\zeta\chi_{\sigma}(\zeta))\big|\leq C(1+\zeta)\ \textrm{ for any }n\geq 0\,,\ [\partial_{\zeta}^{n},\zeta\chi_{\sigma}(\zeta)]f|_{\zeta=0}=n\partial_{\zeta}^{n-1}f|_{\zeta=0}\ \textrm{ for any }n\geq 1\,.

Furthermore, by letting
k\geq 3
,
F^{0}=F^{0}(\rho^{0},\mathfrak{u}^{0},T^{0},\nabla_{x}(\rho^{0},\mathfrak{u}^{0},T^{0}))
, and employing the standard Sobolev embedding arguments, one sees that for any
2\gamma+|\beta|+n\leq k

\displaystyle\big|\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\int_{0}^{\frac{3}{\sigma}}

\displaystyle(1+\zeta)^{l}|\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta}\partial_{\zeta}^{n}(F^{0}\Theta)|^{2}\mathrm{d}\bar{x}\mathrm{d}\zeta\big|\leq\|F^{0}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}_{t,\bar{x}}}\|\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta}\partial_{\zeta}^{n}\Theta\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{l}}

\displaystyle+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}0\neq|\beta^{\prime}|+2\gamma^{\prime}\leq|\beta|+2\gamma\\
\beta^{\prime}\leq\beta,\gamma^{\prime}\leq\gamma\end{subarray}}C_{\beta}^{\beta^{\prime}}C_{\gamma}^{\gamma^{\prime}}\|\partial_{t}^{\gamma^{\prime}}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta^{\prime}}F^{0}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{4}_{\bar{x}}}\|(1+\zeta)^{\frac{l}{2}}\partial_{t}^{\gamma-\gamma^{\prime}}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta-\beta^{\prime}}\partial_{\zeta}^{n}\Theta\|^{2}_{L^{4}_{\bar{x}}L^{2}_{\zeta}}

\displaystyle\leq C(E_{k+1})\|\Theta(t)\|^{2}_{\mathbb{H}^{k}_{l}(\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+})}\leq C(E_{k+1})\|\Theta(t)\|^{2}_{\mathbb{H}^{k}_{l}(\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+})}

holds for all
\Theta\in\mathbb{H}^{k}_{l}(\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+})
, which will be directly used to deal with the coefficients depending on
(\rho^{0},\mathfrak{u}^{0},T^{0})
in what follows. Here the symbol
E_{k+1}
is defined in (
2.1
).

Step 1. The zero-derivatives estimates:
L^{2}_{l_{0}}
-bounds.
For integer
l_{0}
given in (
1.65
) with
s=k
, multiplying the
\Theta
-equation of (
5.8
) with
(1+\zeta)^{l_{0}}\Theta
, and integrating the resultant over
[0,t]\times\mathbb{R}^{2}\times[0,\tfrac{3}{\sigma}]
, one has

\displaystyle\tfrac{1}{2}\|\Theta(t)\|_{L^{2}_{l_{0}}}^{2}-\tfrac{1}{2}\|\Theta(0)\|_{L^{2}_{l_{0}}}^{2}=-\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\int_{0}^{\frac{3}{\sigma}}(\partial_{x_{3}}\mathfrak{u}^{0}_{3}\zeta+u^{0}_{1,3})\chi_{\sigma}(\zeta)\partial_{\zeta}\Theta(1+\zeta)^{l_{0}}\Theta\mathrm{d}\bar{x}\mathrm{d}\zeta\mathrm{d}s

\displaystyle-\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\int_{0}^{\frac{3}{\sigma}}\tfrac{2}{3}\mathrm{div}_{x}\mathfrak{u}^{0}\Theta(1+\zeta)^{l_{0}}\Theta\mathrm{d}\bar{x}\mathrm{d}\zeta\mathrm{d}s+\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\int_{0}^{\frac{3}{\sigma}}\tilde{\kappa}\partial_{\zeta}^{2}\Theta(1+\zeta)^{l_{0}}\Theta\mathrm{d}\bar{x}\mathrm{d}\zeta\mathrm{d}s

\displaystyle+\lambda\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\int_{0}^{\frac{3}{\sigma}}\Delta_{\bar{x}}\Theta(1+\zeta)^{l_{0}}\Theta\mathrm{d}\bar{x}\mathrm{d}\zeta\mathrm{d}s+\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\int_{0}^{\frac{3}{\sigma}}\tilde{g}^{\sigma}(1+\zeta)^{l_{0}}\Theta\mathrm{d}\bar{x}\mathrm{d}\zeta\mathrm{d}s

\displaystyle=:I_{1}+I_{2}+I_{3}+I_{4}+I_{5}\,,

where
u^{0}_{1,3}=\sqrt{T^{0}}(\rho^{0}\sqrt{T^{0}}+1)>0
is given in (
1.50
). Straightforward calculation gives us

\displaystyle I_{1}=

\displaystyle-\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}(\partial_{x_{3}}\mathfrak{u}^{0}_{3}\zeta+u^{0}_{1,3})\chi_{\sigma}(\zeta)(1+\zeta)^{l_{0}}\tfrac{1}{2}\Theta^{2}|_{\zeta=0}^{\zeta=\tfrac{3}{\sigma}}\mathrm{d}\bar{x}\mathrm{d}s

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\int_{0}^{\frac{3}{\sigma}}\{\partial_{x_{3}}\mathfrak{u}^{0}_{3}\partial_{\zeta}\big[\zeta(1+\zeta)^{l_{0}}\chi_{\sigma}(\zeta)\big]+u^{0}_{1,3}\partial_{\zeta}\big[(1+\zeta)^{l_{0}}\chi_{\sigma}(\zeta)\big]\}\tfrac{1}{2}\Theta^{2}\mathrm{d}\bar{x}\mathrm{d}\zeta\mathrm{d}s

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\tfrac{1}{2}\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}u^{0}_{1,3}\Theta^{2}|_{\zeta=0}\mathrm{d}\bar{x}\mathrm{d}s+C(E_{k+1})\int_{0}^{t}\|\Theta(s)\|_{L^{2}_{l_{0}}}^{2}\mathrm{d}s

(5.10)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C(E_{k+1})\int_{0}^{t}\|\Theta|_{\zeta=0}(s)\|^{2}_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{2})}\mathrm{d}s+C(E_{k+1})\int_{0}^{t}\|\Theta(s)\|_{L^{2}_{l_{0}}}^{2}\mathrm{d}s

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\tfrac{1}{4}\int_{0}^{t}\tilde{\kappa}_{0}\|\partial_{\zeta}\Theta(s)\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{l_{0}}}\mathrm{d}s+C(E_{k+1})\int_{0}^{t}\|\Theta(s)\|_{L^{2}_{l_{0}}}^{2}\mathrm{d}s\,,

where the facts about
\chi_{\sigma}(\zeta)
given in (
5.9
) have been used, and the last inequality is derived from the inequality (
5.1
) in Lemma
5.1
and the Young’s inequality. Furthermore,

\displaystyle|I_{2}|\leq C(E_{k+1})\int_{0}^{t}\|\Theta(s)\|_{L^{2}_{l_{0}}}^{2}\mathrm{d}s\,,\quad|I_{5}|\leq C\int_{0}^{t}\|(\Theta,\tilde{g}^{\sigma})(s)\|_{L^{2}_{l_{0}}}^{2}\mathrm{d}s\,.

Together with the boundary conditions in (
5.8
), one can also compute

\displaystyle I_{3}=

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\tilde{\kappa}(1+\zeta)^{l_{0}}\partial_{\zeta}\Theta\Theta|_{\zeta=0}^{\zeta=\frac{3}{\sigma}}\mathrm{d}\bar{x}\mathrm{d}s-\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\int_{0}^{\frac{3}{\sigma}}(1+\zeta)^{l_{0}}(\partial_{\zeta}\Theta)^{2}\mathrm{d}\bar{x}\mathrm{d}\zeta\mathrm{d}s

\displaystyle-\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\int_{0}^{\frac{3}{\sigma}}l_{0}\tilde{\kappa}(1+\zeta)^{l_{0}-1}\partial_{\zeta}\Theta\Theta\mathrm{d}\bar{x}\mathrm{d}\zeta\mathrm{d}s

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle-\int_{0}^{t}\tilde{\kappa}_{0}\|\partial_{\zeta}\Theta(s)\|_{L^{2}_{l_{0}}}^{2}\mathrm{d}s+\tfrac{1}{2}\int_{0}^{t}\tilde{\kappa}_{0}\|\partial_{\zeta}\Theta(s)\|_{L^{2}_{l_{0}}}^{2}\mathrm{d}s

\displaystyle+C\int_{0}^{t}\|\Theta(s)\|_{L^{2}_{l_{0}}}^{2}\mathrm{d}s-\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\tilde{\kappa}R_{\theta}\Theta^{2}|_{\zeta=0}\mathrm{d}\bar{x}\mathrm{d}s

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle-\tfrac{1}{2}\int_{0}^{t}\tilde{\kappa}_{0}\|\partial_{\zeta}\Theta(s)\|_{L^{2}_{l_{0}}}^{2}\mathrm{d}s-\int_{0}^{t}\tilde{\kappa}_{0}R^{0}_{\theta}\|\Theta|_{\zeta=0}(s)\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{2})}^{2}\mathrm{d}s+C\int_{0}^{t}\|\Theta(s)\|_{L^{2}_{l_{0}}}^{2}\mathrm{d}s\,,

where the lower bounds (
2.9
) and (
5.7
), i.e.,
0<\tilde{\kappa}_{0}\leq\tilde{\kappa}
and
0<R^{0}_{\theta}\leq R_{\theta}
, are also used. For
I_{4}
, one has

I_{4}=-\lambda\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\int_{0}^{\frac{3}{\sigma}}(1+\zeta)^{l_{0}}|\nabla_{\bar{x}}\Theta|^{2}\mathrm{d}\bar{x}\mathrm{d}\zeta\mathrm{d}s=\lambda\int_{0}^{t}\|\nabla_{\bar{x}}\Theta(s)\|_{L^{2}_{l_{0}}}^{2}\mathrm{d}s\,.

Therefore, one has

(5.11)

\displaystyle\|\Theta(s)\|_{L^{2}_{l_{0}}}^{2}+\tfrac{1}{2}\int_{0}^{t}\tilde{\kappa}_{0}\|\partial_{\zeta}\Theta(s)\|_{L^{2}_{l_{0}}}^{2}+\lambda\|\nabla_{\tilde{x}}\Theta(s)\|_{L^{2}_{l_{0}}}^{2}\mathrm{d}s+\int_{0}^{t}c_{2}^{\prime}\|\Theta|_{\zeta=0}(s)\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{2})}^{2}\mathrm{d}s

\displaystyle\leq C\big(\|\Theta(0)\|_{L^{2}_{l_{0}}}^{2}+\int_{0}^{t}\|\tilde{g}^{\sigma}(s)\|_{L^{2}_{l_{0}}}^{2}\mathrm{d}s\big)+C(E_{k+1})\int_{0}^{t}\|\Theta(s)\|_{L^{2}_{l_{0}}}^{2}\mathrm{d}s\,,

where
c_{2}^{\prime}=\tilde{\kappa}_{0}R^{0}_{\theta}>0
.

Step 2. The higher order
(t,\bar{x})
-derivatives estimates:
\mathbb{H}^{r}_{l,0}(\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+})
-bounds.
For
2\gamma+|\beta|=r
with
\gamma\in\mathbb{N},\beta\in\mathbb{N}^{2}
and
1\leq r\leq k
, applying
\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta}
to the
\Theta
-equation of (
5.8
) infers that

(5.12)

\displaystyle\partial_{t}\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta}\Theta+\bar{\mathfrak{u}}^{0}\cdot\nabla_{\bar{x}}\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta}\Theta+(\partial_{x_{3}}\mathfrak{u}^{0}_{3}\zeta+u^{0}_{1,3})\chi_{\sigma}(\zeta)\partial_{\zeta}\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta}\Theta

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta}(\tilde{\kappa}\partial_{\zeta}^{2}\Theta)+\lambda\Delta_{\bar{x}}\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta}\Theta-[\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta},\bar{\mathfrak{u}}^{0}\cdot\nabla_{\bar{x}}]\Theta-\zeta\chi_{\sigma}(\zeta)[\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta},\partial_{x_{3}}\mathfrak{u}^{0}_{3}\partial_{\zeta}]\Theta

\displaystyle-\chi_{\sigma}(\zeta)[\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta},u^{0}_{1,3}\partial_{\zeta}]\Theta-\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta}(\tfrac{2}{3}\mathrm{div}_{\bar{x}}\mathfrak{u}^{0}\Theta)+\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta}\tilde{g}^{\sigma}\,,

where
[X,Y]=XY-YX
is the commutator operator. Multiplying (
5.12
) with
(1+\zeta)^{l_{r}}\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta}\Theta
and integrating over
[0,t]\times\mathbb{R}^{2}\times[0,\tfrac{3}{\sigma}]
reduce to

(5.13)

\displaystyle\tfrac{1}{2}\|\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta}\Theta(s)\|_{L^{2}_{l_{r}}}^{2}-\tfrac{1}{2}\|\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta}\Theta(0)\|_{L^{2}_{l_{r}}}^{2}=\sum_{k=1}^{9}II_{k}\,,

where

\displaystyle II_{1}=

\displaystyle-\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\int_{0}^{\frac{3}{\sigma}}(\partial_{x_{3}}\mathfrak{u}^{0}_{3}\zeta+u^{0}_{1,3})\chi_{\sigma}(\zeta)\partial_{\zeta}\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta}\Theta\cdot(1+\zeta)^{l_{r}}\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta}\Theta\mathrm{d}\bar{x}\mathrm{d}\zeta\mathrm{d}s\,,

\displaystyle II_{2}=

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\int_{0}^{\frac{3}{\sigma}}\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta}(\tilde{\kappa}\partial_{\zeta}^{2}\Theta)(1+\zeta)^{l_{r}}\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta}\Theta\mathrm{d}\bar{x}\mathrm{d}\zeta\mathrm{d}s\,,

\displaystyle II_{3}=

\displaystyle\lambda\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\int_{0}^{\frac{3}{\sigma}}\Delta_{\bar{x}}\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta}\Theta\cdot(1+\zeta)^{l_{r}}\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta}\Theta\mathrm{d}\bar{x}\mathrm{d}\zeta\mathrm{d}s\,,

\displaystyle II_{4}=

\displaystyle-\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\int_{0}^{\frac{3}{\sigma}}\bar{\mathfrak{u}}^{0}\cdot\nabla_{\bar{x}}\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta}\Theta\cdot(1+\zeta)^{l_{r}}\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta}\Theta\mathrm{d}\bar{x}\mathrm{d}\zeta\mathrm{d}s\,,

\displaystyle II_{5}=

\displaystyle-\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\int_{0}^{\frac{3}{\sigma}}[\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta},\bar{\mathfrak{u}}^{0}\cdot\nabla_{\bar{x}}]\Theta\cdot(1+\zeta)^{l_{r}}\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta}\Theta\mathrm{d}\bar{x}\mathrm{d}\zeta\mathrm{d}s\,,

\displaystyle II_{6}=

\displaystyle-\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\int_{0}^{\frac{3}{\sigma}}\zeta\chi_{\sigma}(\zeta)[\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta},\partial_{x_{3}}\mathfrak{u}^{0}_{3}\partial_{\zeta}]\Theta\cdot(1+\zeta)^{l_{r}}\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta}\Theta\mathrm{d}\bar{x}\mathrm{d}\zeta\mathrm{d}s\,,

\displaystyle II_{7}=

\displaystyle-\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\int_{0}^{\frac{3}{\sigma}}\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta}(\tfrac{2}{3}\mathrm{div}_{x}\mathfrak{u}^{0}\Theta)\cdot(1+\zeta)^{l_{r}}\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta}\Theta\mathrm{d}\bar{x}\mathrm{d}\zeta\mathrm{d}s\,,

\displaystyle II_{8}=

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\int_{0}^{\frac{3}{\sigma}}\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta}\tilde{g}^{\sigma}\cdot(1+\zeta)^{l_{r}}\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta}\Theta\mathrm{d}\bar{x}\mathrm{d}\zeta\mathrm{d}s\,,

\displaystyle II_{9}=

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\int_{0}^{\frac{3}{\sigma}}\chi_{\sigma}(\zeta)[\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta},u^{0}_{1,3}\partial_{\zeta}]\Theta\cdot(1+\zeta)^{l_{r}}\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta}\Theta\mathrm{d}\bar{x}\mathrm{d}\zeta\mathrm{d}s\,,

We then estimate the terms in the RHS of (
5.13
) one by one. Integrating by parts with respect to the variable
\zeta
and employing the similar derivation of
I_{1}
in (
5
) before give us

(5.14)

\displaystyle II_{1}\leq

\displaystyle\tfrac{1}{4}\int_{0}^{t}\tilde{\kappa}_{0}\|\partial_{\zeta}\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta}\Theta(s)\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{l_{r}}}\mathrm{d}s+C(E_{k+1})\int_{0}^{t}\|\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta}\Theta(s)\|_{L^{2}_{l_{r}}}^{2}\mathrm{d}s\,.

For
II_{2}
, a direct calculation infers that

\displaystyle II_{2}=

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta}(\tilde{\kappa}\partial_{\zeta}\Theta)(1+\zeta)^{l_{r}}\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta}\Theta|_{\zeta=0}^{\zeta=\frac{3}{\sigma}}\mathrm{d}\bar{x}\mathrm{d}s-\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\int_{0}^{\frac{3}{\sigma}}\tilde{\kappa}(1+\zeta)^{l_{r}}(\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta}\Theta)^{2}\mathrm{d}\bar{x}\mathrm{d}\zeta\mathrm{d}s

\displaystyle-\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\int_{0}^{\frac{3}{\sigma}}(1+\zeta)^{l_{r}}[\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta},\tilde{\kappa}\partial_{\zeta}]\Theta\cdot\partial_{\zeta}\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta}\Theta\mathrm{d}\bar{x}\mathrm{d}\zeta\mathrm{d}s=:II_{21}+II_{22}+II_{23}\,.

The quantity
II_{21}
is bounded by

\displaystyle-\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\tilde{\kappa}R_{\theta}(\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta}\Theta)^{2}|_{\zeta=0}\mathrm{d}\bar{x}\mathrm{d}s-\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}[\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta},\tilde{\kappa}R_{\theta}]\Theta\cdot\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta}\Theta|_{\zeta=0}\mathrm{d}\bar{x}\mathrm{d}s

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle-\tfrac{3}{4}\int_{0}^{t}\tilde{\kappa}_{0}R^{0}_{\theta}\|\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta}\Theta|_{\zeta=0}(s)\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{2})}^{2}\mathrm{d}s+C(E_{k+1})\int_{0}^{t}\|\Theta|_{\zeta=0}(s)\|^{2}_{\mathbb{H}^{r-1}(\mathbb{R}^{2})}\mathrm{d}s\,,

where the boundary values
\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta}(\tilde{\kappa}\partial_{\zeta}\Theta)|_{\zeta=0}=\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta}(\tilde{\kappa}R_{\theta}\Theta)|_{\zeta=0}
and
\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta}\Theta|_{\zeta=\frac{3}{\sigma}}=0
have been used. The quantity
II_{22}
is bounded by

\displaystyle-\int_{0}^{t}\tilde{\kappa}_{0}\|\partial_{\zeta}\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta}\Theta(s)\|_{L^{2}_{l_{r}}}^{2}\mathrm{d}s\,,

and the quantity
II_{23}
is bounded by

\displaystyle\tfrac{1}{4}\int_{0}^{t}\tilde{\kappa}_{0}\|\partial_{\zeta}\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta}\Theta(s)\|_{L^{2}_{l_{r}}}^{2}\mathrm{d}s+C(E_{k+1})\sum_{j=1}^{r-1}\sum_{2\tilde{\gamma}+|\tilde{\beta}|=j}\int_{0}^{t}\|\partial_{\zeta}\partial_{t}^{\tilde{\gamma}}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\tilde{\beta}}\Theta(s)\|_{L^{2}_{l_{j+1}}}^{2}\mathrm{d}s

\displaystyle\leq\tfrac{1}{4}\int_{0}^{t}\tilde{\kappa}_{0}\|\partial_{\zeta}\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta}\Theta(s)\|_{L^{2}_{l_{r}}}^{2}\mathrm{d}s+C(E_{k+1})\int_{0}^{t}\|\partial_{\zeta}\Theta(s)\|^{2}_{\mathbb{H}^{r-1}_{l,0}(\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+})}\mathrm{d}s\,,

where we require
l_{j}\geq l_{j+1}
. The above estimates also require the lower bounds (
2.9
) and (
5.7
). Consequently,

\displaystyle II_{2}\leq

\displaystyle-\tfrac{3}{4}\int_{0}^{t}\tilde{\kappa}_{0}\|\partial_{\zeta}\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta}\Theta(s)\|_{L^{2}_{l_{r}}}^{2}\mathrm{d}s-\tfrac{3}{4}\int_{0}^{t}\tilde{\kappa}_{0}R^{0}_{\theta}\|\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta}\Theta|_{\zeta=0}(s)\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{2})}^{2}\mathrm{d}s

(5.15)

\displaystyle+C(E_{k+1})\int_{0}^{t}\|\partial_{\zeta}\Theta(s)\|^{2}_{\mathbb{H}^{r-1}_{l,0}(\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+})}+\|\Theta|_{\zeta=0}(s)\|^{2}_{\mathbb{H}^{r-1}(\mathbb{R}^{2})}\mathrm{d}s\,.

Similarly, one also has

(5.16)

\displaystyle II_{3}=-\lambda\int_{0}^{t}\|\nabla_{\bar{x}}\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta}\Theta(s)\|_{L^{2}_{l_{r}}}^{2}\mathrm{d}s\,,\ |II_{5}|\leq C(E_{k+1})\int_{0}^{t}\|\Theta(s)\|_{\mathbb{H}^{r}_{l,0}(\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+})}^{2}\mathrm{d}s\,,

\displaystyle|II_{7}|+|II_{8}|\leq C(E_{k+1})\int_{0}^{t}\|\Theta(s)\|_{\mathbb{H}^{r}_{l,0}(\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+})}^{2}\mathrm{d}s+C\int_{0}^{t}\|\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta}\tilde{g}^{\sigma}(s)\|_{L^{2}_{l_{r}}}^{2}\mathrm{d}s\,,

and

(5.17)

\displaystyle|II_{4}|=\big|\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\int_{0}^{\frac{3}{\sigma}}\mathrm{div}_{\bar{x}}\bar{\mathfrak{u}}^{0}(1+\zeta)^{l_{r}}\tfrac{1}{2}(\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta}\Theta(s))^{2}\mathrm{d}\bar{x}\mathrm{d}\zeta\mathrm{d}s\big|\leq C(E_{k+1})\int_{0}^{t}\|\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta}\Theta(s)\|_{L^{2}_{l_{r}}}^{2}\mathrm{d}s\,,

and

\displaystyle|II_{6}|\leq

\displaystyle C(E_{k+1})\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\int_{0}^{\frac{3}{\sigma}}(1+\zeta)^{l_{r}+1}\sum_{j=0}^{r-1}\sum_{2\tilde{\gamma}+|\tilde{\beta}|=j}|\partial_{\zeta}\partial_{t}^{\tilde{\gamma}}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\tilde{\beta}}\Theta||\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta}\Theta|\mathrm{d}\bar{x}\mathrm{d}\zeta\mathrm{d}s

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C(E_{k+1})\sum_{j=0}^{r-1}\sum_{2\tilde{\gamma}+|\tilde{\beta}|=j}\Big(\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\int_{0}^{\frac{3}{\sigma}}(1+\zeta)^{l_{r}+2}|\partial_{\zeta}\partial_{t}^{\tilde{\gamma}}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\tilde{\beta}}\Theta|^{2}\bar{x}\mathrm{d}\zeta\mathrm{d}s\Big)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\times\Big(\int_{0}^{t}\|\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta}\Theta(s)\|_{L^{2}_{l_{r}}}^{2}\mathrm{d}s\Big)^{\frac{1}{2}}

(5.18)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C(E_{k+1})\int_{0}^{t}\|\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta}\Theta(s)\|_{L^{2}_{l_{r}}}^{2}+\|\partial_{\zeta}\Theta(s)\|^{2}_{\mathbb{H}^{r-1}_{l,0}(\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+})}\mathrm{d}s\,.

Here
l_{r-1}=l_{r}+2
is required, which gives the definition of
l_{j}
in (
1.65
). Furthermore, the quantity
II_{9}
can easily bounded by

(5.19)

\displaystyle II_{9}\leq

\displaystyle C(E_{k+1})\int_{0}^{t}\|\partial_{\zeta}\Theta(s)\|^{2}_{\mathbb{H}^{r-1}_{l,0}(\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+})}\mathrm{d}s+C(E_{k+1})\int_{0}^{t}\|\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta}\Theta(s)\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{l_{r}}}\mathrm{d}s\,.

It thereby derived from substituting the estimates (
5.14
), (
5
), (
5.16
), (
5.17
), (
5
) and (
5.19
) into (
5.13
) that

(5.20)

\displaystyle\|\Theta(t)\|_{l,r,0}^{2}+\int_{0}^{t}\tilde{\kappa}_{0}\|\partial_{\zeta}\Theta(s)\|_{l,r,0}^{2}+\lambda\|\nabla_{\bar{x}}\Theta(s)\|_{l,r,0}^{2}+\tilde{\kappa}_{0}R^{0}_{\theta}\|\Theta|_{\zeta=0}(s)\|_{\Gamma,r}^{2}\mathrm{d}s

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\Big(\|\Theta(0)\|_{l,r,0}^{2}+\int_{0}^{t}\|\tilde{g}^{\sigma}(s)\|_{l,r,0}^{2}\mathrm{d}s\Big)+C(E_{k+1})\int_{0}^{t}\|\Theta(s)\|_{\mathbb{H}^{r}_{l,0}(\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+})}^{2}\mathrm{d}s

\displaystyle+C(E_{k+1})\int_{0}^{t}\|\partial_{\zeta}\Theta(s)\|_{\mathbb{H}^{r-1}_{l,0}(\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+})}^{2}+\|\Theta|_{\zeta=0}(s)\|_{\mathbb{H}^{r-1}(\mathbb{R}^{2})}^{2}\mathrm{d}s

for
1\leq r\leq k
(
k\geq 3
). Together with (
5.11
) and (
5.20
), we can apply the induction for
0\leq l\leq k(k\geq 3)
to imply

(5.21)

\displaystyle\|\Theta(t)\|_{\mathbb{H}^{r}_{l,0}(\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+})}^{2}+\int_{0}^{t}\|\partial_{\zeta}\Theta(s)\|_{\mathbb{H}^{r}_{l,0}(\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+})}^{2}+\lambda\|\nabla_{\bar{x}}\Theta(s)\|_{\mathbb{H}^{r}_{l,0}(\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+})}^{2}+\|\Theta|_{\zeta=0}(s)\|_{\mathbb{H}^{r}(\mathbb{R}^{2})}^{2}\mathrm{d}s

\displaystyle\leq C(E_{k+1})\Big(\|\Theta(0)\|_{\mathbb{H}^{r}_{l,0}(\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+})}^{2}+\int_{0}^{t}\|(\Theta,\tilde{g}^{\sigma})(s)\|_{\mathbb{H}^{r}_{l,0}(\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+})}^{2}\mathrm{d}s\Big)

for any
0\leq l\leq k(k\geq 3)
.

Step 3. The mixed
(t,\bar{x},\zeta)
-derivatives estimates:
\sum_{1\leq n\leq r}\mathbb{H}^{r}_{l,n}(\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+})
-bounds.
While estimating the higher order
(t,\bar{x},\zeta)
-derivatives, we shall subtly deal with boundary values of higher order
\zeta
-derivatives of
\Theta
. In order to overcome the issues, we first give the following lemma, which can be proved directly. Here we omit the details of proof for simplicity of presentation.

Lemma 5.2
.

Assume
\Theta(t,\bar{x},\zeta)
is a smooth solution to the linear parabolic equation on
\Theta
in (
5.8
) over
(t,\bar{x},\zeta)\in[0,\tau]\times\mathbb{R}^{2}\times[0,\tfrac{3}{\sigma}]
. Then the boundary values of higher oder
\zeta
-derivatives of
\Theta
satisfy that for
m\geq 1
:

(5.22)

\displaystyle\tilde{\kappa}\partial_{\zeta}^{2m+1}\Theta|_{\zeta=\frac{3}{\sigma}}=(\partial_{t}+\bar{\mathfrak{u}}^{0}\cdot\nabla_{\bar{x}}-\lambda\Delta_{\bar{x}}+\tfrac{2}{3}\mathrm{div}_{x}\mathfrak{u}^{0})\partial_{\zeta}^{2m-1}\Theta|_{\zeta=\frac{3}{\sigma}}\,,\ \tilde{\kappa}\partial_{\zeta}^{2m}\Theta|_{\zeta=\frac{3}{\sigma}}=0

and

(5.23)

\displaystyle\partial_{\zeta}^{m+1}\Theta|_{\zeta=0}=

\displaystyle(\mathscr{L}+\tilde{\mathscr{L}}_{m-1})\partial_{\zeta}^{m-1}\Theta|_{\zeta=0}+\hat{\mathscr{L}}\partial_{\zeta}^{m-1}\tilde{g}^{\sigma}|_{\zeta=0}\,,

where

\displaystyle\tilde{\mathscr{L}}_{m-1}f=\tilde{\kappa}^{-1}\big(\bar{\mathfrak{u}}^{0}\cdot\nabla_{\bar{x}}+\tfrac{2}{3}\mathrm{div}_{x}\mathfrak{u}^{0}+(m-1)\partial_{x_{3}}\mathfrak{u}^{0}_{3}\big)f\,,

(5.24)

\displaystyle\mathscr{L}f=\tilde{\kappa}^{-1}(\partial_{t}-\lambda\Delta_{\bar{x}})f\,,\quad\hat{\mathscr{L}}f=-\tilde{\kappa}^{-1}f\,.

Moreover, if
m
is odd, there holds

\displaystyle\partial_{\zeta}^{m+1}\Theta|_{\zeta=0}=\prod_{i=1}^{\frac{m+1}{2}}(\mathscr{L}+\tilde{\mathscr{L}}_{m+1-2i})\Theta|_{\zeta=0}+\sum_{i=0}^{\frac{m-1}{2}}\prod_{j=1}^{i}(\mathscr{L}+\tilde{\mathscr{L}}_{m+1-2j})\hat{\mathscr{L}}\partial_{\zeta}^{m-1-2i}\tilde{g}^{\sigma}|_{\zeta=0}

If
m
is even, there holds

\displaystyle\partial_{\zeta}^{m+1}\Theta|_{\zeta=0}=\prod_{i=1}^{\frac{m}{2}}(\mathscr{L}+\tilde{\mathscr{L}}_{m+1-2i})(R_{\theta}\Theta)|_{\zeta=0}+\sum_{i=0}^{\frac{m}{2}-1}\prod_{j=1}^{i}(\mathscr{L}+\tilde{\mathscr{L}}_{m+1-2j})\hat{\mathscr{L}}\partial_{\zeta}^{m-1-2i}\tilde{g}^{\sigma}|_{\zeta=0}\,.

One notices that

(5.25)

\displaystyle\tilde{\mathscr{L}}_{m}\thicksim\tilde{\mathscr{L}}:=\tilde{\kappa}^{-1}\big(\bar{\mathfrak{u}}^{0}\cdot\nabla_{\bar{x}}+\tfrac{2}{3}\mathrm{div}_{x}\mathfrak{u}^{0}+\partial_{x_{3}}\mathfrak{u}^{0}_{3}\big)

while carrying the operators
\tilde{\mathscr{L}}_{m}
in the following estimates. For simplicity of presentation, we will employ the relations on the boundary
\zeta=0

(5.26)

\displaystyle\partial_{\zeta}^{m+1}\Theta\thicksim\left\{\begin{aligned} &(\mathscr{L}+\tilde{\mathscr{L}})^{\frac{m+1}{2}}\Theta+\sum_{i=0}^{\frac{m-1}{2}}(\mathscr{L}+\tilde{\mathscr{L}})^{i}\hat{\mathscr{L}}\partial_{\zeta}^{m-1-2i}\tilde{g}^{\sigma}\ \textrm{for odd }m\,,\\
&(\mathscr{L}+\tilde{\mathscr{L}})^{\frac{m}{2}}(R_{\theta}\Theta)+\sum_{i=0}^{\frac{m}{2}-1}(\mathscr{L}+\tilde{\mathscr{L}})^{i}\hat{\mathscr{L}}\partial_{\zeta}^{m-1-2i}\tilde{g}^{\sigma}\ \textrm{for even }m\,,\end{aligned}\right.

in the rest of the paper. We then return to the proof of Proposition
2.2
.

Case 1. The first-order
\zeta
-derivatives:
\mathbb{H}^{r}_{l,1}(\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+})
-bounds.
Applying
\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta}\partial_{\zeta}
to the
\Theta
-equation of (
5.8
) with
2\gamma+|\beta|=r-1
,
1\leq r\leq k
(
k\geq 3
) yields

(5.27)

\displaystyle\partial_{t}\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta}\partial_{\zeta}\Theta+\bar{\mathfrak{u}}^{0}\cdot\nabla_{\bar{x}}\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta}\partial_{\zeta}\Theta+(\partial_{x_{3}}\mathfrak{u}^{0}_{3}\zeta+u^{0}_{1,3})\chi_{\sigma}(\zeta)\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta}\partial_{\zeta}^{2}\Theta

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta}(\tilde{\kappa}\partial_{\zeta}^{3}\Theta)+\lambda\Delta_{\bar{x}}\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta}\partial_{\zeta}\Theta-\zeta\chi_{\sigma}(\zeta)[\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta},\partial_{x_{3}}\mathfrak{u}^{0}_{3}\partial_{\zeta}^{2}]\Theta

\displaystyle-\partial_{x_{3}}\mathfrak{u}^{0}_{3}\partial_{\zeta}(\zeta\chi_{\sigma}(\zeta))\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta}\partial_{\zeta}\Theta-[\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta},\bar{\mathfrak{u}}^{0}\cdot\nabla_{\bar{x}}]\partial_{\zeta}\Theta-[\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta}\partial_{\zeta},u^{0}_{1,3}\chi_{\sigma}(\zeta)\partial_{\zeta}]\Theta

\displaystyle-\partial_{\zeta}(\zeta\chi_{\sigma}(\zeta))[\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta},\partial_{x_{3}}\mathfrak{u}^{0}_{3}\partial_{\zeta}]\Theta-\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta}\partial_{\zeta}(\tfrac{2}{3}\mathrm{div}_{x}\mathfrak{u}^{0}\Theta)+\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta}\partial_{\zeta}\tilde{g}^{\sigma}\,.

By Lemma
5.2
,

(5.28)

\tilde{\kappa}\partial_{\zeta}^{2}\Theta|_{\zeta=0}=\mathscr{L}_{\Theta}(\Theta,\tilde{g}^{\sigma})|_{\zeta=0}\,,\quad\Theta|_{\zeta=\frac{3}{\sigma}}=\partial_{\zeta}^{2}\Theta|_{\zeta=\frac{3}{\sigma}}=0\,,

where
\mathscr{L}_{\Theta}(\Theta,\tilde{g}^{\sigma})=\big(\partial_{t}\Theta-\lambda\Delta_{\bar{x}}\Theta+\bar{\mathfrak{u}}^{0}\cdot\nabla_{\bar{x}}\Theta+\tfrac{2}{3}\mathrm{div}_{x}\mathfrak{u}^{0}\Theta-\tilde{g}^{\sigma}\big)
. Multiplying (

) with

, and integrating over

, one has

\displaystyle\tfrac{1}{2}\|\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta}\partial_{\zeta}\Theta(t)\|_{L^{2}_{l_{r}}}^{2}-\tfrac{1}{2}\|\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta}\partial_{\zeta}\Theta(0)\|_{L^{2}_{l_{r}}}^{2}=\sum_{k=0}^{11}III_{k}\,,

where

\displaystyle III_{1}=

\displaystyle-\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\int_{0}^{\frac{3}{\sigma}}\bar{\mathfrak{u}}^{0}\cdot\nabla_{\bar{x}}\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta}\partial_{\zeta}\Theta\cdot(1+\zeta)^{l_{r}}\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta}\partial_{\zeta}\Theta\mathrm{d}\bar{x}\mathrm{d}\zeta\mathrm{d}s\,,

\displaystyle III_{2}=

\displaystyle-\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\int_{0}^{\frac{3}{\sigma}}(\partial_{x_{3}}\mathfrak{u}^{0}_{3}\zeta+u^{0}_{1,3})\chi_{\sigma}(\zeta)\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta}\partial_{\zeta}^{2}\Theta(1+\zeta)^{l_{r}}\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta}\partial_{\zeta}^{2}\Theta\mathrm{d}\bar{x}\mathrm{d}\zeta\mathrm{d}s\,,

\displaystyle III_{3}=

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\int_{0}^{\frac{3}{\sigma}}\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta}(\tilde{\kappa}\partial_{\zeta}^{3}\Theta)\cdot(1+\zeta)^{l_{r}}\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta}\partial_{\zeta}\Theta\mathrm{d}\bar{x}\mathrm{d}\zeta\mathrm{d}s\,,

\displaystyle III_{4}=

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\int_{0}^{\frac{3}{\sigma}}\lambda\Delta_{\bar{x}}\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta}\partial_{\zeta}\Theta\cdot(1+\zeta)^{l_{r}}\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta}\partial_{\zeta}\Theta\mathrm{d}\bar{x}\mathrm{d}\zeta\mathrm{d}s\,,

\displaystyle III_{5}=

\displaystyle-\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\int_{0}^{\frac{3}{\sigma}}\zeta\chi_{\sigma}(\zeta)[\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta},\partial_{x_{3}}\mathfrak{u}^{0}_{3}\partial_{\zeta}^{2}]\Theta\cdot(1+\zeta)^{l_{r}}\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta}\partial_{\zeta}\Theta\mathrm{d}\bar{x}\mathrm{d}\zeta\mathrm{d}s\,,

\displaystyle III_{6}=

\displaystyle-\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\int_{0}^{\frac{3}{\sigma}}\partial_{x_{3}}\mathfrak{u}^{0}_{3}\partial_{\zeta}(\zeta\chi_{\sigma}(\zeta))\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta}\partial_{\zeta}\Theta\cdot(1+\zeta)^{l_{r}}\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta}\partial_{\zeta}\Theta\mathrm{d}\bar{x}\mathrm{d}\zeta\mathrm{d}s\,,

\displaystyle III_{7}=

\displaystyle-\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\int_{0}^{\frac{3}{\sigma}}[\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta},\bar{\mathfrak{u}}^{0}\cdot\nabla_{\bar{x}}]\partial_{\zeta}\Theta\cdot(1+\zeta)^{l_{r}}\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta}\partial_{\zeta}\Theta\mathrm{d}\bar{x}\mathrm{d}\zeta\mathrm{d}s\,,

\displaystyle III_{8}=

\displaystyle-\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\int_{0}^{\frac{3}{\sigma}}\partial_{\zeta}(\zeta\chi_{\sigma}(\zeta))[\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta},\partial_{x_{3}}\mathfrak{u}^{0}_{3}\partial_{\zeta}]\Theta\cdot(1+\zeta)^{l_{r}}\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta}\partial_{\zeta}\Theta\mathrm{d}\bar{x}\mathrm{d}\zeta\mathrm{d}s\,,

\displaystyle III_{9}=

\displaystyle-\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\int_{0}^{\frac{3}{\sigma}}\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta}\partial_{\zeta}\big(\tfrac{2}{3}\mathrm{div}_{x}\mathfrak{u}^{0}\Theta\big)\cdot(1+\zeta)^{l_{r}}\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta}\partial_{\zeta}\Theta\mathrm{d}\bar{x}\mathrm{d}\zeta\mathrm{d}s\,,

\displaystyle III_{10}=

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\int_{0}^{\frac{3}{\sigma}}\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta}\partial_{\zeta}\tilde{g}^{\sigma}\cdot(1+\zeta)^{l_{r}}\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta}\partial_{\zeta}\Theta\mathrm{d}\bar{x}\mathrm{d}\zeta\mathrm{d}s\,,

\displaystyle III_{11}=

\displaystyle-\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\int_{0}^{\frac{3}{\sigma}}[\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta}\partial_{\zeta},u^{0}_{1,3}\chi_{\sigma}(\zeta)\partial_{\zeta}]\Theta\cdot(1+\zeta)^{l_{r}}\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta}\partial_{\zeta}\Theta\mathrm{d}\bar{x}\mathrm{d}\zeta\mathrm{d}s\,.

Integration by parts over
\bar{x}\in\mathbb{R}^{2}
,
\zeta\in[0,\frac{3}{\sigma}]
and Sobolev theory yield

(5.29)

\displaystyle|III_{1}|\leq

\displaystyle C(E_{k+1})\int_{0}^{t}\|\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta}\partial_{\zeta}\Theta(s)\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{l_{r}}}\mathrm{d}s\,,

(5.30)

\displaystyle III_{2}\leq

\displaystyle\tfrac{1}{4}\int_{0}^{t}\tilde{\kappa}_{0}\|\partial_{\zeta}\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta}\partial_{\zeta}\Theta(s)\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{l_{r}}}\mathrm{d}s+C(E_{k+1})\int_{0}^{t}\|\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta}\partial_{\zeta}\Theta(s)\|_{L^{2}_{l_{r}}}^{2}\mathrm{d}s\,,

where the properties of
\chi_{\sigma}(\zeta)
in (
5.9
) and the similar arguments of derivation

in (
5
) have been used. For the term

, it is decomposed as

\displaystyle III_{3}=

\displaystyle-\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\int_{0}^{\frac{3}{\sigma}}\tilde{\kappa}(1+\zeta)^{l_{r}}(\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta}\partial_{\zeta}^{2}\Theta)^{2}+\tilde{\kappa}l_{r}(1+\zeta)^{l_{r}-1}\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta}\partial_{\zeta}^{2}\Theta\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta}\partial_{\zeta}\Theta\mathrm{d}\bar{x}\mathrm{d}\zeta\mathrm{d}s

\displaystyle-\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\int_{0}^{\frac{3}{\sigma}}[\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta},\tilde{\kappa}\partial_{\zeta}^{2}]\Theta\cdot[(1+\zeta)^{l_{r}}+l_{r}(1+\zeta)^{l_{r}-1}]\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta}\partial_{\zeta}\Theta

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}(1+\zeta)^{l_{r}}\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta}(\tilde{\kappa}\partial_{\zeta}^{2}\Theta)\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta}\partial_{\zeta}\Theta|_{\zeta=0}^{\zeta=\frac{3}{\sigma}}\mathrm{d}\bar{x}\mathrm{d}s\,.

By the lower bounds (
5.7
), Hölder and Young inequalities, the first term in the right-hand side of the decomposition

can be bounded by

\displaystyle-\tfrac{3}{4}\int_{0}^{t}\tilde{\kappa}_{0}\|\partial_{\zeta}^{2}\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta}\Theta(s)\|_{L^{2}_{l_{r}}}^{2}\mathrm{d}s+C(E_{k+1})\int_{0}^{t}\|\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta}\partial_{\zeta}\Theta(s)\|_{L^{2}_{l_{r}}}^{2}\mathrm{d}s\,,

and the second term can be bounded by

. We further denote the third term by

. There therefore holds

(5.31)

\displaystyle III_{3}\leq

\displaystyle III_{3B}-\tfrac{3}{4}\int_{0}^{t}\tilde{\kappa}_{0}\|\partial_{\zeta}^{2}\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta}\Theta(s)\|_{L^{2}_{l_{r}}}^{2}\mathrm{d}s+C(E_{k+1})\int_{0}^{t}\|\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta}\partial_{\zeta}\Theta(s)\|_{L^{2}_{l_{r}}}^{2}\mathrm{d}s

\displaystyle+C(E_{k+1})\int_{0}^{t}\|\partial_{\zeta}\Theta(s)\|_{\mathbb{H}^{r-1}_{l,1}(\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+})}^{2}\mathrm{d}s\,.

Due to the boundary values of (
5.8
) and (

), the quantity

reads

\displaystyle III_{3B}=

\displaystyle\underbrace{-\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta}(\partial_{t}-\lambda\Delta_{\bar{x}})\Theta\cdot\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta}(R_{\theta}\Theta)|_{\zeta=0}\mathrm{d}\bar{x}\mathrm{d}s}_{III_{3B}^{1}}

\displaystyle\underbrace{-\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta}(\bar{\mathfrak{u}}^{0}\cdot\nabla_{\bar{x}}\Theta)\cdot\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta}(R_{\theta}\Theta)|_{\zeta=0}\mathrm{d}\bar{x}\mathrm{d}s}_{III_{3B}^{2}}

(5.32)

\displaystyle\underbrace{-\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta}\big(\tfrac{2}{3}\mathrm{div}_{x}\mathfrak{u}^{0}\Theta\big)\cdot\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta}(R_{\theta}\Theta)|_{\zeta=0}\mathrm{d}\bar{x}\mathrm{d}s}_{III_{3B}^{3}}

\displaystyle+\underbrace{\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta}\tilde{g}^{\sigma}\cdot\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta}(R_{\theta}\Theta)|_{\zeta=0}\mathrm{d}\bar{x}\mathrm{d}s}_{III_{3B}^{4}}\,.

We first deal with the quantity

. We deduce from integration by parts over

that

\displaystyle III_{3B}^{1}

\displaystyle=-\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\!\tfrac{1}{2}R_{\theta}\big(\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta}\Theta\big)^{2}|_{\zeta=0}|_{s=0}^{s=t}\mathrm{d}\bar{x}-\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\lambda R_{\theta}|\nabla_{\bar{x}}\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta}\Theta|^{2}|_{\zeta=0}\mathrm{d}\bar{x}\mathrm{d}s

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{t}\!\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\!\tfrac{1}{2}(\partial_{t}+\lambda\Delta_{\bar{x}})R_{\theta}(\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta}\Theta)^{2}|_{\zeta=0}\mathrm{d}\bar{x}\mathrm{d}s

\displaystyle\underbrace{-\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta}\partial_{t}\Theta\cdot[\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta},R_{\theta}]\Theta|_{\zeta=0}\mathrm{d}\bar{x}\mathrm{d}s}_{III_{3B}^{11}}+\underbrace{\lambda\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta}\Delta_{\bar{x}}\Theta\cdot[\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta},R_{\theta}]\Theta|_{\zeta=0}\mathrm{d}\bar{x}\mathrm{d}s}_{III_{3B}^{12}}

\displaystyle\leq-\tfrac{R^{0}_{\theta}}{2}\|\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta}\Theta|_{\zeta=0}(t)\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{2})}^{2}-\int_{0}^{t}\lambda R^{0}_{\theta}\|\nabla_{\bar{x}}\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta}\Theta|_{\zeta=0}(s)\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{2})}^{2}\mathrm{d}s

(5.33)

\displaystyle+C(E_{k+1})\Big(\|\Theta(0)\|_{\mathbb{H}^{r}_{l}(\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+})}^{2}+\int_{0}^{t}\|\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta}\Theta|_{\zeta=0}(s)\|^{2}_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{2})}\mathrm{d}s\Big)+III_{3B}^{11}+III_{3B}^{12}\,,

where the lower bounds (
2.9
) and the inequality

|-\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\tfrac{1}{2}R_{\theta}(\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta}\Theta)^{2}(0)|_{\zeta=0}\mathrm{d}\bar{x}|\leq C(E_{k+1})\|\Theta(0)\|_{\mathbb{H}^{r}_{l}(\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+})}^{2}

implied by (
5.1
) in Lemma
5.1
have been used in the last inequality. For the term
III_{3B}^{11}
, if
\beta=0,2\gamma=r-1
,

\displaystyle III_{3B}^{11}=

\displaystyle-\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\Theta\cdot[\partial_{t}^{\gamma},R_{\theta}]\Theta|_{\zeta=0}|_{s=0}^{s=t}\mathrm{d}\bar{x}-\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\Theta\cdot\partial_{t}[\partial_{t}^{\gamma},R_{\theta}]\Theta|_{\zeta=0}\mathrm{d}\bar{x}\mathrm{d}s

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\tfrac{R^{0}_{\theta}}{4}\|\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta}\Theta|_{\zeta=0}(t)\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{2})}^{2}+C(E_{k+1})\|\Theta|_{\zeta=0}(t)\|^{2}_{\mathbb{H}^{r-3}(\mathbb{R}^{2})}

\displaystyle+C(E_{k+1})\Big(\|\Theta(0)\|^{2}_{\mathbb{H}^{r}_{l}(\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+})}+\int_{0}^{t}\|\Theta(s)\|^{2}_{\mathbb{H}^{r}_{l,0}(\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+})}+\|\Theta(s)\|^{2}_{\mathbb{H}^{r}_{l,1}(\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+})}\mathrm{d}s\Big)\,,

where the lower bounds (
2.9
) and trace inequality (
5.1
) in Lemma
5.1
are used.

If
|\beta|\geq 2
, without loss of generality, we assume
\beta\geq 2e_{1}=2(1,0)
, i.e.,
\beta_{1}\geq 2
. Then, (
5.2
) in Lemma

implies

\displaystyle III_{3B}^{11}=

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\partial_{x_{1}}\big(\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta-2e_{1}}\partial_{t}\Theta\big)\cdot\partial_{x_{1}}\big\{[\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta},R_{\theta}]\Theta\big\}|_{\zeta=0}\mathrm{d}\bar{x}\mathrm{d}s

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C(E_{k+1})\int_{0}^{t}\|\Theta(s)\|^{2}_{\mathbb{H}^{r}_{l,0}(\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+})}+\|\Theta(s)\|^{2}_{\mathbb{H}^{r}_{l,1}(\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+})}\mathrm{d}s\,.

If
|\beta|=1,2\gamma=r-2
, where

is required to be even. Then,

\displaystyle III_{3B}^{11}=

\displaystyle-\sum_{i=1}^{2}\delta_{r}(\beta=e_{i})\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{e_{i}}\partial_{t}\Theta\partial_{\bar{x}}^{e_{i}}R_{\theta}\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\Theta|_{\zeta=0}\mathrm{d}\bar{x}\mathrm{d}s

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta}\Theta\cdot\partial_{t}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta}[\partial_{t}^{\gamma},R_{\theta}]\Theta|_{\zeta=0}\mathrm{d}\bar{x}\mathrm{d}s-\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta}\Theta\cdot\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta}[\partial_{t}^{\gamma},R_{\theta}]\Theta|_{\zeta=0}|_{s=0}^{s=t}\mathrm{d}\bar{x}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{2}\delta_{r}(\beta=e_{i})\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{t}\Theta\partial_{\bar{x}}^{e_{i}}(\partial_{\bar{x}}^{e_{i}}R_{\theta}\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\Theta)|_{\zeta=0}\mathrm{d}\bar{x}\mathrm{d}s

\displaystyle+C(E_{k+1})\sum_{j=0}^{r-1}\sum_{2\tilde{\gamma}+|\tilde{\beta}|=j}\int_{0}^{t}\|\partial_{t}^{\tilde{\gamma}}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\tilde{\beta}}\Theta|_{\zeta=0}(s)\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{2})}^{2}\mathrm{d}s

\displaystyle+\tfrac{R^{0}_{\theta}}{8}\|\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta}\Theta|_{\zeta=0}(t)\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{2})}^{2}+C(E_{k+1})\sum_{j=0}^{r-3}\sum_{2\tilde{\gamma}+|\tilde{\beta}|=j}\|\partial_{t}^{\tilde{\gamma}}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\tilde{\beta}}\Theta|_{\zeta=0}(t)\|_{L^{(}\mathbb{R}^{2})}^{2}

\displaystyle+C(E_{k+1})\sum_{j=0}^{r-1}\sum_{2\tilde{\gamma}+|\tilde{\beta}|=j}\|\partial_{t}^{\tilde{\gamma}}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\tilde{\beta}}\Theta|_{\zeta=0}(0)\|_{L^{(}\mathbb{R}^{2})}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\tfrac{R^{0}_{\theta}}{8}\|\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta}\Theta|_{\zeta=0}(t)\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{2})}^{2}+C(E_{k+1})\|\Theta|_{\zeta=0}(t)\|^{2}_{\mathbb{H}^{r-3}(\mathbb{R}^{2})}

\displaystyle+\epsilon_{0}\sum_{i=1}^{2}\delta_{r}(\beta=e_{i})\int_{0}^{t}\|\partial_{t}^{\gamma+1}\Theta|_{\zeta=0}(s)\|^{2}_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{2})}\mathrm{d}s

\displaystyle+C(E_{k+1})\Big(\|\Theta(0)\|^{2}_{\mathbb{H}^{r}_{l}(\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+})}+\int_{0}^{t}\|\Theta(s)\|^{2}_{\mathbb{H}^{r}_{l,0}(\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+})}+\|\Theta(s)\|^{2}_{\mathbb{H}^{r}_{l,1}(\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+})}\mathrm{d}s\Big)

for some small

to be determined, where (

\delta_{r}(\beta=e_{i})=\left\{\begin{array}[]{l}1\,,\qquad r\,{\text{is\ even\ and}}\,\beta=e_{i}\,,\\
