Consequently,

\displaystyle III_{3B}^{11}\leq

\displaystyle\tfrac{3R^{0}_{\theta}}{8}\|\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta}\Theta|_{\zeta=0}(t)\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{2})}^{2}+C(E_{k+1})\|\Theta|_{\zeta=0}(t)\|^{2}_{\mathbb{H}^{r-3}(\mathbb{R}^{2})}

(5.35)

\displaystyle+\epsilon_{0}\sum_{i=1}^{2}\delta_{r}(\beta=e_{i})\int_{0}^{t}\|\partial_{t}^{\gamma+1}\Theta|_{\zeta=0}(s)\|^{2}_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{2})}\mathrm{d}s

\displaystyle+C(E_{k+1})\Big(\|\Theta(0)\|^{2}_{\mathbb{H}^{r}_{l}(\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+})}+\int_{0}^{t}\|\Theta(s)\|^{2}_{\mathbb{H}^{r}_{l,0}(\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+})}+\|\Theta(s)\|^{2}_{\mathbb{H}^{r}_{l,1}(\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+})}\mathrm{d}s\Big)\,.

For

, as similarly in (

), one has

\displaystyle=-\lambda\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\nabla_{\bar{x}}\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta}\Theta\cdot\nabla_{\bar{x}}[\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta},R_{\theta}]\Theta|_{\zeta=0}\mathrm{d}\bar{x}\mathrm{d}s

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\tfrac{1}{2}\lambda R^{0}_{\theta}\int_{0}^{t}\|\nabla_{\bar{x}}\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta}\Theta|_{\zeta=0}(s)\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{2})}^{2}\mathrm{d}s+C(E_{k+1})\int_{0}^{t}\|\Theta(s)\|^{2}_{\mathbb{H}^{r}_{l,0}(\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+})}+\|\Theta(s)\|^{2}_{\mathbb{H}^{r}_{l,1}(\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+})}\mathrm{d}s\,.

Therefore, from plugging (

) and (

) into (

), one has

\displaystyle III_{3B}^{1}\leq

\displaystyle-\tfrac{R^{0}_{\theta}}{8}\|\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta}\Theta|_{\zeta=0}(t)\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{2})}^{2}-\tfrac{1}{2}\lambda\int_{0}^{t}R^{0}_{\theta}\|\nabla_{\bar{x}}\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta}\Theta|_{\zeta=0}(s)\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{2})}^{2}\mathrm{d}s

\displaystyle+\epsilon_{0}\sum_{i=1}^{2}\delta_{r}(\beta=e_{i})\int_{0}^{t}\|\partial_{t}^{\gamma+1}\Theta|_{\zeta=0}(s)\|^{2}_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{2})}\mathrm{d}s+C(E_{k+1})\|\Theta|_{\zeta=0}(t)\|^{2}_{\mathbb{H}^{r-3}(\mathbb{R}^{2})}

(5.37)

\displaystyle+C(E_{k+1})\Big(\|\Theta(0)\|^{2}_{\mathbb{H}^{r}_{l}(\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+})}+\int_{0}^{t}\|\Theta(s)\|^{2}_{\mathbb{H}^{r}_{l,0}(\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+})}+\|\Theta(s)\|^{2}_{\mathbb{H}^{r}_{l,1}(\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+})}\mathrm{d}s\Big)\,.

For the term

, there holds

\displaystyle III_{3B}^{2}=

\displaystyle-\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}[\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta},\bar{\mathfrak{u}}^{0}\cdot\nabla_{\bar{x}}]\Theta\cdot\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta}(R_{\theta}\Theta)|_{\zeta=0}\mathrm{d}\bar{x}\mathrm{d}s

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}

\displaystyle\partial_{t}^{\tilde{\gamma}}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\tilde{\beta}}\Theta\mathrm{div}_{\bar{x}}\big\{\bar{\mathfrak{u}}^{0}[\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta},R_{\theta}]\Theta\big\}|_{\zeta=0}\mathrm{d}\bar{x}\mathrm{d}s+\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\mathrm{div}_{\bar{x}}(R_{\theta}\bar{\mathfrak{u}}^{0})\tfrac{1}{2}\big(\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta}\Theta\big)^{2}|_{\zeta=0}\mathrm{d}\bar{x}\mathrm{d}s

(5.38)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C(E_{k+1})\int_{0}^{t}\|\Theta(s)\|^{2}_{\mathbb{H}^{r}_{l,0}(\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+})}+\|\Theta(s)\|^{2}_{\mathbb{H}^{r}_{l,1}(\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+})}\mathrm{d}s\,.

and

It is thereby derived from substituting the bounds (

)-(

), and combining with (

\displaystyle III_{3}\leq

\displaystyle-\tfrac{3}{4}\int_{0}^{t}\tilde{\kappa}_{0}\|\partial_{\zeta}\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta}\partial_{\zeta}\Theta(s)\|_{L^{2}_{l_{r}}}^{2}\mathrm{d}s+C(E_{k+1})\int_{0}^{t}\|\partial_{\zeta}\Theta(s)\|_{\mathbb{H}^{r-1}_{l,1}(\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+})}^{2}\mathrm{d}s

\displaystyle-\tfrac{R^{0}_{\theta}}{8}\|\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta}\Theta|_{\zeta=0}(t)\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{2})}^{2}-\tfrac{1}{2}\lambda R^{0}_{\theta}\int_{0}^{t}\|\nabla_{\bar{x}}\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta}\Theta|_{\zeta=0}(s)\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{2})}^{2}\mathrm{d}s

\displaystyle+\epsilon_{0}\sum_{i=1}^{2}\delta_{r}(\beta=e_{i})\int_{0}^{t}\|\partial_{t}^{\gamma+1}\Theta|_{\zeta=0}(s)\|^{2}_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{2})}\mathrm{d}s+C(E_{k+1})\|\Theta|_{\zeta=0}(t)\|^{2}_{\mathbb{H}^{r-3}(\mathbb{R}^{2})}

(5.41)

\displaystyle+C(E_{k+1})\Big(\|\Theta(0)\|^{2}_{\mathbb{H}^{r}_{l}(\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+})}+\int_{0}^{t}\|(\Theta,\tilde{g}^{\sigma})(s)\|_{\mathbb{H}^{r}_{l,0}(\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+})}^{2}+\|(\Theta,\tilde{g}^{\sigma})(s)\|_{\mathbb{H}^{r}_{l,1}(\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+})}^{2}\mathrm{d}s\Big)\,.

Direct calculation gives us

(5.42)

\displaystyle III_{4}=-\lambda\int_{0}^{t}\|\nabla_{\bar{x}}\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta}\partial_{\zeta}\Theta(s)\|_{L^{2}_{l_{r}}}^{2}\mathrm{d}s\,.

For
III_{5}
and

, one has

\displaystyle III_{5}\leq

\displaystyle C(E_{k+1})\sum_{j=0}^{r-2}\sum_{2\tilde{\gamma}+|\tilde{\beta}|=j}\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\int_{0}^{\frac{3}{\sigma}}(1+\zeta)^{l_{r}+1}|\partial_{t}^{\tilde{\gamma}}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\tilde{\beta}}\partial_{\zeta}^{2}\Theta||\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta}\partial_{\zeta}\Theta|\mathrm{d}\bar{x}\mathrm{d}\zeta\mathrm{d}s

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C(E_{k+1})\sum_{j=0}^{r-2}\sum_{2\tilde{\gamma}+|\tilde{\beta}|=j}\Big(\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\int_{0}^{\frac{3}{\sigma}}(1+\zeta)^{l_{r}+2}|\partial_{\zeta}\partial_{t}^{\tilde{\gamma}}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\tilde{\beta}}\partial_{\zeta}\Theta|^{2}\mathrm{d}\bar{x}\mathrm{d}\zeta\mathrm{d}s\Big)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\times\Big(\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\int_{0}^{\frac{3}{\sigma}}(1+\zeta)^{l_{r}}|\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta}\partial_{\zeta}\Theta|^{2}\mathrm{d}\bar{x}\mathrm{d}\zeta\mathrm{d}s\Big)^{\frac{1}{2}}

and

(5.44)

\displaystyle III_{6}\leq

\displaystyle C(E_{k+1})\int_{0}^{t}\|\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta}\partial_{\zeta}\Theta(s)\|_{L^{2}_{l_{r}}}^{2}\mathrm{d}s\,.

Similarly, one also has

In summary, it is derived from (

)-(

)-(

) and summing up for

that

\displaystyle\|\Theta(s)\|_{l,r,1}^{2}+\int_{0}^{t}\tilde{\kappa}_{0}\|\partial_{\zeta}\Theta(s)\|_{l,r,1}^{2}+\lambda\|\nabla_{\bar{x}}\Theta(s)\|_{l,r,1}^{2}\mathrm{d}s

\displaystyle+\tfrac{R^{0}_{\theta}}{4}\|\Theta|_{\zeta=0}(t)\|_{\Gamma,r-1}^{2}+\lambda R^{0}_{\theta}\int_{0}^{t}\|\nabla_{\bar{x}}\Theta|_{\zeta=0}(s)\|_{\Gamma,r-1}^{2}\mathrm{d}s

\displaystyle\leq C(E_{k+1})\int_{0}^{t}\|\partial_{\zeta}\Theta(s)\|_{\mathbb{H}^{r-1}_{l,1}(\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+})}^{2}\mathrm{d}s+C(E_{k+1})\|\Theta|_{\zeta=0}(t)\|^{2}_{\mathbb{H}^{r-3}(\mathbb{R}^{2})}

(5.47)

\displaystyle+\epsilon_{0}\sum_{2\gamma+|\beta|=r-1}\sum_{i=1}^{2}\delta_{r}(\beta=e_{i})\int_{0}^{t}\|\partial_{t}^{\gamma+1}\Theta|_{\zeta=0}(s)\|^{2}_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{2})}\mathrm{d}s

\displaystyle+C(E_{k+1})\Big(\|\Theta(0)\|^{2}_{\mathbb{H}^{r}_{l}(\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+})}+\int_{0}^{t}\|(\Theta,\tilde{g}^{\sigma})(s)\|_{\mathbb{H}^{r}_{l,0}(\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+})}^{2}+\|(\Theta,\tilde{g}^{\sigma})(s)\|_{\mathbb{H}^{r}_{l,1}(\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+})}^{2}\mathrm{d}s\Big)

for all

and some undetermined small constant
\epsilon_{0}>0
. Together with (

) and (

), the induction for

implies that

\displaystyle\|\Theta(s)\|_{\mathbb{H}^{r}_{l,1}(\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+})}^{2}+\int_{0}^{t}\|\partial_{\zeta}\Theta(s)\|_{\mathbb{H}^{r}_{l,1}(\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+})}^{2}+\lambda\|\nabla_{\bar{x}}\Theta(s)\|_{\mathbb{H}^{r}_{l,1}(\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+})}^{2}\mathrm{d}s

\displaystyle+\|\Theta|_{\zeta=0}(t)\|_{\mathbb{H}^{r-1}(\mathbb{R}^{2})}^{2}+\lambda\int_{0}^{t}\|\nabla_{\bar{x}}\Theta|_{\zeta=0}(s)\|_{\mathbb{H}^{r-1}(\mathbb{R}^{2})}^{2}\mathrm{d}s

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C(E_{k+1})\epsilon_{0}\underbrace{\sum_{j=0}^{r}\sum_{2\gamma+|\beta|=j-1}\sum_{i=1}^{2}\delta_{j}(\beta=e_{i})\int_{0}^{t}\|\partial_{t}^{\gamma+1}\Theta|_{\zeta=0}(s)\|^{2}_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{2})}\mathrm{d}s}_{=:\mathscr{A}_{B,1}^{r}(\Theta)(t)}

(5.48)

\displaystyle+C(E_{k+1})\Big(\|\Theta(0)\|^{2}_{\mathbb{H}^{r}_{l}(\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+})}+\int_{0}^{t}\|(\Theta,\tilde{g}^{\sigma})(s)\|_{\mathbb{H}^{r}_{l,0}(\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+})}^{2}+\|(\Theta,\tilde{g}^{\sigma})(s)\|_{\mathbb{H}^{r}_{l,1}(\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+})}^{2}\mathrm{d}s\Big)

for some small
\epsilon_{0}>0
to determined.

Case 2.
n
-order
\zeta
-derivatives:
\mathbb{H}^{r}_{l,n}(\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+})
-bounds (
2\leq n\leq r
).
For
2\leq n\leq r
with
2\gamma+|\beta|=r-n
, applying
\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta}\partial_{\zeta}^{n}
to the
\Theta
-equation of (
5.8
) infers that

(5.49)

\displaystyle\partial_{t}\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta}\partial_{\zeta}^{n}\Theta+\bar{\mathfrak{u}}^{0}\cdot\nabla_{\bar{x}}\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta}\partial_{\zeta}^{n}\Theta+[\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta},\bar{\mathfrak{u}}^{0}\cdot\nabla_{\bar{x}}]\partial_{\zeta}^{n}\Theta+\partial_{x_{3}}\mathfrak{u}^{0}_{3}[\partial_{\zeta}^{n},\zeta\chi_{\sigma}(\zeta)]\partial_{\zeta}\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta}\Theta

\displaystyle+(\partial_{x_{3}}\mathfrak{u}^{0}_{3}\zeta+u^{0}_{1,3})\chi_{\sigma}(\zeta)\partial_{\zeta}\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta}\partial_{\zeta}^{n}\Theta+\partial_{\zeta}^{n}\big\{\zeta\chi_{\sigma}(\zeta)[\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta},\partial_{x_{3}}\mathfrak{u}^{0}_{3}]\partial_{\zeta}\Theta\big\}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta}(\tilde{\kappa}\partial_{\zeta}^{n+2}\Theta)+\lambda\Delta_{\bar{x}}\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta}\partial_{\zeta}^{n}\Theta+\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta}\partial_{\zeta}^{n}\tilde{g}^{\sigma}

\displaystyle-\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta}\partial_{\zeta}^{n}(\tfrac{2}{3}\mathrm{div}_{x}\mathfrak{u}^{0}\Theta)-[\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta}\partial_{\zeta}^{n},u^{0}_{1,3}\chi_{\sigma}(\zeta)\partial_{\zeta}]\Theta\,.

From taking inner product with
(1+\gamma)^{l_{r}}\partial_{\gamma}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta}\partial_{\zeta}^{n}\Theta
over
[0,t]\times\mathbb{R}^{2}\times[0,\tfrac{3}{\sigma}]
in (
5.49
), one obtains

\displaystyle\tfrac{1}{2}\|\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta}\partial_{\zeta}^{n}\Theta(t)\|_{L^{2}_{l_{r}}}^{2}-\tfrac{1}{2}\|\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta}\partial_{\zeta}^{n}\Theta(0)\|_{L^{2}_{l_{r}}}^{2}=\sum_{k=1}^{10}IV_{k}\,,

where

\displaystyle IV_{1}=

\displaystyle-\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\int_{0}^{\frac{3}{\sigma}}\bar{\mathfrak{u}}^{0}\cdot\nabla_{\bar{x}}\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta}\partial_{\zeta}^{n}\Theta\cdot(1+\zeta)^{l_{r}}\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta}\partial_{\zeta}^{n}\Theta\mathrm{d}\bar{x}\mathrm{d}\zeta\mathrm{d}s\,,

\displaystyle IV_{2}=

\displaystyle-\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\int_{0}^{\frac{3}{\sigma}}[\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta},\bar{\mathfrak{u}}^{0}\cdot\nabla_{\bar{x}}]\partial_{\zeta}^{n}\Theta\cdot(1+\zeta)^{l_{r}}\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta}\partial_{\zeta}^{n}\Theta\mathrm{d}\bar{x}\mathrm{d}\zeta\mathrm{d}s\,,

\displaystyle IV_{3}=

\displaystyle-\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\int_{0}^{\frac{3}{\sigma}}(\partial_{x_{3}}\mathfrak{u}^{0}_{3}\zeta+u^{0}_{1,3})\chi_{\sigma}(\zeta)\partial_{\zeta}\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta}\partial_{\zeta}^{n}\Theta\cdot(1+\zeta)^{l_{r}}\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta}\partial_{\zeta}^{n}\Theta\mathrm{d}\bar{x}\mathrm{d}\zeta\mathrm{d}s\,,

\displaystyle IV_{4}=

\displaystyle-\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\int_{0}^{\frac{3}{\sigma}}\partial_{x_{3}}\mathfrak{u}^{0}_{3}[\partial_{\zeta}^{n},\zeta\chi_{\sigma}(\zeta)]\partial_{\zeta}\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta}\Theta\cdot(1+\zeta)^{l_{r}}\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta}\partial_{\zeta}^{n}\Theta\mathrm{d}\bar{x}\mathrm{d}\zeta\mathrm{d}s\,,

\displaystyle IV_{5}=

\displaystyle-\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\int_{0}^{\frac{3}{\sigma}}\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta}\partial_{\zeta}^{n}(\tfrac{2}{3}\mathrm{div}_{x}\mathfrak{u}^{0}\Theta)\cdot(1+\zeta)^{l_{r}}\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta}\partial_{\zeta}^{n}\Theta\mathrm{d}\bar{x}\mathrm{d}\zeta\mathrm{d}s\,,

\displaystyle IV_{6}=

\displaystyle-\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\int_{0}^{\frac{3}{\sigma}}\partial_{\zeta}^{n}\big\{\zeta\chi_{\sigma}(\zeta)[\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta},\partial_{x_{3}}\mathfrak{u}^{0}_{3}]\partial_{\zeta}\Theta\big\}\cdot(1+\zeta)^{l_{r}}\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta}\partial_{\zeta}^{n}\Theta\mathrm{d}\bar{x}\mathrm{d}\zeta\mathrm{d}s\,,

\displaystyle IV_{7}=

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\int_{0}^{\frac{3}{\sigma}}\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta}(\tilde{\kappa}\partial_{\zeta}^{n+2}\Theta)\cdot(1+\zeta)^{l_{r}}\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta}\partial_{\zeta}^{n}\Theta\mathrm{d}\bar{x}\mathrm{d}\zeta\mathrm{d}s\,,

\displaystyle IV_{8}=

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\int_{0}^{\frac{3}{\sigma}}\lambda\Delta_{\bar{x}}\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta}\partial_{\zeta}^{n}\Theta\cdot(1+\zeta)^{l_{r}}\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta}\partial_{\zeta}^{n}\Theta\mathrm{d}\bar{x}\mathrm{d}\zeta\mathrm{d}s\,,

\displaystyle IV_{9}=

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\int_{0}^{\frac{3}{\sigma}}\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta}\partial_{\zeta}^{n}\tilde{g}^{\sigma}\cdot(1+\zeta)^{l_{r}}\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta}\partial_{\zeta}^{n}\Theta\mathrm{d}\bar{x}\mathrm{d}\zeta\mathrm{d}s\,,

\displaystyle IV_{10}=

\displaystyle-\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\int_{0}^{\frac{3}{\sigma}}[\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta}\partial_{\zeta}^{n},u^{0}_{1,3}\chi_{\sigma}(\zeta)\partial_{\zeta}]\Theta\cdot(1+\zeta)^{l_{r}}\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta}\partial_{\zeta}^{n}\Theta\mathrm{d}\bar{x}\mathrm{d}\zeta\mathrm{d}s\,.

It is easy to estimate that

\displaystyle|IV_{1}|+|IV_{2}|\leq C(E_{k+1})\int_{0}^{t}\|\Theta(s)\|_{l,r,n}^{2}\mathrm{d}s\,.

Together with the properties of
\chi_{\sigma}(\zeta)
in (
5.9
),

\displaystyle IV_{3}\leq\tfrac{1}{4}\int_{0}^{t}\tilde{\kappa}_{0}\|\partial_{\zeta}\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta}\partial_{\zeta}^{n}\Theta(s)\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{l_{r}}}\mathrm{d}s+C(E_{k+1})\int_{0}^{t}\|\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta}\partial_{\zeta}^{n}\Theta(s)\|_{L^{2}_{l_{r}}}^{2}\mathrm{d}s\,,

and

\displaystyle|IV_{4}|\leq

\displaystyle C(E_{k+1})\sum_{\tilde{n}=0}^{n-1}\int_{0}^{t}(1+\zeta)^{l_{r}+1}|\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta}\partial_{\zeta}^{\tilde{n}+1}\Theta||\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta}\partial_{\zeta}^{n}\Theta|\mathrm{d}s

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C(E_{k+1})\sum_{\tilde{n}=1}^{n}\int_{0}^{t}\|\Theta(s)\|^{2}_{\mathbb{H}^{r}_{l,\tilde{n}}(\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+})}\mathrm{d}s\,.

Moreover, we similarly have

\displaystyle|IV_{6}|\leq

\displaystyle C(E_{k+1})\int_{0}^{t}\|\partial_{\zeta}\Theta(s)\|^{2}_{\mathbb{H}^{r-1}_{l,n}(\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+})}+C(E_{k+1})\sum_{\tilde{n}=1}^{n}\int_{0}^{t}\|\Theta(s)\|^{2}_{\mathbb{H}^{r}_{l,\tilde{n}}(\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+})}\mathrm{d}s\,,

and

\displaystyle|IV_{5}|\leq C(E_{k+1})\int_{0}^{t}\|\Theta(s)\|^{2}_{\mathbb{H}^{r}_{l,n}(\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+})}\mathrm{d}s\,,\quad IV_{8}=-\lambda\int_{0}^{t}\|\nabla_{\bar{x}}\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta}\partial_{\zeta}^{n}\Theta(s)\|_{L^{2}_{l_{r}}}^{2}\mathrm{d}s\,,

\displaystyle|IV_{9}|+|IV_{10}|\leq C\int_{0}^{t}\|\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta}\partial_{\zeta}^{n}(\tilde{g}^{\sigma},\Theta)(s)\|_{L^{2}_{l_{r}}}^{2}\mathrm{d}s+C(E_{k+1})\int_{0}^{t}\|\partial_{\zeta}\Theta(s)\|^{2}_{\mathbb{H}^{r-1}_{l,n}(\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+})}\mathrm{d}s\,.

It remains to control the term
IV_{7}
, which can be decomposed as follows:

\displaystyle IV_{7}=

\displaystyle\underbrace{\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta}(\tilde{\kappa}\partial_{\zeta}^{n+1}\Theta)(1+\zeta)^{l_{r}}\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta}\partial_{\zeta}^{n}\Theta|_{\zeta=0}^{\zeta=\frac{3}{\sigma}}\mathrm{d}\bar{x}\mathrm{d}s}_{IV_{7B}}

\displaystyle-\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\int_{0}^{\frac{3}{\sigma}}\tilde{\kappa}(1+\zeta)^{l_{r}}(\partial_{\zeta}\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta}\partial_{\zeta}^{n}\Theta)^{2}\mathrm{d}\bar{x}\mathrm{d}\zeta\mathrm{d}s

\displaystyle-\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\int_{0}^{\frac{3}{\sigma}}[\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta},\tilde{\kappa}\partial_{\zeta}^{n+1}]\Theta\cdot(1+\zeta)^{l_{r}}\partial_{\zeta}\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta}\partial_{\zeta}^{n}\Theta\mathrm{d}\bar{x}\mathrm{d}\zeta\mathrm{d}s

(5.50)

\displaystyle-\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\int_{0}^{\frac{3}{\sigma}}\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta}(\tilde{\kappa}\partial_{\zeta}^{n+1}\Theta)\cdot l_{r}(1+\zeta)^{l_{r}-1}\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta}\partial_{\zeta}^{n}\Theta\mathrm{d}\bar{x}\mathrm{d}\zeta\mathrm{d}s\,.

Due to the lower bound (
5.7
), the second term in the right-hand side of (
5
) can be bounded by

\displaystyle-\int_{0}^{t}\tilde{\kappa}_{0}\|\partial_{\zeta}\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta}\partial_{\zeta}^{n}\Theta\|_{L^{2}_{l_{r}}}^{2}\mathrm{d}s\,,

and the last two terms in that can be bounded by

\displaystyle\tfrac{1}{4}\int_{0}^{t}\tilde{\kappa}_{0}\|\partial_{\zeta}\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta}\partial_{\zeta}^{n}\Theta\|_{L^{2}_{l_{r}}}^{2}\mathrm{d}s+C(E_{k+1})\int_{0}^{t}\|\partial_{\zeta}\Theta(s)\|^{2}_{\mathbb{H}^{r-1}_{l,n}(\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+})}+\|\Theta(s)\|^{2}_{\mathbb{H}^{r}_{l,n}(\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+})}\mathrm{d}s\,.

Together with the bound of
IV_{7B}
in Lemma
5.3
below, one has

\displaystyle IV_{7}\leq

\displaystyle-\tfrac{3}{4}\int_{0}^{t}\tilde{\kappa}_{0}\|\partial_{\zeta}\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta}\partial_{\zeta}^{n}\Theta\|_{L^{2}_{l_{r}}}^{2}\mathrm{d}s-\mathscr{E}_{B,n}^{\gamma,\beta}(\Theta)(t)+C(\tau,E_{k+1})\sup_{t\in[0,\tau]}\|\tilde{g}^{\sigma}(t)\|^{2}_{\mathbb{H}^{r+1}_{l}(\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+})}

\displaystyle+\epsilon_{0}\mathscr{A}_{B,n}(\Theta)(t)+C(E_{k+1})\int_{0}^{t}\|\partial_{\zeta}\Theta(s)\|^{2}_{\mathbb{H}^{r-1}_{l,n}(\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+})}+\|\Theta(s)\|^{2}_{\mathbb{H}^{r}_{l,n}(\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+})}\mathrm{d}s

\displaystyle+C(E_{k+1})\Big(\|\Theta(0)\|^{2}_{\mathbb{H}^{r}_{l}(\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+})}+\sum_{p=0,1}\|\Theta(t)\|^{2}_{\mathbb{H}^{r}_{l,p}(\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+})}+\int_{0}^{t}\sum_{p=0,1}\|\Theta(s)\|^{2}_{\mathbb{H}^{r}_{l,p}(\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+})}\mathrm{d}s\Big)

for some undetermined small constant
\epsilon_{0}>0
.

Collecting the above bounds of
IV_{i}(1\leq i\leq 9)
and summing up for
2\gamma+|\beta|=r-n
reduce to

\displaystyle\|\Theta(t)\|_{l,r,n}^{2}+\int_{0}^{t}\tilde{\kappa}_{0}\|\partial_{\zeta}\Theta(s)\|_{l,r,n}^{2}+\lambda\|\nabla_{\bar{x}}\Theta(s)\|_{l,r,n}^{2}\mathrm{d}s+\sum_{2\gamma+|\beta|+n=r}\mathscr{E}_{B,n}^{\gamma,\beta}(\Theta)(t)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C(E_{k+1})\Big(\sum_{2\gamma+|\beta|+n=r}\epsilon_{0}\mathscr{A}_{B,n}(\Theta)(t)+\sum_{p=0,1}\|\Theta(t)\|^{2}_{\mathbb{H}^{r}_{l,p}(\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+})}+\int_{0}^{t}\|\partial_{\zeta}\Theta(s)\|^{2}_{\mathbb{H}^{r-1}_{l,n}(\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+})}\Big)

\displaystyle+C(\tau,E_{k+1})\Big(\|\Theta(0)\|^{2}_{\mathbb{H}^{r}_{l}(\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+})}+\int_{0}^{t}\sum_{0\leq\tilde{n}\leq n}\|\Theta(s)\|^{2}_{\mathbb{H}^{r}_{l,\tilde{n}}(\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+})}\mathrm{d}s+\sup_{t\in[0,\tau]}\|\tilde{g}^{\sigma}(t)\|^{2}_{\mathbb{H}^{r+1}_{l}(\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+})}\Big)

for
2\leq r\leq k(k\geq 3)
and
2\leq n\leq r
. Together with (
5.11
), (
5.21
) and (
5
), one derives from the induction for
1\leq r\leq k(k\geq 3)
that

(5.51)

\displaystyle\|\Theta(t)\|^{2}_{\mathbb{H}^{r}_{l,n}(\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+})}+\sum_{j=0}^{r}\sum_{2\gamma+|\beta|=j-n}\mathscr{E}_{B,n}^{\gamma,\beta}(\Theta)(t)

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{t}\|\partial_{\zeta}\Theta(s)\|^{2}_{\mathbb{H}^{r}_{l,n}(\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+})}+\lambda\|\nabla_{\bar{x}}\Theta(s)\|^{2}_{\mathbb{H}^{r}_{l,n}(\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+})}\mathrm{d}s

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C(\tau,E_{k+1})\Big(\epsilon_{0}\mathscr{A}_{B,n}^{r}(\Theta)(t)+\epsilon_{0}\mathscr{A}_{B,1}^{r}(\Theta)(t)+\|\Theta(0)\|^{2}_{\mathbb{H}^{r}_{l}(\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+})}\Big)

\displaystyle+C(\tau,E_{k+1})\Big(\sum_{0\leq\tilde{n}\leq n}\int_{0}^{t}\|\Theta(s)\|^{2}_{\mathbb{H}^{r}_{l,\tilde{n}}(\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+})}+\sup_{t\in[0,\tau]}\|\tilde{g}^{\sigma}(t)\|^{2}_{\mathbb{H}^{r+1}_{l}(\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+})}\Big)

for
2\leq r\leq k(k\geq 3)
,
2\leq n\leq r
and some small
\epsilon_{0}>0
to be determined, where

(5.52)

\displaystyle\mathscr{A}_{B,n}^{r}(\Theta)(t)=

\displaystyle\sum_{j=0}^{r}\sum_{2\gamma+|\beta|=j-n}\mathscr{A}_{B,n}^{\gamma,\beta}(\Theta)(t)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{j=0}^{r}\sum_{2\gamma+|\beta|=j-n}\sum_{i=1}^{2}\delta_{j}(\beta=e_{i})\int_{0}^{t}\|\partial_{t}^{\gamma+\frac{n+1}{2}}\Theta|_{\zeta=0}(s)\|^{2}_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{2})}\mathrm{d}s\,.

Finally, collecting the estimates (
5.11
), (
5.21
), (
5
) and (
5.51
) implies

\displaystyle\|\Theta(t)\|^{2}_{\mathbb{H}^{r}_{l}(\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+})}+\int_{0}^{t}\|\partial_{\zeta}\Theta(s)\|^{2}_{\mathbb{H}^{r}_{l}(\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+})}+\lambda\|\nabla_{\bar{x}}\Theta(s)\|^{2}_{\mathbb{H}^{r}_{l}(\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+})}\mathrm{d}s

\displaystyle+\widetilde{\mathscr{E}}_{B,r}(\Theta)(t)+\lambda\int_{0}^{t}\|\nabla_{\bar{x}}\Theta|_{\zeta=0}(s)\|^{2}_{\mathbb{H}^{r-1}(\mathbb{R}^{2})}\mathrm{d}s

\displaystyle\leq C(\tau,E_{k+1})\Big(\epsilon_{0}\sum_{n=1}^{r}\mathscr{A}_{B,n}^{r}(\Theta)(t)+\|\Theta(0)\|^{2}_{\mathbb{H}^{r}_{l}(\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+})}\Big)

(5.53)

\displaystyle+C(\tau,E_{k+1})\Big(\int_{0}^{t}\|\Theta(s)\|^{2}_{\mathbb{H}^{r}_{l}(\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+})}+\sup_{t\in[0,\tau]}\|\tilde{g}^{\sigma}(t)\|^{2}_{\mathbb{H}^{r+1}_{l}(\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+})}\Big)

for any
1\leq r\leq k(k\geq 3)
, where

(5.54)

\displaystyle\widetilde{\mathscr{E}}_{B,r}(\Theta)(t)=

\displaystyle\sum_{j=0}^{r}\sum_{n=2}^{r}\sum_{2\gamma+|\beta|=j-n}\mathscr{E}_{B,n}^{\gamma,\beta}(\Theta)(t)+\sum_{n=0,1}\delta_{n}\|\Theta|_{\zeta=0}(t)\|^{2}_{\mathbb{H}^{r-1}(\mathbb{R}^{2})}

\displaystyle+\sum_{n=0,1}\int_{0}^{t}(1-\delta_{n})\|\Theta|_{\zeta=0}(s)\|^{2}_{\mathbb{H}^{r}(\mathbb{R}^{2})}+\delta_{n}\lambda\|\nabla_{\bar{x}}\Theta|_{\zeta=0}(s)\|^{2}_{\mathbb{H}^{r-1}(\mathbb{R}^{2})}\mathrm{d}s\,.

Here
\delta_{n}
is defined in Lemma
5.3
, i.e.,
\delta_{n}=0
for even
n
and
\delta_{n}=1
for odd
n
. In particular, we set
r=k\geq 3
in (
5
). We need to control the quantity
\sum_{n=1}^{k}\mathscr{A}_{B,n}^{k}(\Theta)(t)
in the right-hand side of (
5
), namely,

\displaystyle\sum_{n=1}^{k}\mathscr{A}_{B,n}^{k}(\Theta)(t)=\sum_{n=1}^{k}\sum_{j=0}^{k}\sum_{2\gamma+|\beta|=j-n}\sum_{i=1}^{2}\delta_{j}(\beta=e_{i})\int_{0}^{t}\|\partial_{t}^{\gamma+\frac{n+1}{2}}\Theta|_{\zeta=0}(s)\|^{2}_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{2})}\mathrm{d}s\,.

It is easily derived from the trace inequality (
5.1
) in Lemma
5.1
that

(5.55)

\displaystyle\sum_{n=1}^{k}\sum_{j=0}^{k-1}\sum_{2\gamma+|\beta|=j-n}\sum_{i=1}^{2}\delta_{j}(\beta=e_{i})\int_{0}^{t}\|\partial_{t}^{\gamma+\frac{n+1}{2}}\Theta|_{\zeta=0}(s)\|^{2}_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{2})}\mathrm{d}s

\displaystyle\leq C\int_{0}^{t}\|\Theta(s)\|^{2}_{\mathbb{H}^{k}_{l}(\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+})}\mathrm{d}s\,.

We now consider the case
j=k
. Recall the definition
\delta_{k}(\beta=e_{i})
in (
5.34
), i.e.,
\delta_{k}(\beta=e_{i})=1
for even
k
and
\beta=e_{i}
, otherwise
\delta_{k}(\beta=e_{i})=0
. If
k
is odd,

\displaystyle\sum_{n=1}^{k}\sum_{2\gamma+|\beta|=k-n}\sum_{i=1}^{2}\delta_{k}(\beta=e_{i})\int_{0}^{t}\|\partial_{t}^{\gamma+\frac{n+1}{2}}\Theta|_{\zeta=0}(s)\|^{2}_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{2})}\mathrm{d}s=0\,.

If
k
is even,

\displaystyle\sum_{n=1}^{k}\sum_{2\gamma+|\beta|=k-n}\sum_{i=1}^{2}\delta_{k}(\beta=e_{i})\int_{0}^{t}\|\partial_{t}^{\gamma+\frac{n+1}{2}}\Theta|_{\zeta=0}(s)\|^{2}_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{2})}\mathrm{d}s

\displaystyle\leq C_{k}^{\prime}\int_{0}^{t}\|\Theta|_{\zeta=0}(s)\|^{2}_{\mathbb{H}^{r}(\mathbb{R}^{2})}\mathrm{d}s\leq C_{k}\widetilde{\mathscr{E}}_{B,r}(\Theta)(t)

for some constant
C_{k}>0
. There therefore holds

(5.56)

\displaystyle C(\tau,E_{k+1})\epsilon_{0}\sum_{n=1}^{k}

\displaystyle\mathscr{A}_{B,n}^{k}(\Theta)(t)

\displaystyle\leq\epsilon_{0}C(\tau,E_{k+1})C_{k}\widetilde{\mathscr{E}}_{B,r}(\Theta)(t)+C(\tau,E_{k+1})\int_{0}^{t}\|\Theta(s)\|^{2}_{\mathbb{H}^{k}_{l}(\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+})}\mathrm{d}s\,.

We now choose an
\epsilon_{0}\in(0,1)
such that

\displaystyle 0<\epsilon_{0}C(\tau,E_{k+1})C_{k}\leq\tfrac{1}{2}\,.

By plugging (
5.56
) into the inequality (
5
) with
r=k
, one has

\displaystyle\|\Theta(t)\|^{2}_{\mathbb{H}^{k}_{l}(\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+})}+\int_{0}^{t}\|\partial_{\zeta}\Theta(s)\|^{2}_{\mathbb{H}^{k}_{l}(\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+})}+\lambda\|\nabla_{\bar{x}}\Theta(s)\|^{2}_{\mathbb{H}^{k}_{l}(\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+})}\mathrm{d}s

\displaystyle+\tfrac{1}{2}\widetilde{\mathscr{E}}_{B,k}(\Theta)(t)+\lambda\int_{0}^{t}\|\nabla_{\bar{x}}\Theta|_{\zeta=0}(s)\|^{2}_{\mathbb{H}^{k-1}(\mathbb{R}^{2})}\mathrm{d}s

(5.57)

\displaystyle\leq C(\tau,E_{k+1})\Big(\|\Theta(0)\|^{2}_{\mathbb{H}^{k}_{l}(\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+})}+\int_{0}^{t}\|\Theta(s)\|^{2}_{\mathbb{H}^{k}_{l}(\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+})}\mathrm{d}s+\sup_{t\in[0,\tau]}\|\tilde{g}^{\sigma}(t)\|^{2}_{\mathbb{H}^{k+1}_{l}(\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+})}\Big)\,.

By the analogous arguments of the bound (
5
) for
\Theta
, one can also obtain the uniform bounds for
\mho

(5.58)

\displaystyle\|\mho(t)\|^{2}_{\mathbb{H}^{k}_{l}(\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+})}+\int_{0}^{t}\|\partial_{\zeta}\mho(s)\|^{2}_{\mathbb{H}^{k}_{l}(\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+})}+\lambda\|\nabla_{\bar{x}}\mho(s)\|^{2}_{\mathbb{H}^{k}_{l}(\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+})}\mathrm{d}s

\displaystyle+\tfrac{1}{2}\widetilde{\mathscr{E}}_{B,k}(\mho)(t)+\lambda\int_{0}^{t}\|\nabla_{\bar{x}}\mho|_{\zeta=0}(s)\|^{2}_{\mathbb{H}^{k-1}(\mathbb{R}^{2})}\mathrm{d}s

\displaystyle\leq C(\tau,E_{k+1})\Big(\|\mho(0)\|^{2}_{\mathbb{H}^{k}_{l}(\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+})}+\int_{0}^{t}\|(\mho,\Theta)(s)\|^{2}_{\mathbb{H}^{k}_{l}(\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+})}\mathrm{d}s+\sup_{t\in[0,\tau]}\|\tilde{f}^{\sigma}(t)\|^{2}_{\mathbb{H}^{k+1}_{l}(\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+})}\Big)\,.

Here the quantity
\int_{0}^{t}\|(\mho,\Theta)(s)\|^{2}_{\mathbb{H}^{k}_{l}(\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+})}\mathrm{d}s
is resulted from the term
\tfrac{\nabla_{\bar{x}}p^{0}}{3T^{0}}\Theta
in the
\mho
-equation of (
5.5
). From (
5
) and (
5.58
),

\displaystyle\|(\mho,\Theta)(t)\|^{2}_{\mathbb{H}^{k}_{l}(\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+})}+\int_{0}^{t}\|\partial_{\zeta}(\mho,\Theta)(s)\|^{2}_{\mathbb{H}^{k}_{l}(\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+})}+\lambda\|\nabla_{\bar{x}}(\mho,\Theta)(s)\|^{2}_{\mathbb{H}^{k}_{l}(\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+})}\mathrm{d}s

\displaystyle+\tfrac{1}{2}\widetilde{\mathscr{E}}_{B,k}(\mho,\Theta)(t)+\lambda\int_{0}^{t}\|\nabla_{\bar{x}}(\mho,\Theta)|_{\zeta=0}(s)\|^{2}_{\mathbb{H}^{k-1}(\mathbb{R}^{2})}\mathrm{d}s

\displaystyle\leq C(\tau,E_{k+1})\Big(\|(\mho,\Theta)(0)\|^{2}_{\mathbb{H}^{k}_{l}(\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+})}+\int_{0}^{t}\|(\mho,\Theta)(s)\|^{2}_{\mathbb{H}^{k}_{l}(\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+})}\mathrm{d}s+\sup_{t\in[0,\tau]}\|(\tilde{f}^{\sigma},\tilde{g}^{\sigma})(t)\|^{2}_{\mathbb{H}^{k+1}_{l}(\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+})}\Big)\,,

which implies by the Grönwall inequality that

(5.59)

\displaystyle\|(\mho,\Theta)(t)\|^{2}_{\mathbb{H}^{k}_{l}(\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+})}+\int_{0}^{t}\|\partial_{\zeta}(\mho,\Theta)(s)\|^{2}_{\mathbb{H}^{k}_{l}(\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+})}+\lambda\|\nabla_{\bar{x}}(\mho,\Theta)(s)\|^{2}_{\mathbb{H}^{k}_{l}(\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+})}\mathrm{d}s

\displaystyle+\widetilde{\mathscr{E}}_{B,k}(\mho,\Theta)(t)+\lambda\int_{0}^{t}\|\nabla_{\bar{x}}(\mho,\Theta)|_{\zeta=0}(s)\|^{2}_{\mathbb{H}^{k-1}(\mathbb{R}^{2})}\mathrm{d}s

\displaystyle\leq C(\tau,E_{k+1})\Big(\|(\mho,\Theta)(0)\|^{2}_{\mathbb{H}^{k}_{l}(\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+})}+\sup_{t\in[0,\tau]}\|(\tilde{f}^{\sigma},\tilde{g}^{\sigma})(t)\|^{2}_{\mathbb{H}^{k+1}_{l}(\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+})}\Big)\,.

Based on the uniform estimates (
5.59
), we can first take the limit
\sigma\to 0+
, and then
\lambda\to 0+
. Then, by using (
5.8
) and (
5.59
), the bound (
2.12
) holds, and Proposition
2.2
is proved. Here the details are omitted for simplicity of presentation.
∎

Now we give the following lemma to control the boundary term
IV_{7B}
.

Lemma 5.3
.

Let
e_{1}=(1,0),e_{2}=(0,1)\in\mathbb{N}^{2}
. For
2\leq n\leq r
and
2\gamma+|\beta|+n=r
, let
IV_{7B}
be given in (
5
), i.e.,

\displaystyle IV_{7B}=\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta}(\tilde{\kappa}\partial_{\zeta}^{n+1}\Theta)(1+\zeta)^{l_{r}}\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta}\partial_{\zeta}^{n}\Theta|_{\zeta=0}^{\zeta=\frac{3}{\sigma}}\mathrm{d}\bar{x}\mathrm{d}s\,.

There hold

(5.60)

\displaystyle IV_{7B}\leq

\displaystyle-\mathscr{E}_{B,n}^{\gamma,\beta}(\Theta)(t)+\epsilon_{0}\mathscr{A}_{B,n}^{\gamma,\beta}(\Theta)(t)+C(\tau,E_{k+1})\sup_{t\in[0,\tau]}\|\tilde{g}^{\sigma}(t)\|^{2}_{\mathbb{H}^{r+1}_{l}(\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+})}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle C(E_{k+1})\Big(\|\Theta(0)\|^{2}_{\mathbb{H}^{r}_{l}(\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+})}+\sum_{p=0,1}\|\Theta(t)\|^{2}_{\mathbb{H}^{r}_{l,p}(\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+})}+\int_{0}^{t}\sum_{p=0,1}\|\Theta(s)\|^{2}_{\mathbb{H}^{r}_{l,p}(\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+})}\mathrm{d}s\Big)

for some small
\epsilon_{0}>0
to be determined, where the symbols
\mathscr{A}_{B,n}^{\gamma,\beta}(\Theta)(t)
and
\mathscr{E}_{B,n}^{\gamma,\beta}(\Theta)(t)
are

(5.61)

\displaystyle\mathscr{A}_{B,n}^{\gamma,\beta}(\Theta)(t)=\sum_{i=1}^{2}\delta_{r}(\beta=e_{i})\int_{0}^{t}\|\partial_{t}^{\gamma+\frac{n+1}{2}}\Theta|_{\zeta=0}(s)\|^{2}_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{2})}\mathrm{d}s\,,

and

(5.62)

\displaystyle\mathscr{E}_{B,n}^{\gamma,\beta}(\Theta)(t)=

\displaystyle\tfrac{1}{2}\delta_{n}c_{n}\|\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta}(\partial_{t}-\lambda\Delta_{\bar{x}})^{\frac{n-1}{2}}\Theta|_{\zeta=0}(t)\|^{2}_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{2})}

\displaystyle+\lambda\delta_{n}c_{n}\int_{0}^{t}\|\nabla_{\bar{x}}\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta}(\partial_{t}-\lambda\Delta_{\bar{x}})^{\frac{n-1}{2}}\Theta|_{\zeta=0}(s)\|^{2}_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{2})}\mathrm{d}s

\displaystyle+\tfrac{1}{2}(1-\delta_{n})c_{0}\int_{0}^{t}\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta}(\partial_{t}-\lambda\Delta_{\bar{x}})^{\frac{n}{2}}\Theta|_{\zeta=0}(s)\|^{2}_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{2})}\mathrm{d}s

for some constant
c_{0},c_{n}>0
. Here
\delta_{n}=1
for odd
n
and
\delta_{n}=0
for even
n
. The symbol
\delta_{r}(\beta=e_{i})
is defined in (
5.34
). Namely, its value is 1 if
r
is even and
\beta=e_{i}
. Otherwise, its value will vanish.

Remark 5.1
.

When
r
is even and
|\beta|=1
, the relation
2\gamma+|\beta|+n=r
means that
n
must be odd. In other words, the quantity
\mathscr{A}_{B,n}(\Theta)(t)
will be zero for the case of even
n
. Furthermore, in order to obtain the bound of
IV_{7B}
in (
5.60
), the source term
\tilde{g}^{\sigma}
shall assume
(r+1)
-order derivatives (i.e., in
\mathbb{H}^{r+1}_{l}(\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+})
). The details can be found in (
5.76
) below. Here a first order time derivative is equivalently regarded as a second order
\bar{x}
-derivative.

Proof.

From Lemma
5.2
, we see that for
2\leq n\leq r
,
\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta}(\tilde{\kappa}\partial_{\zeta}^{n+1}\Theta)(1+\zeta)^{l_{r}}\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta}\partial_{\zeta}^{n}\Theta|_{\zeta=\frac{3}{\sigma}}=0
. One therefore has

(5.63)

\displaystyle IV_{7B}=-\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta}(\tilde{\kappa}\partial_{\zeta}^{n+1}\Theta)\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta}\partial_{\zeta}^{n}\Theta|_{\zeta=0}\mathrm{d}\bar{x}\mathrm{d}s\,.

Case 1.
n\in[2,r]
is even.
From Lemma
5.2
and remarks (
5.26
), we have
2\gamma+|\beta|+n=r
and

(5.64)

\displaystyle IV_{7B}=

\displaystyle-\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\tilde{\kappa}R_{\theta}\{\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta}(\mathscr{L}+\tilde{\mathscr{L}})^{\frac{n}{2}}\Theta\}^{2}|_{\zeta=0}\mathrm{d}\bar{x}\mathrm{d}s

\displaystyle-\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta}(\mathscr{L}+\tilde{\mathscr{L}})^{\frac{n}{2}}\Theta\cdot(\mathscr{L}^{\star}_{5}\Theta+\mathscr{L}^{\star}_{6}\tilde{g}^{\sigma}+\tilde{\kappa}R_{\theta}\mathscr{L}^{\star}_{7}\tilde{g}^{\sigma})|_{\zeta=0}\mathrm{d}\bar{x}\mathrm{d}s

\displaystyle-\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}(\mathscr{L}^{\star}_{5}\Theta+\mathscr{L}^{\star}_{6}\tilde{g}^{\sigma})\cdot\mathscr{L}^{\star}_{7}\tilde{g}^{\sigma}|_{\zeta=0}\mathrm{d}\bar{x}\mathrm{d}s=:IV_{7B}^{e1}+IV_{7B}^{e2}+IV_{7B}^{e3}\,,

where

(5.65)

\displaystyle\mathscr{L}^{\star}_{5}\Theta=

\displaystyle\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta}[\tilde{\kappa}(\mathscr{L}+\tilde{\mathscr{L}})^{\frac{n}{2}},R_{\theta}]\Theta+[\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta},\tilde{\kappa}R_{\theta}](\mathscr{L}+\tilde{\mathscr{L}})^{\frac{n}{2}}\Theta\,,

\displaystyle\mathscr{L}^{\star}_{6}\tilde{g}^{\sigma}=

\displaystyle\sum_{i=0}^{\frac{n}{2}-1}\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta}\{\tilde{\kappa}(\mathscr{L}+\tilde{\mathscr{L}})^{i}\hat{\mathscr{L}}\partial_{\zeta}^{n-1-2i}\tilde{g}^{\sigma}\}\,,\ \mathscr{L}^{\star}_{7}\tilde{g}^{\sigma}=\sum_{i=0}^{\frac{n}{2}-1}\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta}(\mathscr{L}+\tilde{\mathscr{L}})^{i}\hat{\mathscr{L}}\partial_{\zeta}^{n-2-2i}\tilde{g}^{\sigma}\,.

From the lower bounds (
2.9
) and (
5.7
), we know
\tilde{\kappa}R_{\theta}\geq\tilde{\kappa}_{0}R_{\theta}^{0}:=c_{0}^{\prime}>0
. Then the quantity
IV_{7B}^{e1}
can be bounded by

(5.66)

\displaystyle IV_{7B}^{e1}\leq-c_{0}^{\prime}\int_{0}^{t}\|\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta}(\mathscr{L}+\tilde{\mathscr{L}})^{\frac{n}{2}}\Theta|_{\zeta=0}(s)\|^{2}_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{2})}\mathrm{d}s\,.

One further observes that
\mathscr{L}^{\star}_{5}
is an at most
(r-1)
-order differential operator,
\mathscr{L}^{\star}_{6}
is
(r-1)
-order, and
\mathscr{L}^{\star}_{7}
is
(r-2)
-order. There therefore hold

(5.67)

\displaystyle IV_{7B}^{e2}\leq

\displaystyle\tfrac{1}{2}c_{0}^{\prime}\int_{0}^{t}\|\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta}(\mathscr{L}+\tilde{\mathscr{L}})^{\frac{n}{2}}\Theta|_{\zeta=0}(s)\|^{2}_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{2})}\mathrm{d}s

\displaystyle+C(E_{k+1})\int_{0}^{t}\sum_{p=0,1}\|\Theta(s)\|^{2}_{\mathbb{H}^{r}_{l,p}(\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+})}+\sum_{0\leq\tilde{n}\leq n}\|\tilde{g}^{\sigma}(s)\|^{2}_{\mathbb{H}^{r}_{l,\tilde{n}}(\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+})}\mathrm{d}s

and

(5.68)

\displaystyle IV_{7B}^{e3}\leq C(E_{k+1})\int_{0}^{t}\sum_{p=0,1}\|\Theta(s)\|^{2}_{\mathbb{H}^{r}_{l,p}(\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+})}+\sum_{0\leq\tilde{n}\leq n}\|\tilde{g}^{\sigma}(s)\|^{2}_{\mathbb{H}^{r}_{l,\tilde{n}}(\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+})}\mathrm{d}s\,,

where the trace inequalities (
5.1
)-(
5.2
) in Lemma
5.1
have been utilized. Consequently, the bounds (
5.66
), (
5.67
) and (
5.68
) tell us

\displaystyle IV_{7B}\leq

\displaystyle-\tfrac{1}{2}c_{0}^{\prime}\int_{0}^{t}\|\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta}(\mathscr{L}+\tilde{\mathscr{L}})^{\frac{n}{2}}\Theta|_{\zeta=0}(s)\|^{2}_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{2})}\mathrm{d}s

\displaystyle+C(E_{k+1})\int_{0}^{t}\sum_{p=0,1}\|\Theta(s)\|^{2}_{\mathbb{H}^{r}_{l,p}(\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+})}+\sum_{0\leq\tilde{n}\leq n}\|\tilde{g}^{\sigma}(s)\|^{2}_{\mathbb{H}^{r}_{l,\tilde{n}}(\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+})}\mathrm{d}s\,,

when
n\in[2,r]
is even. Recalling the definitions of
\mathscr{L}
and
\tilde{\mathscr{L}}
in Lemma
5.2
, one easily computes

\displaystyle\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta}(\mathscr{L}+\tilde{\mathscr{L}})^{\frac{n}{2}}=\tilde{\kappa}^{-\frac{n}{2}}\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta}(\partial_{t}-\lambda\Delta_{\bar{x}})^{\frac{n}{2}}+\mathscr{L}^{\star}_{0}\,,

where

\displaystyle\mathscr{L}^{\star}_{0}=

\displaystyle[\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta},\tilde{\kappa}^{-\frac{n}{2}}](\partial_{t}-\lambda\Delta_{\bar{x}})^{\frac{n}{2}}+\sum_{p=1}^{\frac{n}{2}}C_{\frac{n}{2}}^{p}\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta}\mathscr{L}^{\frac{n}{2}-p}\tilde{\mathscr{L}}^{p}+\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta}\big(\mathscr{L}^{\frac{n}{2}}-\tilde{\kappa}^{-\frac{n}{2}}(\partial_{t}-\lambda\Delta_{\bar{x}})^{\frac{n}{2}}\big)

is an
(r-1)
-order differential operator. Together with the trace inequality (
5.1
) in Lemma
5.1
, one has

\displaystyle c_{0}^{\prime\prime}\|\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta}(\partial_{t}-\lambda\Delta_{\bar{x}})^{\frac{n}{2}}\Theta|_{\zeta=0}(s)\|^{2}_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{2})}\leq\|\tilde{\kappa}^{-\frac{n}{2}}\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta}(\partial_{t}-\lambda\Delta_{\bar{x}})^{\frac{n}{2}}\Theta|_{\zeta=0}(s)\|^{2}_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{2})}

\displaystyle\leq 2\|\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta}(\mathscr{L}+\tilde{\mathscr{L}})^{\frac{n}{2}}\Theta|_{\zeta=0}(s)\|^{2}_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{2})}+2\|\mathscr{L}^{\star}_{0}\Theta|_{\zeta=0}(s)\|^{2}_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{2})}

\displaystyle\leq 2\|\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta}(\mathscr{L}+\tilde{\mathscr{L}})^{\frac{n}{2}}\Theta|_{\zeta=0}(s)\|^{2}_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{2})}+C(E_{k+1})\sum_{p=0,1}\|\Theta(s)\|^{2}_{\mathbb{H}^{r}_{l,p}(\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+})}

for some constant
c_{0}^{\prime\prime}>0
. We thereby know

(5.69)

\displaystyle IV_{7B}\leq

\displaystyle-\tfrac{1}{2}c_{0}\int_{0}^{t}\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta}(\partial_{t}-\lambda\Delta_{\bar{x}})^{\frac{n}{2}}\Theta|_{\zeta=0}(s)\|^{2}_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{2})}\mathrm{d}s

\displaystyle+C(E_{k+1})\int_{0}^{t}\sum_{p=0,1}\|\Theta(s)\|^{2}_{\mathbb{H}^{r}_{l,p}(\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+})}+\sum_{0\leq\tilde{n}\leq n}\|\tilde{g}^{\sigma}(s)\|^{2}_{\mathbb{H}^{r}_{l,\tilde{n}}(\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+})}\mathrm{d}s\,,

where
c_{0}=\frac{1}{2}c_{0}^{\prime}c_{0}^{\prime\prime}>0
.

Case 2.
n\in[2,r]
is odd.
It is further derived from Lemma
5.2
and remarks (
5.26
) that

\displaystyle IV_{7B}=

\displaystyle-\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta}\big\{\tilde{\kappa}(\mathscr{L}+\tilde{\mathscr{L}})^{\frac{n+1}{2}}\Theta\big\}\cdot\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta}(\mathscr{L}+\tilde{\mathscr{L}})^{\frac{n-1}{2}}(R_{\theta}\Theta)|_{\zeta=0}\mathrm{d}\bar{x}\mathrm{d}s

\displaystyle-\sum_{j=0}^{\frac{n-3}{2}}\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta}[\tilde{\kappa}(\mathscr{L}+\tilde{\mathscr{L}})^{\frac{n+1}{2}}\Theta]\cdot\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta}(\mathscr{L}+\tilde{\mathscr{L}})^{j}\hat{\mathscr{L}}\partial_{\zeta}^{n-2-2j}\tilde{g}^{\sigma}|_{\zeta=0}\mathrm{d}\bar{x}\mathrm{d}s

\displaystyle-\sum_{i=0}^{\frac{n-1}{2}}\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta}[\tilde{\kappa}(\mathscr{L}+\tilde{\mathscr{L}})^{i}\hat{\mathscr{L}}\partial_{\zeta}^{n-1-2i}\tilde{g}^{\sigma}]

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\times\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta}(\mathscr{L}+\tilde{\mathscr{L}})^{\frac{n-1}{2}}(R_{\theta}\Theta)|_{\zeta=0}\mathrm{d}\bar{x}\mathrm{d}s

(5.70)

\displaystyle-\sum_{i=0}^{\frac{n-1}{2}}\sum_{j=0}^{\frac{n-3}{2}}\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta}[\tilde{\kappa}(\mathscr{L}+\tilde{\mathscr{L}})^{i}\hat{\mathscr{L}}\partial_{\zeta}^{n-1-2i}\tilde{g}^{\sigma}]

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\times\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta}(\mathscr{L}+\tilde{\mathscr{L}})^{j}\hat{\mathscr{L}}\partial_{\zeta}^{n-2-2j}\tilde{g}^{\sigma}|_{\zeta=0}\mathrm{d}\bar{x}\mathrm{d}s

\displaystyle=:

\displaystyle IV_{7B}^{1}+IV_{7B}^{2}+IV_{7B}^{3}+IV_{7B}^{4}\,,

where the differential operators
\mathscr{L}
,
\hat{\mathscr{L}}
and
\tilde{\mathscr{L}}
are defined in (
5.2
) and (
5.25
). While
2\gamma+|\beta|+n=r
, one observes that the differential operators
\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta}[\tilde{\kappa}(\mathscr{L}+\tilde{\mathscr{L}})^{i}\hat{\mathscr{L}}\partial_{\zeta}^{n-1-2i}]
(
0\leq i\leq\frac{n-1}{2}
) and
\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\zeta}^{\beta}(\mathscr{L}+\tilde{\mathscr{L}})^{\frac{n-1}{2}}
are all (
r-1
)-order, and
\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta}(\mathscr{L}+\tilde{\mathscr{L}})^{j}\hat{\mathscr{L}}\partial_{\zeta}^{n-2-2j}
(
0\leq j\leq\frac{n-3}{2}
) are
(r-2)
-order differential operators. Consequently, the trace inequality (
5.1
) in Lemma
5.1
tells us

(5.71)

\displaystyle|IV_{7B}^{3}+IV_{7B}^{4}|\leq C(E_{k+1})\int_{0}^{t}\|\Theta(s)\|^{2}_{\mathbb{H}^{r}_{l,0}(\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+})}+\|\Theta(s)\|^{2}_{\mathbb{H}^{r}_{l,1}(\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+})}+\sum_{\tilde{n}=0}^{n}\|\tilde{g}^{\sigma}(s)\|^{2}_{\mathbb{H}^{r}_{l,\tilde{n}}(\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+})}\mathrm{d}s\,.

Next we control the quantity
IV_{7B}^{2}
. One first computes that

(5.72)

\displaystyle\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta}[\tilde{\kappa}(\mathscr{L}+\tilde{\mathscr{L}})^{\frac{n+1}{2}}]=

\displaystyle\tilde{\kappa}^{-\frac{n-1}{2}}\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta}(\partial_{t}-\lambda\Delta_{\bar{x}})^{\frac{n+1}{2}}+\mathscr{L}^{\star}_{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\tilde{\kappa}^{-\frac{n-1}{2}}\partial_{t}^{\gamma+\frac{n+1}{2}}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta}+\tilde{\kappa}^{-\frac{n-1}{2}}\Delta_{\bar{x}}\mathscr{L}^{\star}_{1}+\mathscr{L}^{\star}_{2}\,,

where

(5.73)

\displaystyle\mathscr{L}^{\star}_{1}=

\displaystyle\sum_{j=1}^{\frac{n+1}{2}}C_{\frac{n+1}{2}}^{j}(-\lambda)^{j}\partial_{t}^{\gamma+\frac{n+1}{2}-j}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta}\Delta_{\bar{x}}^{j-1}\,,

\displaystyle\mathscr{L}^{\star}_{2}=

\displaystyle[\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta},\tilde{\kappa}^{-\frac{n-1}{2}}](\partial_{t}-\lambda\Delta_{\bar{x}})^{\frac{n+1}{2}}+\sum_{j=1}^{\frac{n+1}{2}}C_{\frac{n+1}{2}}^{j}\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta}(\tilde{\kappa}\mathscr{L}^{\frac{n+1}{2}-j}\tilde{\mathscr{L}}^{j})

\displaystyle+\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta}\big\{\tilde{\kappa}\mathscr{L}^{\frac{n+1}{2}}-\tilde{\kappa}^{-\frac{n-1}{2}}(\partial_{t}-\lambda\Delta_{\bar{x}})^{\frac{n+1}{2}}\big\}\,.

We observe that
\mathscr{L}^{\star}_{1}
is
(r-1)
-order differential operator, and
\mathscr{L}^{\star}_{2}
is
r
-order differential operator, where the highest order derivatives must contain
\nabla_{\bar{x}}
. Consequently, the trace inequalities (
5.1
)-(
5.2
) in Lemma
5.1
imply that

\displaystyle-\sum_{j=0}^{\frac{n-3}{2}}\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\Big(\tilde{\kappa}^{-\frac{n-1}{2}}\Delta_{\bar{x}}\mathscr{L}^{\star}_{1}+\mathscr{L}^{\star}_{2}\Big)\Theta\cdot\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta}(\mathscr{L}+\tilde{\mathscr{L}})^{j}\hat{\mathscr{L}}\partial_{\zeta}^{n-2-2j}\tilde{g}^{\sigma}|_{\zeta=0}\mathrm{d}\bar{x}\mathrm{d}s

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{j=0}^{\frac{n-3}{2}}\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\nabla_{\bar{x}}\mathscr{L}^{\star}_{1}\Theta\cdot\nabla_{\bar{x}}\Big\{\tilde{\kappa}^{-\frac{n-1}{2}}\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta}(\mathscr{L}+\tilde{\mathscr{L}})^{j}\hat{\mathscr{L}}\partial_{\zeta}^{n-2-2j}\tilde{g}^{\sigma}\Big\}|_{\zeta=0}\mathrm{d}\bar{x}\mathrm{d}s

\displaystyle-\sum_{j=0}^{\frac{n-3}{2}}\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\mathscr{L}^{\star}_{2}\Theta\cdot\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta}(\mathscr{L}+\tilde{\mathscr{L}})^{j}\hat{\mathscr{L}}\partial_{\zeta}^{n-2-2j}\tilde{g}^{\sigma}|_{\zeta=0}\mathrm{d}\bar{x}\mathrm{d}s

(5.74)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C(E_{k+1})\int_{0}^{t}\|\Theta(s)\|^{2}_{\mathbb{H}^{r}_{l,0}(\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+})}+\|\Theta(s)\|^{2}_{\mathbb{H}^{r}_{l,1}(\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+})}+\sum_{\tilde{n}=0}^{n}\|\tilde{g}^{\sigma}(s)\|^{2}_{\mathbb{H}^{r}_{l,\tilde{n}}(\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+})}\mathrm{d}s\,.

For the quantity
IV_{7B}^{2}
, it remains to estimate the term

\displaystyle IV_{7B}^{21}(\beta)=:-\sum_{j=0}^{\frac{n-3}{2}}\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\tilde{\kappa}^{-\frac{n-1}{2}}\partial_{t}^{\gamma+\frac{n+1}{2}}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta}\Theta\cdot\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta}(\mathscr{L}+\tilde{\mathscr{L}})^{j}\hat{\mathscr{L}}\partial_{\zeta}^{n-2-2j}\tilde{g}^{\sigma}|_{\zeta=0}\mathrm{d}\bar{x}\mathrm{d}s\,.

If
|\beta|\geq 1
, similar arguments in (
5
) reduce to

\displaystyle IV_{7B}^{21}(\beta)=

\displaystyle\sum_{j=0}^{\frac{n-3}{2}}\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\partial_{t}^{\gamma+\frac{n-1}{2}}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta}\Theta\cdot\partial_{t}\big\{\tilde{\kappa}^{-\frac{n-1}{2}}\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta}(\mathscr{L}+\tilde{\mathscr{L}})^{j}\hat{\mathscr{L}}\partial_{\zeta}^{n-2-2j}\tilde{g}^{\sigma}\big\}|_{\zeta=0}\mathrm{d}\bar{x}\mathrm{d}s

\displaystyle-\sum_{j=0}^{\frac{n-3}{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\partial_{t}^{\gamma+\frac{n-1}{2}}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta}\Theta\cdot\tilde{\kappa}^{-\frac{n-1}{2}}\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta}(\mathscr{L}+\tilde{\mathscr{L}})^{j}\hat{\mathscr{L}}\partial_{\zeta}^{n-2-2j}\tilde{g}^{\sigma}|_{\zeta=0}|_{s=0}^{s=t}\mathrm{d}\bar{x}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C(E_{k+1})\int_{0}^{t}\|\Theta(s)\|^{2}_{\mathbb{H}^{r}_{l,0}(\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+})}+\|\Theta(s)\|^{2}_{\mathbb{H}^{r}_{l,1}(\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+})}+\sum_{\tilde{n}=0}^{n}\|\tilde{g}^{\sigma}(s)\|^{2}_{\mathbb{H}^{r}_{l,\tilde{n}}(\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+})}\mathrm{d}s

\displaystyle+C(E_{k+1})\big(\|\Theta(0)\|^{2}_{\mathbb{H}^{r}_{l}(\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+})}+\sum_{p=0,1}\|\Theta(t)\|^{2}_{\mathbb{H}^{r}_{l,p}(\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+})}+\sup_{t\in[0,\tau]}\sum_{\tilde{n}=0}^{n}\|\tilde{g}^{\sigma}(t)\|^{2}_{\mathbb{H}^{r}_{l,\tilde{n}}(\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+})}\big)\,.

If
\beta=0
, we have
2\gamma=r-n
. Here
r
is also odd. Then
\partial_{t}\big\{\tilde{\kappa}^{-\frac{n-1}{2}}\partial_{t}^{\gamma}(\mathscr{L}+\tilde{\mathscr{L}})^{j}\hat{\mathscr{L}}\partial_{\zeta}^{n-2-2j}\big\}

(0\leq j\leq\frac{n-3}{2})
are
r
-order differential operators. It is therefore derived from the trace inequality (
5.1
) that

(5.75)

\displaystyle\|\partial_{t}\big\{\tilde{\kappa}^{-\frac{n-1}{2}}\partial_{t}^{\gamma}(\mathscr{L}+\tilde{\mathscr{L}})^{j}\hat{\mathscr{L}}\partial_{\zeta}^{n-2-2j}\big\}\tilde{g}^{\sigma}|_{\zeta=0}(t)\|^{2}_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{2})}\leq C(E_{k+1})\sum_{\tilde{n}=0}^{n}\|\tilde{g}^{\sigma}(t)\|^{2}_{\mathbb{H}^{r+1}_{l,\tilde{n}}(\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+})}\,.

In other words, the source term
\tilde{g}^{\sigma}(t,\bar{x},\zeta)
shall be in
\mathbb{H}^{r+1}_{l}(\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+})
. Consequently,

\displaystyle IV_{7B}^{21}(\beta)=

\displaystyle\sum_{j=0}^{\frac{n-3}{2}}\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\partial_{t}^{\gamma+\frac{n-1}{2}}\Theta\cdot\partial_{t}\big\{\tilde{\kappa}^{-\frac{n-1}{2}}\partial_{t}^{\gamma}(\mathscr{L}+\tilde{\mathscr{L}})^{j}\hat{\mathscr{L}}\partial_{\zeta}^{n-2-2j}\tilde{g}^{\sigma}\big\}|_{\zeta=0}\mathrm{d}\bar{x}\mathrm{d}s

\displaystyle-\sum_{j=0}^{\frac{n-3}{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\partial_{t}^{\gamma+\frac{n-1}{2}}\Theta\cdot\tilde{\kappa}^{-\frac{n-1}{2}}\partial_{t}^{\gamma}(\mathscr{L}+\tilde{\mathscr{L}})^{j}\hat{\mathscr{L}}\partial_{\zeta}^{n-2-2j}\tilde{g}^{\sigma}|_{\zeta=0}|_{s=0}^{s=t}\mathrm{d}\bar{x}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C(E_{k+1})\int_{0}^{t}\|\Theta(s)\|^{2}_{\mathbb{H}^{r}_{l,0}(\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+})}+\|\Theta(s)\|^{2}_{\mathbb{H}^{r}_{l,1}(\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+})}+\sum_{\tilde{n}=0}^{n}\|\tilde{g}^{\sigma}(s)\|^{2}_{\mathbb{H}^{r+1}_{l,\tilde{n}}(\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+})}\mathrm{d}s

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle C(E_{k+1})\big(\|\Theta(0)\|^{2}_{\mathbb{H}^{r}_{l}(\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+})}+\sum_{p=0,1}\|\Theta(t)\|^{2}_{\mathbb{H}^{r}_{l,p}(\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+})}+\sup_{t\in[0,\tau]}\sum_{\tilde{n}=0}^{n}\|\tilde{g}^{\sigma}(t)\|^{2}_{\mathbb{H}^{r}_{l,\tilde{n}}(\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+})}\big)\,.

In summary, one sees that for all
\beta\in\mathbb{N}^{2}
with
2\gamma+|\beta|+n=r

(5.76)

\displaystyle IV_{7B}^{21}(\beta)\leq C(E_{k+1})\Big(\sum_{p=0,1}\|\Theta(t)\|^{2}_{\mathbb{H}^{r}_{l,p}(\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+})}+\int_{0}^{t}\sum_{p=0,1}\|\Theta(s)\|^{2}_{\mathbb{H}^{r}_{l,p}(\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+})}\mathrm{d}s

\displaystyle+\|\Theta(0)\|^{2}_{\mathbb{H}^{r}_{l}(\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+})}+(1+t)\sup_{t\in[0,\tau]}\sum_{\tilde{n}=0}^{n}\|\tilde{g}^{\sigma}(t)\|^{2}_{\mathbb{H}^{r+1}_{l,\tilde{n}}(\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+})}\Big)\,.

Then, (
5
)-(
5.76
) reduce to

(5.77)

\displaystyle IV_{7B}^{2}\leq C(E_{k+1})\Big(\sum_{p=0,1}\|\Theta(t)\|^{2}_{\mathbb{H}^{r}_{l,p}(\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+})}+\int_{0}^{t}\sum_{p=0,1}\|\Theta(s)\|^{2}_{\mathbb{H}^{r}_{l,p}(\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+})}\mathrm{d}s

\displaystyle+\|\Theta(0)\|^{2}_{\mathbb{H}^{r}_{l}(\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+})}+(1+t)\sup_{t\in[0,\tau]}\sum_{\tilde{n}=0}^{n}\|\tilde{g}^{\sigma}(t)\|^{2}_{\mathbb{H}^{r+1}_{l,\tilde{n}}(\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+})}\Big)\,.

We next control the term
IV_{7B}^{1}
in (
5
). By direct calculation,

(5.78)

\displaystyle\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta}(\mathscr{L}

\displaystyle+\tilde{\mathscr{L}})^{\frac{n-1}{2}}(R_{\theta}\,\cdot)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle R_{\theta}\tilde{\kappa}^{-\frac{n-1}{2}}\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta}(\partial_{t}-\lambda\Delta_{\bar{x}})^{\frac{n-1}{2}}+[\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta},R_{\theta}\tilde{\kappa}^{-\frac{n-1}{2}}]\partial_{t}^{\gamma}(\partial_{t}-\lambda\Delta_{\bar{x}})^{\frac{n-1}{2}}+\mathscr{L}^{\star}_{3}\,,

where

(5.79)

\displaystyle\mathscr{L}^{\star}_{3}=

\displaystyle\sum_{q=1}^{\frac{n-1}{2}}C_{\frac{n-1}{2}}^{q}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta}\big\{R_{\theta}\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\mathscr{L}^{\frac{n-1}{2}-q}\tilde{\mathscr{L}}^{q}\big\}+\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta}[\partial_{t}^{\gamma}(\mathscr{L}+\tilde{\mathscr{L}})^{\frac{n-1}{2}},R_{\theta}]

\displaystyle+\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta}\Big\{R_{\theta}\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\big(\mathscr{L}^{\frac{n-1}{2}}-\tilde{\kappa}^{-\frac{n-1}{2}}(\partial_{t}-\lambda\Delta_{\bar{x}})^{\frac{n-1}{2}}\big)+R_{\theta}[\partial_{t}^{\gamma},\tilde{\kappa}^{-\frac{n-1}{2}}](\partial_{t}-\lambda\Delta_{\bar{x}})^{\frac{n-1}{2}}\Big\}

is an
(r-2)
-order differential operator. Moreover, due to the definition of
\tilde{\mathscr{L}}
in (
5.25
), the highest order (
(r-2)
-order) derivatives of
\mathscr{L}^{\star}_{3}
must contain
\nabla_{\bar{x}}
. Together with (
5.72
) and (
5.78
), we can decompose the term
IV_{7B}^{1}
as

(5.80)

\displaystyle IV_{7B}^{1}=

\displaystyle-\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\mathscr{L}^{\star}_{2}\Theta\cdot\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta}(\mathscr{L}+\tilde{\mathscr{L}})^{\frac{n-1}{2}}(R_{\theta}\Theta)|_{\zeta=0}\mathrm{d}\bar{x}\mathrm{d}s

\displaystyle-\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\tilde{\kappa}^{-\frac{n-1}{2}}\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta}(\partial_{t}-\lambda\Delta_{\bar{x}})^{\frac{n+1}{2}}\Theta\cdot\mathscr{L}^{\star}_{3}\Theta|_{\zeta=0}\mathrm{d}\bar{x}\mathrm{d}s

\displaystyle-\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\tilde{\kappa}^{-\frac{n-1}{2}}\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta}(\partial_{t}-\lambda\Delta_{\bar{x}})^{\frac{n+1}{2}}\Theta

\displaystyle\times[\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta},R_{\theta}\tilde{\kappa}^{-\frac{n-1}{2}}]\partial_{t}^{\gamma}(\partial_{t}-\lambda\Delta_{\bar{x}})^{\frac{n-1}{2}}\Theta|_{\zeta=0}\mathrm{d}\bar{x}\mathrm{d}s

\displaystyle-\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}R_{\theta}\tilde{\kappa}^{-n+1}\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta}(\partial_{t}-\lambda\Delta_{\bar{x}})^{\frac{n+1}{2}}\Theta\cdot\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta}(\partial_{t}-\lambda\Delta_{\bar{x}})^{\frac{n-1}{2}}\Theta|_{\zeta=0}\mathrm{d}\bar{x}\mathrm{d}s

\displaystyle=:

\displaystyle IV_{7B}^{11}+IV_{7B}^{12}+IV_{7B}^{13}+IV_{7B}^{14}\,.

It is thereby implied by the trace inequalities (
5.1
)-(
5.2
) in Lemma
5.1
that

(5.81)

\displaystyle IV_{7B}^{11}\leq

\displaystyle C(E_{k+1})\int_{0}^{t}\|\Theta(s)\|^{2}_{\mathbb{H}^{r}_{l,0}(\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+})}+\|\Theta(s)\|^{2}_{\mathbb{H}^{r}_{l,1}(\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+})}\mathrm{d}s\,,

and

\displaystyle IV_{7B}^{12}=

\displaystyle-\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\tilde{\kappa}^{-\frac{n-1}{2}}\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta}(\partial_{t}-\lambda\Delta_{\bar{x}})^{\frac{n-1}{2}}\Theta\cdot\mathscr{L}^{\star}_{3}\Theta|_{\zeta=0}|_{s=0}^{s=t}\mathrm{d}\bar{x}

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta}(\partial_{t}-\lambda\Delta_{\bar{x}})^{\frac{n-1}{2}}\Theta\cdot(\partial_{t}+\lambda\Delta_{\bar{x}})\big\{\tilde{\kappa}^{-\frac{n-1}{2}}\mathscr{L}^{\star}_{3}\Theta\big\}|_{\zeta=0}\mathrm{d}\bar{x}\mathrm{d}s

(5.82)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C(E_{k+1})\Big(\|\Theta(0)\|^{2}_{\mathbb{H}^{r}_{l}(\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+})}+\sum_{p=0,1}\|\Theta(t)\|^{2}_{\mathbb{H}^{r}_{l,p}(\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+})}+\int_{0}^{t}\sum_{p=0,1}\|\Theta(s)\|^{2}_{\mathbb{H}^{r}_{l,p}(\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+})}\mathrm{d}s\Big)\,.

If
\beta=0
, we have
IV_{7B}^{13}=0
. If
|\beta|\geq 2
, we assume
\beta\geq 2e_{1}=2(1,0)
. One then has

\displaystyle IV_{7B}^{13}=

\displaystyle-\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta-2e_{1}}(\partial_{t}-\lambda\Delta_{\bar{x}})^{\frac{n+1}{2}}\Theta

\displaystyle\times\partial_{\bar{x}}^{2e_{1}}\big\{\tilde{\kappa}^{-\frac{n-1}{2}}[\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta},R_{\theta}\tilde{\kappa}^{-\frac{n-1}{2}}]\partial_{t}^{\gamma}(\partial_{t}-\lambda\Delta_{\bar{x}})^{\frac{n-1}{2}}\Theta\big\}|_{\zeta=0}\mathrm{d}\bar{x}\mathrm{d}s

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C(E_{k+1})\int_{0}^{t}\|\Theta(s)\|^{2}_{\mathbb{H}^{r}_{l,0}(\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+})}+\|\Theta(s)\|^{2}_{\mathbb{H}^{r}_{l,1}(\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+})}\mathrm{d}s\,.

If
|\beta|=1
, the facts that
2\gamma+|\beta|+n=r
and
n\in[2,r]
is odd imply that
r
is even. We then have
2\gamma+n=r-1
and

\displaystyle IV_{7B}^{13}=

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{2}\delta_{r}(\beta=e_{i})\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\partial_{t}^{\gamma+\frac{n+1}{2}}\Theta

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\times\partial_{\bar{x}}^{e_{i}}\big\{\tilde{\kappa}^{-\frac{n-1}{2}}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{e_{i}}(R_{\theta}\tilde{\kappa}^{-\frac{n-1}{2}})\partial_{t}^{\gamma}(\partial_{t}-\lambda\Delta_{\bar{x}})^{\frac{n-1}{2}}\Theta\big\}|_{\zeta=0}\mathrm{d}\bar{x}\mathrm{d}s

\displaystyle+\sum_{i=1}^{2}\delta_{r}(\beta=e_{i})\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\sum_{q=1}^{\frac{n+1}{2}}C_{\frac{n+1}{2}}^{q}(-\lambda)^{q}\nabla_{\bar{x}}\partial_{t}^{\gamma+\frac{n+1}{2}-q}\Delta_{\bar{x}}^{q-1}\Theta

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\cdot\nabla_{\bar{x}}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{e_{i}}\big\{\tilde{\kappa}^{-\frac{n-1}{2}}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{e_{i}}(R_{\theta}\tilde{\kappa}^{-\frac{n-1}{2}})\partial_{t}^{\gamma}(\partial_{t}-\lambda\Delta_{\bar{x}})^{\frac{n-1}{2}}\Theta\big\}|_{\zeta=0}\mathrm{d}\bar{x}\mathrm{d}s

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\epsilon_{0}\sum_{i=1}^{2}\delta_{r}(\beta=e_{i})\int_{0}^{t}\|\partial_{t}^{\gamma+\frac{n+1}{2}}\Theta|_{\zeta=0}(s)\|^{2}_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{2})}\mathrm{d}s

\displaystyle+C(E_{k+1})\int_{0}^{t}\|\Theta(s)\|^{2}_{\mathbb{H}^{r}_{l,0}(\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+})}+\|\Theta(s)\|^{2}_{\mathbb{H}^{r}_{l,1}(\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+})}\mathrm{d}s

for some small
\epsilon_{0}>0
to be determined, where the last inequality is derived from Lemma
5.1
and
\delta_{r}(\beta=e_{i})
is given in (
5.34
). As a result,

(5.83)

\displaystyle IV_{7B}^{13}\leq

\displaystyle\epsilon_{0}\sum_{i=1}^{2}\delta_{r}(\beta=e_{i})\int_{0}^{t}\|\partial_{t}^{\gamma+\frac{n+1}{2}}\Theta|_{\zeta=0}(s)\|^{2}_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{2})}\mathrm{d}s

\displaystyle+C(E_{k+1})\int_{0}^{t}\|\Theta(s)\|^{2}_{\mathbb{H}^{r}_{l,0}(\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+})}+\|\Theta(s)\|^{2}_{\mathbb{H}^{r}_{l,1}(\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+})}\mathrm{d}s\,.

The lower bounds (
2.9
) and (
5.7
) yield that there is a
c_{n}>0
such that

\displaystyle R_{\theta}\tilde{\kappa}^{-n+1}\geq c_{n}>0\,.

Together with Lemma
5.1
, one obtains

\displaystyle IV_{7B}^{14}=

\displaystyle-\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\tfrac{1}{2}R_{\theta}\tilde{\kappa}^{-n+1}\{\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta}(\partial_{t}-\lambda\Delta_{\bar{x}})^{\frac{n-1}{2}}\Theta\}^{2}|_{\zeta=0}\mathrm{d}\bar{x}|_{s=0}^{s=t}

\displaystyle-\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\lambda R_{\theta}\tilde{\kappa}^{-n+1}\big|\nabla_{\bar{x}}\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta}(\partial_{t}-\lambda\Delta_{\bar{x}})^{\frac{n-1}{2}}\Theta\big|^{2}|_{\zeta=0}\mathrm{d}\bar{x}\mathrm{d}s

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\tfrac{1}{2}(\partial_{t}+\lambda\Delta_{\bar{x}})(R_{\theta}\tilde{\kappa}^{-n+1})\{\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta}(\partial_{t}-\lambda\Delta_{\bar{x}})^{\frac{n-1}{2}}\Theta\}^{2}|_{\zeta=0}\mathrm{d}\bar{x}\mathrm{d}s

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle-\tfrac{1}{2}c_{n}\|\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta}(\partial_{t}-\lambda\Delta_{\bar{x}})^{\frac{n-1}{2}}\Theta|_{\zeta=0}(t)\|^{2}_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{2})}

\displaystyle-\lambda c_{n}\int_{0}^{t}\|\nabla_{\bar{x}}\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta}(\partial_{t}-\lambda\Delta_{\bar{x}})^{\frac{n-1}{2}}\Theta|_{\zeta=0}(s)\|^{2}_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{2})}\mathrm{d}s

(5.84)

\displaystyle+C(E_{k+1})\Big(\|\Theta(0)\|^{2}_{\mathbb{H}^{r}_{l}(\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+})}+\int_{0}^{t}\sum_{p=0,1}\|\Theta(s)\|^{2}_{\mathbb{H}^{r}_{l,p}(\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+})}\mathrm{d}s\Big)\,.

From (
5.81
), (
5
), (
5.83
) and (
5
), one thereby knows

\displaystyle IV_{7B}^{1}\leq

\displaystyle-\tfrac{1}{2}c_{n}\|\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta}(\partial_{t}-\lambda\Delta_{\bar{x}})^{\frac{n-1}{2}}\Theta|_{\zeta=0}(t)\|^{2}_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{2})}

\displaystyle-\lambda c_{n}\int_{0}^{t}\|\nabla_{\bar{x}}\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta}(\partial_{t}-\lambda\Delta_{\bar{x}})^{\frac{n-1}{2}}\Theta|_{\zeta=0}(s)\|^{2}_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{2})}\mathrm{d}s

(5.85)

\displaystyle+\epsilon_{0}\sum_{i=1}^{2}\delta_{r}(\beta=e_{i})\int_{0}^{t}\|\partial_{t}^{\gamma+\frac{n+1}{2}}\Theta|_{\zeta=0}(s)\|^{2}_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{2})}\mathrm{d}s

\displaystyle+C(E_{k+1})\Big(\|\Theta(0)\|^{2}_{\mathbb{H}^{r}_{l}(\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+})}+\sum_{p=0,1}\|\Theta(t)\|^{2}_{\mathbb{H}^{r}_{l,p}(\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+})}+\int_{0}^{t}\sum_{p=0,1}\|\Theta(s)\|^{2}_{\mathbb{H}^{r}_{l,p}(\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+})}\mathrm{d}s\Big)\,.

Consequently, if
n\in[2,r]
is odd,

\displaystyle IV_{7B}

\displaystyle\leq-\tfrac{1}{2}c_{n}\|\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta}(\partial_{t}-\lambda\Delta_{\bar{x}})^{\frac{n-1}{2}}\Theta|_{\zeta=0}(t)\|^{2}_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{2})}

\displaystyle-\lambda c_{n}\int_{0}^{t}\|\nabla_{\bar{x}}\partial_{t}^{\gamma}\partial_{\bar{x}}^{\beta}(\partial_{t}-\lambda\Delta_{\bar{x}})^{\frac{n-1}{2}}\Theta|_{\zeta=0}(s)\|^{2}_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{2})}\mathrm{d}s

\displaystyle+\epsilon_{0}\sum_{i=1}^{2}\delta_{r}(\beta=e_{i})\int_{0}^{t}\|\partial_{t}^{\gamma+\frac{n+1}{2}}\Theta|_{\zeta=0}(s)\|^{2}_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{2})}\mathrm{d}s

(5.86)

\displaystyle+C(E_{k+1})(1+t)\sup_{t\in[0,\tau]}\sum_{\tilde{n}=0}^{n}\|\tilde{g}^{\sigma}(t)\|^{2}_{\mathbb{H}^{r+1}_{l,\tilde{n}}(\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+})}

\displaystyle+C(E_{k+1})\Big(\|\Theta(0)\|^{2}_{\mathbb{H}^{r}_{l}(\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+})}+\sum_{p=0,1}\|\Theta(t)\|^{2}_{\mathbb{H}^{r}_{l,p}(\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+})}+\int_{0}^{t}\sum_{p=0,1}\|\Theta(s)\|^{2}_{\mathbb{H}^{r}_{l,p}(\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+})}\mathrm{d}s\Big)

for some small
\epsilon_{0}>0
to be determined. Finally, the bounds (
5.69
) and (
5
) finish the proof of Lemma
5.3
.
∎
